### [108] [$μ_0$: A Scalable 3D Interaction-Trace World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13769)
*Seungjae Lee,Yoonkyo Jung,Jusuk Lee,Jonghun Shin,Amir Hossein Shahidzadeh,Yao-Chih Lee,H. Jin Kim,Jia-Bin Huang,Furong Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: μ₀是一种基于3D轨迹的可扩展世界模型，通过预测交互点的平滑3D轨迹而非像素或动作，实现了跨本体的机器人学习。其TraceExtract系统从多样化视频中自动提取3D监督信号，结合预训练的视觉-语言主干和模块化轨迹专家进行预训练。实验表明μ₀在2D/3D轨迹预测上优于基线方法，且冻结后的μ₀可与动作专家结合，产生与有动作监督的VLA模型竞争的策略性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型面临困境：像素级视频模型消耗大量容量在密集外观重建上，直接动作模型需要本体特定的动作标签，限制了可扩展性。本文旨在寻找一种既紧凑又无需本体特定标签的可扩展世界模型表示。

Method: 提出了μ₀世界模型，基于3D轨迹表示交互点运动；设计了TraceExtract系统自动从视频中提取3D监督信号（关键点选择、全局对齐轨迹构建、分层语言描述关联）；采用预训练的视觉-语言主干加模块化轨迹专家（使用B样条控制点表示查询并预测未来轨迹）的架构。

Result: μ₀在2D和3D轨迹预测上均优于基线（包括轨迹预测模型和tokenized VLM方法）。冻结后的μ₀与动作专家结合，实现的下游轨迹条件策略性能与使用动作监督预训练的VLA模型（如π₀）具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 3D轨迹是一种可扩展且可迁移的跨本体操控表示，能够在无需动作标签的情况下实现有效的世界建模和下游机器人学习。

Abstract: World models that capture how actions induce physical change enable scalable robot learning without reliance on embodiment-specific action labels. Pixel-space video models provide broad visual priors but expend model capacity on dense appearance reconstruction, while direct action models require embodiment-specific labels that hinder scalability. We present $μ_0$, a scalable world model based on 3D traces. Rather than predicting dense pixels or directly modeling actions, $μ_0$ forecasts smooth 3D trajectories for salient interaction points such as objects, tools, hands, and contact regions, yielding a compact, embodiment-agnostic motion interface. To enable training from diverse video sources, our TraceExtract system automatically extracts 3D supervision by selecting keypoints, constructing globally aligned traces, and associating motion segments with hierarchical language captions. This TraceExtract supervision pretrains $μ_0$ by combining a pretrained vision-language backbone with a modular trace expert, which represents each query via B-spline control points and predicts future traces. Experiments show that $μ_0$ outperforms baselines in both 2D and 3D trace prediction, including trace prediction models and tokenized VLM methods. Because $μ_0$ is frozen and reusable, it can be paired with action experts for downstream robot embodiments. Despite action-free pretraining, the resulting trace-conditioned policies achieve performance competitive with VLA models pretrained with action supervision, such as $π_0$. These results establish 3D traces as a scalable and transferable representation for cross-embodiment manipulation.

</details>


### [109] [FlowMo-WM: A World Model with Object Momentum and Hidden Ambient Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13817)
*Yitao Jiang,Luyang Zhao,Muhao Chen,Devin Balkcom*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FlowMo-WM是一种端到端可训练的视觉世界模型，通过从图像-动作历史中推断物体中心运动状态和与隐藏漂移相关的长历史上下文，无需流场直接监督。它将历史分解为短历史潜变量（总结物体中心运动）和长历史上下文（总结缓慢变化的外源影响），利用零上下文残差转换分离动作条件基础动力学与上下文依赖漂移效应。在包含多样隐藏流、扰动和随机车辆动力学的水面仿真环境中，FlowMo-WM在长程预测准确性上优于代表性动作条件潜世界模型。上下文消融实验表明环境上下文对隐藏漂移下的稳定预测至关重要，线性探针表征了学习因子的编码信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作条件世界模型主要在运动受即时控制主导的场景中评估，而水面车辆等真实世界物体会因惯性和隐藏环境漂移（如水流、风）持续运动。现有方法缺乏对隐藏漂移的建模能力，导致在存在环境干扰的长时域预测中性能下降。

Method: 1) 将图像-动作历史分解为短历史潜状态（物体中心运动）和长历史上下文（缓慢变化的外源影响如水流）；2) 提出零上下文残差转换，分离动作条件基础动力学与上下文依赖漂移效应；3) 端到端训练，无需流场直接监督。

Result: 在模拟水面车辆环境中，FlowMo-WM在长时域rollout准确性上优于代表性动作条件潜世界模型。上下文消融（置零或打乱）表明环境上下文对隐藏漂移下稳定预测重要。线性探针分析揭示了学习因子的信息编码特性。

Conclusion: FlowMo-WM通过显式分离物体中心运动与隐藏环境漂移，有效提升了存在隐藏流干扰场景下的长时域预测能力。该方法无需直接监督流场即可建模环境漂移因素，为水面车辆等受惯性及环境力影响的机器人系统提供了更准确的世界模型框架。

Abstract: World models in robot learning predict future states from visual observations and actions, enabling agents to reason about the consequences of their controls. However, many action-conditioned models are evaluated in settings where motion is dominated by immediate control, whereas aquatic surface vehicles and other real-world objects continue moving under inertia and are displaced by hidden ambient drift, such as water currents or wind. We propose FlowMo-WM, an end-to-end trainable visual world model that infers object-centric motion state and a predictive long-history context associated with hidden drift from image-action histories without direct supervision of flow fields. FlowMo-WM factorizes image-action history into a short-history latent state, trained to summarize object-centric motion, and a longer-history context, trained to summarize slowly varying exogenous influences. A zero-context residual transition separates action-conditioned base dynamics from context-dependent drift effects during latent rollout. In simulated aquatic surface-vehicle environments with diverse hidden flows, disturbances, and randomized vehicle dynamics, FlowMo-WM improves long-horizon rollout accuracy over representative action-conditioned latent world models. Prediction-time context ablations, in which the inferred context is zeroed or shuffled during rollout, show that the ambient context is important for stable prediction under hidden drift, while frozen linear probes characterize information encoded in the learned factors.

</details>


### [110] [Multi-Agent Embodied Autonomous Driving: From V2X Information Exchange to Shared World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13840)
*Senkang Hu,Zhengru Fang,Yihang Tao,Zihan Fang,Sam Tak Wu Kwong,Yuguang Fang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过共享世界模型（SWMs）视角，系统综述了380多篇文献，涵盖V2X通信、协同感知、智能体间认知、协同规划、端到端协同驾驶及仿真验证，指出当前评估集中于仿真和离线协议，基础模型缺乏实时安全保障，并提出了多智能体具身自动驾驶（MAEAD）的关键研究优先级：可验证的共享状态维护、鲁棒的意图与计划对齐、以及在通信、延迟和部署约束下的安全协调行动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶正从单车辆智能向多智能体具身系统转变，需要共享感知、推理意图并在不确定性下协调行动。现有研究散落在多个子领域，缺乏统一的组织框架和系统性的研究缺口分析。

Method: 以共享世界模型（SWMs）为核心组织视角，综述380余篇相关文献，包括V2X通信、协同感知、跨智能体认知、协同规划、端到端协同驾驶、仿真与数据引擎等方向，分析当前研究在评估验证和安全性方面的不足。

Result: 当前评估集中在仿真环境、固定基准和离线协议；基于基础模型的协调缺乏在开放交通中的实时安全性保障。

Conclusion: 提出了多智能体具身自动驾驶（MAEAD）的未来研究重点：可验证的共享状态维护、鲁棒的意图与计划对齐、以及在通信、延迟和部署约束下实现安全协调行动。

Abstract: Autonomous driving is shifting from isolated vehicle intelligence toward multi-agent embodied systems that share perception, infer intent, and coordinate action under uncertainty. This survey examines this transition through the lens of Shared World Models (SWMs): predictive cross-agent representations maintained across vehicles, infrastructure, and other traffic participants. We review more than 380 publications spanning vehicle-to-everything (V2X) communication, collaborative perception, inter-agent cognition, cooperative planning, end-to-end cooperative driving, and simulation and data engines for closed-loop validation. The organizing question is how exchanged observations become aligned state, intent-aware interaction, and coordinated downstream action. Across the surveyed literature, evaluation remains concentrated in simulation, curated benchmarks, and offline protocols. Foundation-model-based coordination also lacks verified real-time safety guarantees in open traffic. These gaps motivate key research priorities for multi-agent embodied autonomous driving (MAEAD): verifiable shared-state maintenance, robust intent and plan alignment, and safe coordinated action under communication, latency, and deployment constraints.

</details>


### [111] [Efficient Domain-Adaptive Policy Learning via Kernel Representation with Application to Quadrotor Control under Non-Stationary Disturbances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13842)
*Hongyu Zhou,Mingtian Tan,Vasileios Tzoumas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于核表示的高效域自适应策略学习算法，通过随机傅里叶特征对未知扰动进行可微建模，在离线训练中利用可微仿真快速优化策略，在部署时通过在线最小二乘估计实时更新核参数，实现四旋翼飞行器在复杂非平稳环境下的自适应控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习域自适应策略需要环境表示既能在离线训练时充分表达复杂的仿真到现实差距，又能在部署时支持快速在线自适应，现有方法难以兼顾表达能力和计算效率。

Method: 使用基于随机傅里叶特征的可微核近似对未知扰动建模；离线训练时随机采样核系数和带宽参数生成多样化扰动分布，通过可微仿真利用解析梯度优化控制策略（RTX 4090上仅需50秒）；在线部署时通过最小二乘估计实时更新核系数和带宽实现自适应。

Result: 在四旋翼轨迹跟踪任务中，通过高保真数值仿真和Crazyflie硬件实验验证，在气动效应、风力、地面效应和载荷波动等多种扰动下均表现良好。

Conclusion: 该算法通过核表示结合离线可微仿真与在线自适应更新，高效实现了域自适应策略学习，在复杂非平稳扰动环境下具有实际部署潜力。

Abstract: We present an algorithm for efficient domain-adaptive policy learning via kernel representations. Learning domain-adaptive policies is challenging since it requires an environment representation that is both sufficiently expressive to model complex sim-to-real gaps during offline training, and computationally efficient enough to support rapid online adaptation during deployment. For instance, a quadrotor may encounter time-varying, non-stationary disturbances, such as sudden gusts of wind, payload shifts, or transitions between distinct flight regimes with and without ground effects. To address these challenges, we model unknown disturbances using a differentiable kernel approximation based on random Fourier features. During the offline training phase, we randomly sample kernel coefficients and bandwidth parameters to generate a rich diversity of disturbance profiles. We then optimize the control policy via differentiable simulation with analytical gradients, a process that takes only 50 seconds of training time on an RTX 4090 GPU. During hardware deployment, the policy adapts to non-stationary environments in real time by updating both the kernel coefficients and bandwidth through online least-squares estimation. We evaluate our method on quadrotor trajectory tracking tasks across high-fidelity numerical simulations and hardware experiments using Crazyflie, subjected to various disturbances, including complex aerodynamic effects, wind, ground effects, and payload fluctuations.

</details>


### [112] [Output-Level Regularization Eliminates the Seed Lottery in Single-GPU VLA Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13856)
*Jeffrin Sam,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 微调VLA-JEPA模型时，相同数据和架构下不同随机种子会导致性能从91-94%骤降至65.2%（29个百分点的差距），且无任何错误提示。这种现象称为“种子彩票”，根源是输出坍缩——动作预测器学习输出与视觉输入无关的相似结果。权重级正则化方法（L2、EWC）因其结构盲区无法解决，而输出级正则化（VICReg、Dropout、减半学习率）可完全消除该问题。最简单的修复是修改优化器中的一个参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发现并解释视觉-语言-动作模型微调中存在的隐蔽风险：相同代码、数据和架构下，随机种子差异会导致模型性能出现不可预测的大幅下降（种子彩票现象），且无任何错误提示。

Method: 通过实验分析种子彩票现象的原因——输出坍缩，利用Jacobian零空间理论证明权重级正则化方法的局限性，对比7种方法（权重级：L2、EWC；输出级：VICReg、Dropout、减半学习率等）在不同种子和LIBERO基准下的表现。

Result: 权重级方法（L2、EWC）无法解决种子彩票；输出级方法（VICReg 12种子、Dropout 4种子、减半学习率5种子）消除所有灾难种子（0/21坍缩 vs Baseline 1/13，F(12,11)=28.7, p<0.001）

Conclusion: 输出坍缩是种子彩票的根源，输出级正则化可有效消除该问题，最简单的修复是调整优化器中的学习率参数。

Abstract: Fine-tuning a vision-language-action model (VLA-JEPA) on a single GPU should be simple: load a pretrained checkpoint, run training, deploy. There is a hidden danger. Run the same fine-tuning code thirteen times -- same data, same architecture, different random seed -- and twelve runs produce a robot succeeding 91--94% of the time, while one run silently degrades to 65.2%: a 29 pp gap with no error message, no warning, and no way to predict which seed will fail. We call this the seed lottery. We trace the cause to output collapse: the action predictor quietly learns to produce nearly identical outputs regardless of what the robot sees. Existing weight-level methods (L2, EWC) are structurally blind to this collapse -- they penalize weight changes, but collapse occurs in directions weights can move freely without affecting outputs, a gap we formalize via the Jacobian null-space. Across 7 methods x up to 13 seeds x 3 LIBERO benchmarks, three output-level regularizers -- VICReg (n=12 seeds), Dropout (n=4), and a halved learning rate (n=5) -- each eliminate every catastrophic seed (0/21 combined collapses vs. 1/13 Baseline; F(12,11)=28.7, p<0.001), while weight-level methods (L2, EWC) preserve the lottery. The simplest fix is changing one number in your optimizer config.

</details>


### [113] [ContactWorld: What Matters in Vision-Tactile World Models for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13877)
*Zhiyuan Zhang,Pokuang Zhou,Kaidi Zhang,Adeesh Desai,Temitope Amosa,Davood Soleymanzadeh,Jiuzhou Lei,Minghui Zheng,Yu She*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了ContactWorld基准，系统研究接触丰富操作任务中视觉-触觉世界模型的表现，发现空间结构化和时间连续的表示（如点云结合触觉力场）能显著提升长时域规划性能，触觉在长期规划中愈发重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的操作任务需要世界模型从多模态感官观测中推理复杂接触动力学，但何种表示属性能够支持稳定的长时域规划尚不明确。本文旨在系统探究这一问题。

Method: 构建ContactWorld基准，包含12项接触丰富操作任务（插入、拆卸、旋拧、探索性交互等），系统比较不同视觉-触觉表示（腕部视角、前视角、点云、触觉力场等）在规划任务中的表现。

Result: 空间结构化和时间连续的表示表现最佳：点云将规划成功率从20.7%（腕部视角）和22.0%（前视角）提升至32.1%；点云+触觉力场进一步提升至36.1%。触觉的有效性关键在于跨模态表示兼容性而非单纯模态扩展，且在长时域规划中触觉的作用更加显著。

Conclusion: 对于接触丰富机器人操作，视觉-触觉世界模型应重视表示结构、多模态兼容性以及长时域鲁棒性，点云与触觉力场的结合是当前最优方案。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation requires world models to reason over complex contact dynamics from multimodal sensory observations. However, it remains unclear which representation properties fundamentally support stable long-horizon planning in contact-rich settings. In this paper, we present ContactWorld, a benchmark and systematic empirical study of vision-tactile world models spanning 12 contact-rich manipulation tasks, including insertion, disassembly, screwing, and exploratory interaction. Across extensive experiments, we find that representations that are both spatially structured and temporally continuous consistently achieve the strongest planning performance. In particular, point-cloud observations improve average planning success rates from 20.7% with wrist-view observations and 22.0% with front-view observations to 32.1%. We further find that the effectiveness of tactile sensing depends critically on cross-modal representation compatibility rather than modality scaling alone. Combining point-cloud observations with tactile force-field representations, which preserve richer spatial structure and interaction dynamics, further improves performance to 36.1%, yielding the strongest overall planning performance across all evaluated tasks. Moreover, tactile sensing becomes increasingly important under long-horizon planning objectives, where compounding prediction errors and contact uncertainty accumulate over time. Together, these findings highlight the importance of representation structure, multimodal compatibility, and long-horizon robustness in vision-tactile world models for contact-rich robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [114] [AnyGoal: Vision-Language Guided Multi-Agent Exploration for Training-Free Lifelong Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13878)
*MoniJesu James,Marcelino Julio Fernando,Miguel Altamirano Cabrera,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AnyGoal是一种无需训练的多机器人架构，以VLM为核心进行基于前沿的探索，通过共享的2D高斯贝叶斯价值图（BVM）协调智能体，在GOAT-Bench基准上达到SOTA性能（双智能体Subtask SR 52.4%，比Modular GOAT提升27.5个百分点）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端导航策略在分布外场景中性能严重下降；模块化流水线受限于闭集目标检测召回率；3D快照记忆系统维护密集的视角相关表示且负担重。

Method: 提出AnyGoal架构：1) VLM驱动的前沿探索；2) 共享2D高斯贝叶斯价值图（BVM），维护逐像素(μ, σ²)后验，通过深度锥掩码的精度加权融合更新，子任务间不重置实现终身证据累积；3) 前沿排序结合VLM软评分与BVM的贝叶斯UCB项；4) 带空间分离惩罚和承诺滞后的贪心分配器实现多智能体协调，无需中央控制器。

Result: 双智能体系统在GOAT-Bench val unseen split（360 episodes, 2,669子任务）上达到52.4% Subtask SR, 12.7% SPL，相比Modular GOAT（24.9%）提升27.5个百分点；单智能体AnyGoal达41.9% Subtask SR；四路感知消融实验表明开放词汇检测器将主导故障模式从探索转向目标验证。

Conclusion: AnyGoal通过VLM驱动的决策架构而非感知改进实现性能提升，在严格物理仿真设定下达到在GOAT-Bench上的SOTA。

Abstract: End-to-end navigation policies trained on large simulation corpora degrade sharply when transferred to out-of-distribution scenes, categories, or goal modalities. Modular pipelines such as Modular GOAT are bottlenecked by closed-set object detection recall, while 3D snapshot-memory systems (e.g. 3D-Mem) accumulate dense, view-dependent representations that are heavy to maintain. We present AnyGoal, a training-free multi-robot architecture that places a Vision-Language Model (VLM) at the core of frontier-based exploration and coordinates agents through a shared 2D Gaussian Bayesian Value Map (BVM). The BVM maintains a per-pixel (mu, sigma^2) posterior over goal relevance, updated via precision-weighted fusion of VLM scores through a depth-cone mask, and is never reset between subtasks, yielding lifelong evidence accumulation. Frontiers are ranked by a convex blend of a VLM-as-judge softmax and a Bayesian UCB term on the BVM. A greedy allocator with spatial-separation penalty and commitment hysteresis distributes frontiers across agents without a centralized controller. On the full GOAT-Bench val unseen split (360 episodes, 2,669 subtasks), our dual-agent system achieves 52.4% Subtask SR at 12.7% SPL--state of the art under the strict physical regime (discrete 0.25 m steps, no teleportation, 42 deg HFOV) and a +27.5 pp improvement over Modular GOAT (24.9%). Single-agent AnyGoal achieves 41.9% Subtask SR, showing gains arise from the decision architecture. A four-way perception ablation shows that open-vocabulary detectors shift the dominant failure mode from exploration to goal verification.

</details>


### [115] [Guided Diffusion with Distilled Vision-Language Reliability for Aerial Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13883)
*Ivan Valuev,Iana Zhura,Valerii Serpiva,Didar Seyidov,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种可靠性感知的扩散规划器，通过轻量级网络蒸馏VLM生成场景级可靠性热图，并结合可微ESDF代价函数处理物理和虚拟障碍物，实现了无人机在玻璃、镜面等不可靠区域的安全导航，将障碍物违反率从40.3%降至9.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端生成式模型在干净数据上训练，无法识别不可靠观测（如玻璃、镜面、过曝区域），将其作为有效证据用于规划，导致安全隐患。

Method: 1) 轻量级网络蒸馏VLM的开放词汇推理能力，实时生成场景级可靠性热图；2) 可微两阶段ESDF代价函数，将深度感知的物理障碍物与可靠性热图标记的虚拟障碍物同等处理，引导扩散去噪过程。

Result: 障碍物违反率从40.3%降至9.6%，遍历区域平均可靠性从0.588提升至0.925；消融实验证实可靠性热图是关键组件（0.898→0.783）；蒸馏框架比完整VLM快2倍。

Conclusion: 可靠性感知的扩散规划器在不重新训练的情况下泛化到未见环境，显著提升了无人机在感知退化场景下的导航安全性。

Abstract: Autonomous UAV navigation is conventionally solved by pipelines that separate perception, mapping, and planning into distinct stages, which propagates errors, accumulates latency, and requires environment-specific retuning. End-to-end generative models remove these interfaces by mapping raw observations directly to trajectories, but inherit a subtle failure mode: trained on clean data, they cannot recognise when an observation is unreliable, and treat degraded regions such as glass, mirrors, and overexposed surfaces as valid evidence for planning. We present a reliability-aware diffusion planner for 3D UAV navigation. It conditions trajectory generation on the observation together with a scene-level reliability heatmap that marks where perception cannot be trusted, produced by a lightweight network that distils the open-vocabulary reasoning of a vision-language model within the real-time planning budget. To generalise to unseen environments without retraining, we steer the denoising process with a differentiable two-stage ESDF cost that treats physical obstacles from depth and virtual obstacles from highly unreliable regions on equal footing. In simulation and on a real quadrotor, our planner produces markedly safer trajectories than a state-of-the-art diffusion baseline, reducing the obstacle-violation rate from 40.3% to 9.6% and raising the mean reliability of traversed regions from 0.588 to 0.925. Ablating the reliability term alone drops mean reliability from 0.898 to 0.783, confirming it as the decisive component, while distillation runs the framework up to 2 times faster than the full vision-language model.

</details>


### [116] [PhysVLA: Towards Physically-Grounded VLA for Embodied Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13886)
*Namai Chandra,Shriram Damodaran,Lin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PhysVLA是一种即插即用的推理时框架，通过相位感知有限状态机和选择性欧拉-拉格朗日门控双层次校正，在不重新训练或微调的情况下，为任意冻结的VLA模型注入物理一致性（刚体动力学、接触约束），在多个基准和真实机器人上显著提升成功率和轨迹稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA模型主要拟合行为演示数据，未显式强制执行刚体动力学或接触约束等基本物理原理，导致"物理鸿沟"——标准时间平滑处理在提升轨迹质量的同时引入了短期记忆无法解决的额外失败。

Method: PhysVLA是一个即插即用的推理时框架，无需重训练/微调/权重访问，单步开销<1ms。它拦截预测的控制动作，捕获模拟器或系统状态，并施加双层校正：(i) 相位感知有限状态机，结构化离散任务阶段（接近、抓取、搬运、放置）；(ii) 选择性欧拉-拉格朗日门控，仅在动力学感知器检测到运动学-动力学不一致时激活。

Result: 在LIBERO-Spatial（7-DoF Franka Panda）上，对OpenVLA等四个骨干网络，绝对成功率提升高达17%，稳定性提升高达19%，无单任务退化，轨迹效率提升最高15%；Robosuite Lift跨模拟器扫描中轨迹急动度鲁棒性最高提升10倍；真实Agilex Piper机械臂拾取放置任务成功率提升高达50%。

Conclusion: PhysVLA将物理感知确立为一种可组合的、与骨干无关的运行时模块，能够在无需重新训练的情况下有效迁移到物理硬件，显著提升VLA模型的物理一致性和任务成功率。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models excel at mapping visual inputs and natural language instructions directly to robotic control policies. However, because they are trained primarily to fit behavioural demonstration data, they do not explicitly enforce fundamental physical principles such as rigid-body dynamics or contact constraints. This exposes a critical physics gap: standard temporal smoothing applied on top of single-step or chunked VLAs trades trajectory quality for added failures that short-term memory cannot resolve. To bridge this gap, we introduce PhysVLA (Physics-VLA), a plug-and-play, inference-time framework designed to wrap any frozen VLA backbone without retraining, fine-tuning, or weight access, with less than 1 ms of overhead per control step. PhysVLA intercepts the predicted control action, captures only the simulator or system state, and applies a dual-layered correction: (i) a phase-aware finite-state machine that structures discrete task segments (approach, grasp, transport, and place), and (ii) a selective Euler-Lagrange gate that activates only when a dynamics oracle detects kinodynamic inconsistency. Evaluated across OpenVLA, OpenVLA-OFT, Force-VLA, and Generalist-VLA on LIBERO-Spatial with a 7-DoF Franka Panda, the framework delivers absolute success rate increases of up to 17% and stability increases of up to 19% with no per-task regressions, improves trajectory efficiency by up to 15% across all four backbones, and shows up to a 10x improvement in trajectory jerk robustness on a Robosuite Lift cross-simulator sweep. We further validate the framework on a real Agilex Piper arm with a pick-and-place task, confirming that PhysVLA transfers to physical hardware without retraining, with success-rate improvements of up to 50%, establishing physical awareness as a composable, backbone-agnostic runtime module.

</details>


### [117] [Learning Dynamic Swing-Up of an Inverted Pendulum using Remote Magnetic Actuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13915)
*Viacheslav Sydora,Jasan Zughaibi,Denis von Arx,Quentin Boehler,Michael Muehlebach*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文利用临床级Navion电磁导航系统，首次实现了磁驱动倒立摆的摆起控制。通过结合轨迹优化、时变LQR状态反馈和迭代学习控制（ILC），在六次迭代内成功完成摆起，并发现ILC学习的修正量与高保真磁场模型校准预测的扭矩偏差高度吻合，表明学习与自适应方法能有效处理电磁驱动中的不确定性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电磁导航系统研究多依赖准静态控制，动态控制方法虽已展现优势，但远离平衡态的轨迹跟踪问题仍未解决。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 结合考虑内部eMNS动力学的轨迹优化、时变线性二次型调节器（LQR）状态反馈和迭代学习控制（ILC），利用先前试验数据和系统动力学模型逐步优化前馈指令。

Result: 单独使用LQR无法成功，而ILC在六次迭代内实现了倒立摆的成功摆起。后实验分析表明ILC学习的修正量与高保真磁场模型校准预测的扭矩偏差高度一致。

Conclusion: 学习与自适应方法是处理电磁驱动中不确定性（如患者特异性生理运动模式和磁场模型校准误差）的有效手段，所提方法可推广至导管、导丝等磁驱动器械。

Abstract: Electromagnetic Navigation Systems (eMNS) have gained considerable attention for minimally invasive surgery and targeted drug delivery. While most of the literature relies on quasi-static control of these systems, recent work has demonstrated the benefits of dynamic approaches. However, trajectory tracking far from equilibrium states remains largely unaddressed. We close this gap by demonstrating the first swing-up of a magnetically actuated inverted pendulum using the clinically-ready Navion eMNS. Although the inverted pendulum is not clinically relevant in itself, the proposed method utilizes torques and forces as control objectives, making it applicable to other magnetically actuated devices such as catheters and guidewires. Our approach combines trajectory optimization that accounts for internal eMNS dynamics with time-varying Linear Quadratic Regulator (LQR) state feedback and Iterative Learning Control (ILC), which leverages previous trial data and the system's dynamic model to progressively refine the feedforward command. While LQR alone fails due to the complex phenomena of magnetic actuation, ILC enables successful swing-up within six iterations. Furthermore, post-experimental analysis reveals that the learned ILC correction closely matches the torque discrepancy predicted by high-fidelity magnetic field model calibration, suggesting learning and adaptation as a promising tool to deal with uncertainties in electromagnetic actuation arising, e.g., from patient-specific physiological motion patterns and field model calibration inaccuracies.

</details>


### [118] [An Attention-based Model for Robust Forecasting with Missing Modality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13970)
*Zhitian Zhang,Wenjie Zi,Yunduz Rakhmangulova,Saghar Irandoust,Hossein Hajimirsadeghi,Thibaut Durand*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于CVAE和Transformer的多模态模型，能够在训练和推理时处理缺失模态，在机器人轨迹预测和操作预测任务上优于现有融合方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实机器人系统常面临传感器数据不完整的问题，而现有大多数多模态模型假设训练和推理时所有模态均可用，限制了其在机器人感知与决策中的实用性。

Method: 将模型构建为条件变分自编码器（CVAE），采用基于Transformer的注意力机制架构，学习统一的固定维度表示，即使部分模态缺失也能进行训练和推理。

Result: 在人类轨迹预测和机器人操作预测两个任务、五个多模态数据集上，模型能从缺失数据中有效学习，性能优于先前多模态融合方法。

Conclusion: 所提模型能够处理训练和推理中的模态缺失问题，通过学习所有模态的鲁棒近似表征，提升了多模态机器人学习在现实不完整数据场景下的适用性和性能。

Abstract: Learning with missing modalities is a fundamental challenge in multimodal robot learning, as real-world robotic systems often operate in environments with incomplete sensor data. Attention-based models are appealing for processing multimodal data because they can handle multiple modalities with a single backbone network. However, most multimodal models assume that all modalities are available during both training and inference, limiting their applicability in robotic perception and decision-making. In this paper, we introduce a multimodal model designed to handle missing modalities during both training and inference. The model is formulated as a conditional variational autoencoder (CVAE) and incorporates a transformer-based architecture that leverages attention mechanisms to learn a unified, fixed-dimensional representation, even when some modalities are missing. We show that our proposed model can be trained with missing modalities while approximating a robust representation of all modalities. We evaluate our approach on five multimodal datasets across two robot learning tasks: human trajectory prediction and robot manipulation forecasting. Experimental results demonstrate that our model effectively learns from incomplete data and is superior to prior multimodal fusion approaches.

</details>


### [119] [SplatlessDF: Continuous Distance Field Mapping with Non-Splatting Gaussians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13990)
*Monisha Mushtary Uttsha,Lan Wu,Teresa Vidal-Calleja*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SplatlessDF，利用各向异性高斯元素从空间而非光度视角构建连续距离场，支持距离和梯度查询，并可耦合2DGS实现统一的高斯原语框架，兼顾距离场建模与光度渲染，适用于机器人导航等任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高斯溅射方法主要从光度视角进行场景重建与渲染，缺乏从空间视角构建连续距离场的能力，难以直接支持机器人导航等需要距离和梯度查询的下游任务。

Method: SplatlessDF直接参数化各向异性高斯元素，通过优化恢复可微距离场；设计了独立DF公式和与2DGS耦合的联合DF-渲染公式两种设置，统一基于高斯原语实现连续DF与表面模型学习及光度渲染。

Result: 独立DF公式能高效准确地提供距离和梯度查询；联合DF-渲染公式在改善渲染几何的同时成功建模连续DF。

Conclusion: 高斯溅射式表示不仅适用于表面建模与渲染，还可扩展到适用于机器人导航的建图表示，展示了其更广泛的应用潜力。

Abstract: Recent Gaussian splatting (GS) methods have shown that scenes can be represented efficiently with optimisable Gaussians for high-quality reconstruction and rendering. In this paper, building on this principle, we introduce SplatlessDF, a continuous distance field (DF) mapping framework that uses anisotropic Gaussian elements from a spatial rather than photometric perspective. SplatlessDF directly parameterises the Gaussians and optimises to recover a differentiable DF, enabling distances and gradients to be queried in the spatial domain for downstream robotic tasks such as navigation. Furthermore, SplatlessDF can be coupled with 2D Gaussian splatting (2DGS), providing a unified framework based solely on Gaussian primitives that can learn continuous DF and surface models and supports photometric rendering. We consider two settings: a standalone DF-only formulation and a joint DF-rendering formulation coupled with 2DGS. Experiments show that the standalone formulation provides efficient and accurate distance and gradient queries, while the joint formulation improves rendering geometry and simultaneously models a continuous DF. These results highlight the potential of GS-style representations not only for surface modelling and rendering but also for mapping representations suited to robotic navigation.

</details>


### [120] [From Attacks to Curricula: Learnability-Guided Adversarial Training for Safe Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14032)
*Yuewen Mei,Tong Nie,Jie Sun,Haotian Shi,Wei Ma,Jian Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AlignADV提出了一种可学习性引导的闭环对抗训练框架，通过将对抗场景转化为可解决且与能力对齐的课程，提升了自动驾驶策略训练的收敛效率（最高减少40.6%训练步数）和最终性能（降低碰撞率、提高路线完成率）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗训练框架存在两个问题：1) 攻击导向的碰撞驱动生成器常合成不可解的极端场景，导致策略学习退化；2) 启发式采样器忽略策略能力的动态演化，造成样本效率低和收敛延迟。亟需将对抗场景从"攻击导向"转向"可学习性引导"。

Method: 1) 将对抗场景生成重构为偏好对齐问题，采用直接偏好优化引导生成器产生关键但可解的场景；2) 引入行为指纹捕获演化策略的内在特征，构建多模态能力预测模型，无需昂贵闭环仿真即可估计策略性能；3) 结合可解性对齐场景与能力预测，设计动态课程采样机制，优先采样针对当前策略弱点的场景。

Result: 在Waymo开放运动数据集上，AlignADV相比基线方法减少最高40.6%的训练步数，同时在正常和对抗交通条件下均降低了碰撞率并提升了路线完成率。

Conclusion: 这项工作标志着从攻击导向的场景生成到可学习性引导的策略提升的转变，为更安全高效的自动驾驶训练提供了原则性方向。

Abstract: Closed-loop adversarial training improves autonomous driving safety by exposing policies to rare safety-critical scenarios. Standard pipelines first generate adversarial scenarios and then sample them for policy optimization. However, most existing frameworks remain attack-oriented: collision-driven generators often synthesize unsolvable extreme situations, which can degrade learning, while heuristic samplers ignore the evolving capability of the driving policy, causing sample inefficiency and delayed convergence. We propose AlignADV, a learnability-guided closed-loop adversarial training framework that converts adversarial scenarios into resolvable and capability-aligned curricula. First, we reformulate adversarial scenario generation as a preference alignment problem and employ direct preference optimization to guide the generator toward critical yet resolvable scenarios. Second, we introduce behavioral fingerprints to capture the intrinsic characteristics of the evolving policy and construct a multi-modal capability prediction model that estimates policy performance without expensive closed-loop simulations. By combining resolvability-aligned scenarios with capability predictions, AlignADV develops a dynamic curriculum sampling mechanism that prioritizes scenarios targeting the current policy's vulnerabilities. Experiments on the Waymo Open Motion Dataset demonstrate that AlignADV improves convergence efficiency and final performance, reducing training steps by up to 40.6 percent compared with baseline methods while lowering collision rate and improving route completion under both normal and adversarial traffic conditions. These results highlight a shift from attack-oriented scenario generation to learnability-guided policy improvement, offering a principled direction for safer and more efficient autonomous driving training. Project page: https://meiyuewen.github.io/AlignADV/.

</details>


### [121] [ReactSim-Bench: Benchmarking Reactive Behavior World Model Simulation in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14058)
*Zhiyuan Zhang,Yanlun Peng,Jianing Zhang,Xianda Guo,Zehan Huang,Haoran Liu,Qifeng Li,Shaofeng Zhang,Xiaosong Jia,Junchi Yan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ReactSim-Bench基准，用于评估自动驾驶行为世界模型模拟器的反应能力，通过解耦AV与智能体控制、构建偏离日志的AV行为并引入碰撞、地图及运动学可行性指标，系统评估了多种SOTA模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶行为模拟基准未直接测量反应能力，通常让模拟器联合控制AV和周围智能体，通过日志相似性等指标评估，无法衡量智能体对AV偏离日志行为的可行响应。

Method: 解耦智能体与AV的控制，使用AV规划器模型生成候选行为，经规则和人工验证筛选后作为独立输入；构建2,636个测试场景（三类），采用碰撞、地图和运动学可行性指标评估反应的安全性与合规性。

Result: 系统评估了基于Transformer、扩散模型和下一token预测等多种架构的SOTA模型，分析了重规划频率对性能的影响。

Conclusion: ReactSim-Bench有效衡量了行为世界模型的反应能力，为未来研究提供了方向性见解。

Abstract: Reactive capability is a key property of data-driven behavior world model simulators for autonomous driving simulation systems. With this capability, simulated world agents can respond feasibly to autonomous vehicle (AV) behaviors that differ from the log. However, existing behavior simulation benchmarks do not directly measure reactive capability. They often let the simulator jointly control the AV and surrounding agents and evaluate realism through log similarity or open-loop prediction metrics. In this work, we introduce ReactSim-Bench for evaluating the reactive capability of behavior world model simulation in autonomous driving. We decouple the control of agents and the AV, using AV behaviors that differ from the log and require agents to respond as independent AV inputs. To obtain these AV behaviors, we construct a pipeline that uses an AV planner model to generate candidate behaviors and filters the data using rules and manual verification. Collision metrics, map-based metrics, and kinematic feasibility metrics are used to evaluate the safety and rule compliance of reactive responses. We construct 2,636 test scenarios with three categories and conduct a systematic evaluation of state-of-the-art models across multiple architectures, including Transformer-based, diffusion-based, and next-token-prediction-based models. We further analyze how replan frequency affects performance and provide insights for future studies.

</details>


### [122] [Semidefinite Relaxations for Collision-Free Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14063)
*Bernhard Paus Graesdal,Alexandre Amice,Pablo A. Parrilo,Russ Tedrake*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对无碰撞运动规划的半定松弛进行了理论分析，证明了凸松弛等价于高维空间中的全局最优运动规划，发现了对称性约简使得半定锥大小仅随多项式次数线性增长而不依赖环境维度，在求解速度上比直接非线性规划方法快10-100倍，并在最小加加速度四旋翼RRT规划中作为凸导向函数有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无碰撞运动规划本质上是非凸的困难问题，虽然半定松弛在机器人等领域广泛应用，但其理论性质（何时紧致、何时松弛）缺乏分析，本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 将运动规划精确建模为多项式曲线上的非凸优化问题，提出自然半定松弛；利用几何解释建立凸松弛与高维空间运动规划的等价性；通过对称性约简降低半定锥规模，使其仅与多项式次数线性相关。

Result: 凸松弛等价于高维空间中的全局最优运动规划；半定锥大小与多项式次数线性相关、与环境维度无关；求解速度比SNOPT和IPOPT快10-100倍且方差更低；能可靠找到原问题的局部最优路径。

Conclusion: 半定松弛是无碰撞运动规划高效且理论上可理解的工具，对称性约简显著降低了计算复杂度，在实际RRT规划器中作为凸导向函数表现良好。

Abstract: We study semidefinite relaxations for collision-free motion planning. We focus on a point robot moving from start to goal through spherical obstacles in $\mathbb{R}^n$, subject to path continuity constraints and squared derivative costs; a setting that is conceptually simple yet captures the hardness of collision-free motion planning. We formulate this problem exactly as a nonconvex problem over polynomial curves, and present a natural semidefinite relaxation. We contribute two key theoretical insights; to our knowledge this is the first theoretical analysis of semidefinite relaxations for collision-free motion planning. First, we show that solving the convex relaxation is equivalent to solving, to global optimality, a related motion planning problem in a potentially higher-dimensional space. This geometric interpretation yields necessary and sufficient conditions for tightness, and a clear intuition for when the relaxation is loose. Second, we show that the relaxation admits a symmetry reduction that makes it significantly smaller than one might expect, with positive semidefinite cone sizes that scale linearly with the polynomial degree and are independent of the ambient dimension. The resulting relaxation is 10 to 100 times faster than direct nonlinear programming transcriptions solved with SNOPT and IPOPT, exhibits significantly lower variance in solve times, and reliably finds a locally optimal path for the original problem. We demonstrate its effectiveness as a convex steering function in an RRT planner for minimum-snap quadrotor planning with $C^4$ continuous trajectories.

</details>


### [123] [Development of a 3 in Sewer Pipe Inspection Robot with an Articulated Differential Mechanism using X-shaped Linkages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14070)
*Shoya Umemura,Ryota Taniguchi,Atsushi Kakogawa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种改进型3节式下水道检测机器人，通过连接推进单元提升牵引力和越障能力，并利用差动机构和单线控制实现管道直径适应及障碍物检测，实验验证了其越障性能显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对第一版下水道检测机器人在牵引力不足和越障能力差方面的缺陷，提出改进方案以提升其通过性和实用性。

Method: 通过简单连接推进单元形成耦合结构，引入差动机构实现单线控制姿态变化以适应管道直径变化；同时设计基于驱动轮电机电流负载检测障碍物接触的控制方法，通过放松线缆实现越障。

Result: 在模拟管道实验中，通过驱动轮电流波形进行负载对比，验证了所提控制方法显著提升了铰接式机器人的越障能力。

Conclusion: 改进后的耦合推进单元和基于电流检测的越障控制策略有效增强了管道检测机器人的牵引性能和越障能力。

Abstract: This paper proposes, an improved version of the 3 in sewer pipe inspection robot equipped with an emergency evacuation mechanism. The low traction force and poor stepover capability, which were challenges of the first version, have been improved by simply connecting the propulsion units. The coupled propulsion units feature a differential mechanism capable of posture changes via a single wire, enabling adaptation to pipe diameter variations. To traverse obstacles like pipe joints, a control method was devised that detects obstacle contact through current load on the drive wheel motors and slackens the wire. This method was verified through simulated pipe experiments. Load comparisons were made using current waveforms applied to the drive wheels. Our proposed control method significantly improved the step-over capability of the new articulated robots.

</details>


### [124] [The N2D Haptic Glove: A Multi-Finger Glove for 2D Directional Force Feedback for Contact Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14083)
*Yao-Ting Huang,Jake Honma,Omar Hernandez,Logan Li,Kaitlin Calimbahin,Bryce Hackel,Michael C. Yip*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出N2D Haptic Glove，一种多指可穿戴触觉手套，通过绞盘驱动机构提供平面屈伸指尖力反馈。实验证明，相比仅视觉和单轴触觉基线，平面指尖力反馈显著降低接触力误差、提升操作一致性及用户体验。该设备将完全开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有触觉手套大多仅提供振动或单轴力反馈，缺乏方向性提示，导致用户在遥操作中依赖视觉推断接触力，造成过度按压、控制不一致及精度下降。

Method: 采用绞盘驱动传输机构，为多指提供平面屈伸方向（二维）的指尖力反馈，实现高透明度力渲染。

Result: 在遥操作用户研究中，相比视觉-only和单轴触觉基线，平面指尖力反馈显著降低了精确操作中的接触力误差，提升了试次间一致性及轴向探测任务中的用户体验。

Conclusion: N2D Haptic Glove及方向性指尖触觉设备是接触丰富遥操作、沉浸式VR仿真及机器人模仿学习的有效模态，且将开源以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Humans rely on directional fingertip forces to probe and regulate contact during manipulation, yet most wearable haptic gloves render only vibration or single-axis force, leaving force direction ambiguous. Without directional cues, users must infer contact force from vision alone, often leading to over-pressing, inconsistent control, and reduced precision in robotic teleoperation. We present the N2D Haptic Glove, a multi-finger wearable device that renders planar flexion-extension fingertip forces using capstan-drive transmissions for high-transparency force feedback. Through benchtop validations and a user study involving haptic teleoperation of a robotic arm and hand, we demonstrate that compared to visual-only and single-axis haptic baselines, planar fingertip feedback significantly reduces contact force error during precise manipulation, improves trial-to-trial consistency, and enhances overall user experience in axial probing tasks. These findings establish the N2D Haptic Glove and directional finger-based haptics devices as a promising modality for contact-rich teleoperation, immersive virtual reality simulations, and robot learning from demonstrations. N2D Haptic Glove's hardware and software system will be fully open-sourced at \href{https://ucsdarclab.github.io/n2d-glove/}{this https URL}.

</details>


### [125] [Self-Improving VLA Policies: Selected Diffusion Noise for Spurious-Robust Action Smoothing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14084)
*Duc Minh Nguyen,Bao-Ngoc Dao,Tung M. Luu,Binh Gia Nguyen,Vinh Tong,Anji Liu,Vu N. Duong,Dung D. Le,Daniel Sonntag,Trung Le,Ngan Le,Jan Peter,An Thai Le,Minh Nhat Vu,Mathias Niepert,Khoa D. Doan,Duy M. H. Nguyen,Vien Anh Ngo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SDN方法，通过在测试时选择扩散噪声向量，在不修改模型参数的情况下提升VLA策略的鲁棒性和成功率，在模拟和真实实验中分别提升+8%和+10%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于扩散的VLA策略对虚假视觉相关性和噪声动作生成敏感，导致在扰动下表现脆弱，现有方法缺乏简单有效的测试时鲁棒性增强手段。

Method: SDN在测试时动态采样与参考集最大分离的噪声向量以减少对虚假线索的依赖，同时选择能产生更一致动作轨迹的候选者，实现双目标优化，无需训练或修改模型参数。

Result: 在Google Robot和Widow-X模拟基准以及两个真实世界数据集上，对pi_0、Groot-N1.5、Groot-N1.6等多种VLA策略，SDN在模拟环境中成功率提升+8%，真实环境提升+10%，且动作更平滑稳定。

Conclusion: 扩散噪声选择可以作为测试时增强VLA策略的有效通用机制，显著提升鲁棒性和成功率。

Abstract: Diffusion-based Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies enable strong generalization in robotic manipulation, but remain sensitive to spurious visual correlations and noisy action generation, leading to brittle behavior under perturbations. We introduce Selected Diffusion Noise (SDN), a simple, training-free test-time method that improves both robustness and success rate by leveraging the diffusion noise space as a controllable degree of freedom. SDN dynamically samples noise vectors that are maximally separated from a reference set to mitigate reliance on spurious cues, while selecting candidates that yield more coherent action trajectories. This dual objective encourages stable behavior even under object-masked observations and reduces action jitter without modifying model parameters. We evaluate SDN on two simulation benchmarks (Google Robot, Widow-X) and two real-world robotic datasets across multiple VLA policies, including pi_0, Groot-N1.5, and Groot-N1.6. SDN consistently improves success rates by +8% in simulation and +10% in real-world settings, while producing smoother and more stable actions. Our results highlight that diffusion noise selection can serve as an effective and general mechanism for enhancing VLA policies at test time.

</details>


### [126] [A Modular Dual-Arm Apple Harvesting Robot with Enhanced Field Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14089)
*Keyi Zhu,Kyle Lammers,Chaaran Arunachalam,Kaixiang Zhang,Renfu Lu,Zhaojian Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种模块化双臂苹果采摘机器人，采用垂直堆叠双臂设计实现单树上下分区同时作业，集成了基础模型感知、七阶加加速度受限轨迹生成、线性扫描采摘策略、时序逻辑双臂协调策略等五项改进。在1738次田间试验中，单次成功率80.0%，平均单臂循环时间7.53秒，91.2%的采摘果实达到USDA特级标准，擦伤率2.4%-4.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人苹果采摘面临果园环境下吞吐量低、性能差的问题，阻碍其商业应用。现有水平双臂系统需要多树横向重新定位，效率低下。

Method: (1)基于基础模型的感知管线(Grounding-DINO + EfficientViT-SAM)；(2)七阶加加速度受限轨迹生成+控制障碍函数安全滤波器；(3)10cm缓冲区线性扫描+旋转脱离采摘策略；(4)基于时序逻辑的双臂协调策略+视觉-手臂异步调度；(5)在两个商业果园进行田间验证。

Result: 1738次臂循环，成功率80.0%，平均循环时间7.53秒，91.2%果实达到USDA特级标准，擦伤率2.4%-4.9%。

Conclusion: 该模块化机器人设计在进一步缩短采摘周期和改善密集枝叶遮挡处理的情况下，有潜力实现商业化的苹果采摘。

Abstract: Robotic apple harvesting offers a promising solution to labor shortages in commercial orchards, but low throughput and poor performance in orchard environments hinder its commercial adoption. This paper presents a modular dual-arm apple harvesting robot that uses a vertically stacked arms to enable simultaneous operation in the upper and lower zones of a single tree, simplifying platform positioning from multi-tree lateral repositioning to single-tree stops. Compared to our prior horizontal dual-arm system, the platform integrates 5 advances: (1)a foundation-model-based perception pipeline combining Grounding-DINO and EfficientViT-SAM for robust fruit localization in unstructured outdoor environments; (2)7th-order jerk-bounded trajectory generation paired with a Control Barrier Function safety filter to achieve fast yet safe arm motions; (3)a linear sweep harvesting strategy with a 10cm approach buffer and rotational detachment that improves picking reliability; (4)a temporal-logic-based dual-arm coordination policy with vision-arm async scheduling that maximizes usage of a shared vacuum source; and (5)field validation in 2 commercial orchards covering different apple varieties and tree architectures during the 2025 harvest season. Across the 1738 arm cycles collected in these field trials, the system achieved an 80.0% per-attempt success rate and a mean per-arm cycle time of 7.53s. Fruit damage assessments confirmed that 91.2% of robotically harvested fruit retained the highest USDA grade (Extra Fancy), with bruise rates between 2.4% and 4.9%. With further improvements in the picking cycle time and handling of heavy foliage occlusions, this new modular robot design holds promise for commercial harvesting of apples.

</details>


### [127] [GAIT: Legged Robot Proprioceptive State Estimation with Attention over Inertial-Leg Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14160)
*Young-Rang Seo,Hajun Kim,Sangmin Kim,Dongyun Kang,Hae-Won Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于惯性-腿部(IL)标记化的注意力网络方法，用于腿式机器人的本体状态估计。该方法将IMU和腿部测量值作为独立令牌，通过注意力机制根据接触状态动态重加权测量值，无需显式接触估计器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习型状态估计器将所有传感器测量值拼接为单一向量，无法区分不同测量来源的可靠性差异；而传统接触辅助估计器依赖显式接触估计和静止接触假设，泛化能力有限。

Method: 将惯性测量和腿部测量分别编码为独立令牌，采用注意力机制学习各测量的相对重要性权重，根据足部接触状态动态调整前向运动学测量的可信度，无需显式接触检测模块。

Result: 在Unitree Go1机器人上的实验（包括未建模的碎石地形和未见过的步态模式）表明：该方法在未见步态下优于现有学习型状态估计器，且性能超过基于接触辅助的模型方法。

Conclusion: 注意力机制能够隐式学习接触状态相关的测量可靠性，避免了传统接触辅助估计器的显式建模限制，在未知环境和步态下展现出更强的泛化能力。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a method that applies Inertial-Leg (IL) tokenization to an attention-based network for proprioceptive state estimation in legged robots. Unlike existing learning-based state estimators that concatenate all sensor measurements into a single flat vector, the proposed architecture represents inertial measurements and leg-wise measurements as individual tokens and uses an attention mechanism to learn the relative importance of each measurement.This design allows the network to reweight each measurement according to the current contact condition, reflecting the fact that the reliability of forward kinematic measurements depends on whether the corresponding foot is in contact. Unlike conventional contact-aided estimators, however, the proposed method learns this behavior without relying on an explicit contact estimator or on explicit measurement updates based on a stationary contact assumption. To validate the proposed method, we conducted experiments on a Unitree Go1 robot, including debris terrain not modeled in simulation and gait patterns not seen during training. Experimental results show that the proposed method achieves better estimation performance than existing learning-based state estimators under unseen gait patterns and also improves performance over contact-aided model-based methods.

</details>


### [128] [Robustness without Wrinkles: Parallel Simulation and Robust MPC for Certified Deformable Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14188)
*Wei-Chen Li,Jeffrey Fang,Sasanka Polisetti,Yuexi Song,Glen Chou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CORD-SLS是一种基于GPU并行可微模拟器的实时控制方法，用于安全操作绳索和布料等可变形物体，通过输出反馈鲁棒模型预测控制（MPC）和共形预测校准实现了高速、高安全性的规划与控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在可变形物体操作中难以同时满足实时性、安全性和接触丰富场景下的鲁棒性要求，尤其是在模型和感知存在不确定性的情况下。

Method: 提出CORD-SLS框架，核心包括：(1) GPU并行可微模拟器，通过接触平滑实现基于梯度的规划；(2) 实时GPU并行输出反馈鲁棒MPC算法，利用该模拟器在不确定性下满足约束；(3) 使用共形预测校准视觉反馈和感知误差边界，生成可达管以实现高概率安全控制。

Result: 在绳索和布料的高维、接触丰富操作任务（如避障、布线、折叠、平滑）中，CORD-SLS实现了毫秒级规划速度，在安全性、速度和任务成功率上超越基线方法。

Conclusion: CORD-SLS通过结合可微模拟、鲁棒MPC和共形预测，在可变形物体操作中实现了实时、安全且鲁棒的控制，在仿真和实际硬件上均表现优异。

Abstract: We present CORD-SLS, a real-time control method for safe deformable object manipulation, with a focus on ropes and cloth. At its core is a GPU-parallel differentiable simulator with contact smoothing which enables efficient gradient-based planning through intermittent contact. To robustly satisfy constraints under model and sensing uncertainty, we develop a real-time, GPU-parallel output-feedback robust model predictive control (MPC) algorithm that plans with this simulator. We further show that the simulator accelerates model-based RL for training neural manipulation policies. To improve real-world robustness, we use conformal prediction to calibrate visual-feedback and perception-error bounds for MPC, producing reachable tubes that enable high-probability safe control. We evaluate CORD-SLS on high-dimensional, contact-rich rope and cloth manipulation tasks in simulation and hardware, including obstacle avoidance, routing, folding, and smoothing. Across settings, CORD-SLS achieves millisecond-speed planning, exceeding baselines in safety, speed, and task success.

</details>


### [129] [Short-Horizon Position Accuracy of Single-Track Models: Implications for Motion Planning of Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14216)
*Aron J. Aertssen,Lars A. T. H. van Alen,Igo J. M. Besselink,Rudolf G. M. Huisman,René M. J. G. van de Molengraft*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过实车试验系统比较了三种单轨车辆模型在短时域内的位置精度，揭示了模型复杂度、参数化质量与位置精度之间的权衡关系，为模型预测控制中的模型选择提供依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏对车辆模型位置精度的系统性实验评估，而位置精度直接影响自动驾驶轨迹规划的可行性与安全性。

Method: 使用仪器化测试车辆，通过专用实验辨识三种单轨车辆模型的参数，并在多种驾驶工况下比较模型预测位置与实测位置的短时域精度。

Result: 不同模型在不同工况下表现各异，没有单一模型在所有场景中绝对最优，模型复杂度与位置精度之间存在明确权衡关系。

Conclusion: 模型选择应基于具体应用场景中复杂度、参数化质量与位置精度的综合权衡，而非追求单一最优模型。

Abstract: Accurate and computationally efficient vehicle models are essential for motion planning of autonomous vehicles, where positional accuracy directly affects trajectory feasibility and safety. However, the positional accuracy has not been systematically evaluated against real measurements. Therefore, this paper compares the short-horizon positional accuracy of three single-track vehicle models against vehicle measurements across various driving maneuvers. Model parameters are identified through dedicated experiments with the instrumented test vehicle. Rather than identifying a single best model, this work aims to provide insight into the trade-offs between model complexity, parameterization quality, and positional accuracy for informed model selection in Model Predictive Control applications.

</details>


### [130] [Universal Manipulation Exoskeleton: Learning Compliant Whole-body Policies with Real-time Torque Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14218)
*Litian Liang,Jingxi Xu,Xinda Qi,Yujun Cai,Houzhu Ding,Luqi Wang,Zhixin Sun,Jyh-Herng Chow,Ming Yang,Mark Cutkosky*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种低成本、轻量级的上肢外骨骼系统UME，能够提供实时触觉力矩反馈并记录全身配置和关节力矩信号用于遥操作，支持学习主动柔顺策略，在高度受限空间中实现有效的双臂、全身和主动柔顺操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据采集流程大多缺乏捕获力和力矩数据的能力，无法用于学习主动柔顺策略，而机器人在家庭环境中安全工作需要具备顺应性并能响应接触时的力矩和力反馈。

Method: 设计了一种名为UME的上肢外骨骼，配备嵌入式IMU，提供透明触觉力矩反馈；提出通用重定向算法，可遥操作多种机器人（7DoF OpenArm、7DoF Franka、6DoF X-ARM）。

Result: 在高度受限空间中实现了双臂、全身和主动柔顺策略，包括长程移动操作、力介导的箱体翻转、视觉遮挡箱体推送和空间受限桌面操作等任务，均取得高成功率。

Conclusion: UME结合透明触觉反馈与通用重定向算法，能够有效学习强约束空间下的主动柔顺操作策略，为家庭环境中机器人安全操作提供了可行方案。

Abstract: For robots to work safely in household environments, they need to be compliant and react to torque and force feedback during contact. However, the majority of existing data collection pipelines still lack the ability to capture force and torque data for learning active compliant policies. In this paper, we present Universal Manipulation Exoskeleton (UME), an upper-limb exoskeleton that provides real-time haptic torque feedback while recording whole-arm configurations and joint torque signals for teleoperation. With transparent torque feedback, human operators can even unsheathe kinematically constrained objects while blindfolded. UME is low-cost, lightweight, and portable. Equipped with an embedded IMU, it enables teleoperation for mobile manipulation. With our proposed universal retargeting algorithm, UME can teleoperate a range of robots, including the 7DoF OpenArm, 7DoF Franka, and 6DoF X-ARM. We demonstrate that this combination of capabilities enables learning bimanual, whole-body, and active compliant policies that operate effectively in highly constrained spaces. The learned robust autonomous policies achieve high success rates across a variety of tasks, including long-horizon mobile manipulation, force-mediated box flipping, visually occluded box pushing, and space-constrained tabletop manipulation. Videos, code, and additional information can be found at https://ume-exo.github.io.

</details>


### [131] [Selective Agentic Recovery for UAV Autonomy with a Persistent Mission Runtime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14219)
*Taewoo Park,Kyeonghyun Yoo,Seunghyun Yoo,Hwangnam Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出持久任务运行时（PMR）框架，通过学习型调用价值评估（learned-CVI）选择性地调用外部智能体推理，在保持本地自主飞行的同时，仅在必要时请求远程恢复决策，在无人机故障场景中将成功率从5.0%提升至95.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机在本地路径点执行遇到阻塞通道、无进度或任务级歧义时，需要高层恢复推理。但远程智能体推理每次调用都会引入延迟、资源成本和后端不确定性，因此需要选择性调用机制。

Method: 提出PMR框架：保持任务循环和关键执行本地化，外部智能体推理器仅作为按需恢复模块；提出learned-CVI，通过紧凑的准入门控网络估计远程推理能否显著提升近期任务进展，从而决定是否调用。

Result: 在400次Gazebo/PX4基准测试中，hard/ambiguous场景成功率从5.0%提升至95.0%；相比一次性调用和周期性调用基线分别高出20.0和32.5个百分点；相比手动规则基线减少16.7%的远程调用和29.2%的token消耗。

Conclusion: 有选择性地调用外部智能体推理可以有效平衡自主性与远程推理开销，learned-CVI在提升任务成功率的同时显著降低了调用成本和资源消耗。

Abstract: Agentic AI can support unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) autonomy by providing high-level recovery reasoning when local waypoint- or setpoint-based execution encounters blocked passages, repeated no-progress behavior, or mission-level ambiguity. On physical UAVs, however, remote reasoning is most useful when it is invoked selectively, since each call introduces latency, resource cost, backend uncertainty, and a need to validate the returned decision. This paper presents Persistent Mission Runtime (PMR), a UAV recovery framework that keeps the mission loop and safety-critical execution local while using an external agentic reasoner only as an on-demand recovery module. The reasoner selects from predefined recovery skills, and each returned decision is parsed, verified, safety-filtered, and mapped to local executor actions before it can affect flight. PMR introduces learned Cognitive Value of Invocation (learned-CVI), a compact admission gate that estimates when remote agentic reasoning is likely to improve near-term mission progress enough to justify its operational cost. Across a fixed 400-run Gazebo/PX4 benchmark with eight scenarios, learned-CVI raises hard/ambiguous-regime success from 5.0% under local-only autonomy to 95.0%, outperforms one-shot and periodic reasoning baselines by 20.0 and 32.5 percentage points, and reduces remote-agent calls by 16.7% and logged tokens by 29.2% relative to a manually tuned rule-based invocation baseline.

</details>


### [132] [BIM-Loc: BIM-Integrated Discrepancy-Aware LiDAR-based Indoor Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14237)
*Yinqiang Zhang,Liang Lu,Yipeng Pan,Maolin Lei,Yuhan Xie,Zhanteng Xie,Xiaowei Luo,Jia Pan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BIM-Loc是一种利用建筑信息模型（BIM）进行LiDAR定位的方法，通过多命中射线投射、BIM因子图优化和层次化贝叶斯推断，实现精准定位和在线偏差检测，在特征稀疏的室内环境中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统定位方法在特征稀疏的室内环境中因缺乏明显地标而表现不佳；先验地图虽能增强鲁棒性但难以获取或更新。本文旨在利用设计阶段的BIM模型直接实现定位，无需预先构建高精度地图。

Method: (1) 多命中射线投射策略实现BIM与点云的高效数据关联，将3D观测投影到2D纹理空间；(2) 结合BIM因子的位姿图优化框架，保障里程计、扫描序列与BIM结构之间的一致性；(3) 层次化贝叶斯推断模块，增量更新连续2D表面表示以检测偏差，实现从像素到结构层的更新传播。

Result: 在仿真和真实场景中的广泛评估表明，BIM-Loc在定位精度和鲁棒性上显著优于最先进的基于地图的方法。

Conclusion: BIM-Loc成功利用设计阶段的BIM模型实现了特征稀疏室内环境下的鲁棒定位，并具备在线偏差检测能力，为机器人定位提供了一种无需先验地图的新方案。

Abstract: Accurate and robust localization is a fundamental requirement for service and inspection robots, particularly in feature-sparse indoor environments where traditional systems struggle due to a lack of distinct landmarks. While prior maps can enhance robustness, precise and compact maps capturing real-world details are often unavailable for new or frequently changing environments. This paper presents BIM-Loc, a novel discrepancy-aware LiDAR-based localization method that directly integrates Building Information Models (BIM) from the design phase. BIM-Loc simultaneously estimates trajectories aligned with the BIM coordinate system and identifies discrepancies between real-world observations and the as-designed BIM in an online fashion. Our core contributions include: (1) a novel multi-hit ray casting strategy for efficient BIM-point data association and projection of 3D observations into 2D texture space; (2) a pose graph optimization framework with BIM-integrated factors that enforces consistency among odometry, sequential scans, and BIM structures; and (3) a hierarchical Bayesian inference module that incrementally updates a continuous 2D surface representation for discrepancy detection, propagating updates from the pixel to the structure level. Extensive evaluations in both simulation and real-world applications demonstrate that BIM-Loc significantly outperforms state-of-the-art map-based methods in localization accuracy and robustness.

</details>


### [133] [When and How Severely: Scenario-Specific Safety Envelopes for Driving VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14238)
*Abhinaw Priyadershi,Jelena Frtunikj*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对VLA驾驶规划器的ISO 21448（SOTIF）安全认证问题，提出ODD规范需要二维安全包络：即同时回答“何时开始失效”和“失效严重程度如何”。通过对Alpamayo R1模型在15,968个样本上的评估，发现聚合安全阈值（σ≤50, ADE≤15%）会掩盖容忍更高噪声（σ=70）的场景；GMM识别出6个离散严重度等级；STOP_SIGNAL场景虽容忍更大噪声，但其高严重度（C4/C5）占比约为LANE_KEEPING的4倍，表明单一聚合值不足以描述安全边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA驾驶规划器的SOTIF安全认证依赖于ODD规范，但通常仅使用聚合阈值（如ADE预算下的σ上限）来定义安全边界，未能区分不同场景在失效阈值与失效严重性之间的非单调关系，导致安全评估不完整。

Method: 在15,968个（clip, attack）样本上评估Alpamayo R1（10B参数VLA模型），采用保守聚合阈值分析（σ≤50, ADE≤15%）与GMM聚类（BIC最优k=6）两种方法，分别分析失效阈值分布和失效严重度分布，并将二者联合分析。

Result: 1）保守聚合阈值σ≤50掩盖了部分场景容忍σ=70的噪声。2）GMM识别出6个离散严重度等级，相同均值误差的场景在高严重度（C4/C5）占比上差异显著。3）联合分析发现：STOP_SIGNAL场景相比LANE_KEEPING容忍更大σ噪声，但其C4/C5占比高出约4倍。

Conclusion: 部署级VLA驾驶规划器的SOTIF ODD规范需要二维安全包络（失效阈值×失效严重度），而非对每个危险源仅使用单一聚合值。

Abstract: Safety certification of Vision-Language-Action (VLA) driving planners under ISO 21448 (SOTIF) rests on an Operational Design Domain (ODD) specification that answers two complementary questions: when does the planner start to fail, and how severely does it fail once it does? We evaluate Alpamayo R1, a 10B-parameter open-weight driving VLA, on 15,968 (clip, attack) pairs. We find a conservative-aggregate gap: an aggregate safe threshold of $σ\leq 50$ under a 15% average displacement error (ADE) budget masks well-sampled scenarios that tolerate the top of the tested grid ($σ= 70$). A Gaussian Mixture Model (GMM) on the changed-explanation subset identifies six discrete severity bands (BIC-optimal $k{=}6$), so two perturbation conditions with the same mean error can differ materially in their share of high-severity (C4/C5) failures. Joining the two analyses on the same corpus surfaces a finding neither yields in isolation: the scenarios with the loosest noise thresholds are not those with the lowest high-severity rate: STOP_SIGNAL concentrates roughly $4\times$ the C4/C5 share of LANE_KEEPING despite tolerating a larger $σ$. A deployable SOTIF ODD specification for driving VLAs therefore requires a two-dimensional safety envelope, not a single aggregate value per hazard.

</details>


### [134] [SyLink Hand: A Synergy-Inspired Linkage-Driven Anthropomorphic Hand for Human-Like Dexterity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14250)
*Hao Wu,Yanzhe Wang,Yu Feng,Yitong Li,Jingxiang Guo,Jian Liu,Jianshu Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SyLink Hand，一种受人类手部协同作用启发的仿人灵巧手，通过连杆传动机构在紧凑低成本架构中实现高仿人度，19个关节由11个驱动器驱动，重520g，成本约400美元，实验验证其类人运动学、高负载能力和灵巧抓取操作能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计仿人机械手时，在功能灵巧性与机械简单性之间取得平衡仍是重大挑战。现有设计往往难以同时实现高仿人度、结构紧凑和成本可控，需要借鉴生物力学协同原理来简化驱动配置。

Method: 通过动作捕捉手套分析自然手部运动中关节的强运动学相关性，确定简化但功能完整的自由度配置；基于协同特性设计优化连杆机构协调多关节运动并复现自然手指轨迹；提出新型球面四杆机构实现掌指关节屈伸/外展内收的解耦运动。

Result: 原型采用11个驱动器驱动19个关节，总重520g，制造成本约400美元。实验验证了类人运动学性能、高负载能力和多样化的抓取与操作技能。

Conclusion: 协同启发的连杆式设计有效平衡了仿人度、机械简单性和功能多样性，在需要灵巧操作的机器人应用中具有实用部署潜力。

Abstract: Designing anthropomorphic robotic hands that balance functional dexterity with mechanical simplicity remains a significant challenge. Inspired by human hand synergies, this paper presents the SyLink Hand, an anthropomorphic dexterous hand that integrates biomechanical synergy principles with linkage-driven transmission mechanisms to achieve a high degree of anthropomorphism in appearance, kinematics, and functionality within a compact and cost-effective architecture. Biomechanical analysis of natural hand motions using motion capture gloves reveals strong kinematic correlations among hand joints, providing the basis for a simplified yet functional degree-of-freedom (DOF) configuration. Guided by these synergistic characteristics, optimized linkage mechanisms are employed to coordinate multiple joint motions and reproduce natural finger trajectories. A novel spherical four-bar linkage is further proposed to achieve decoupled flexion/extension (Flex/Ext) and abduction/adduction (Abd/Add) at the metacarpophalangeal joint within a compact form factor. The resulting prototype integrates 19 joints driven by 11 actuators, with a total mass of 520g and a manufacturing cost of approximately USD 400. Experimental evaluations demonstrate its human-like kinematic performance, high load-bearing capability, and versatile grasping and manipulation skills. These results validate that the synergy-inspired, linkage-based design effectively balances anthropomorphism, mechanical simplicity, and functional versatility, highlighting its potential for practical deployment in dexterity-demanding robotic applications.

</details>


### [135] [Optimality-Preserving Decomposition for Scalable QAOA in Natural-Language-Guided Multi-Drone Assignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14252)
*Junyeop Bang,Byongho Lee,Dohyun An,Hwangnam Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种端到端框架，将微调的大语言模型前端与量子-经典混合后端相结合，用于解决多无人机编队的区域分配问题。前端通过SFT和DPO将自然语言指令转换为QUBO约束，后端利用约束保持图分割器、基于分隔符的动态规划合并以及CVaR-QAOA来克服量子比特限制，在理想情况下100%恢复全局最优解，在真实QAOA采样下达到96.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多无人机编队扩展时，区域分配成为NP-hard组合优化问题，经典穷举搜索无法处理；而量子优化虽有望突破经典瓶颈，但将人类意图映射到受限量子硬件存在严重挑战。

Method: 1) 前端：使用Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT)和Direct Preference Optimization (DPO)微调LLM，将自然语言指令转换为结构鲁棒的QUBO约束；2) 后端：提出约束保持图分割器和压缩的基于分隔符的动态规划(DP)合并方法；3) 使用W-state初始化和XY-mixers的Conditional Value-at-Risk Quantum Approximate Optimization (CVaR-QAOA)进行优化。

Result: 该架构在理想化oracle案例上100%恢复全局最优解，在真实QAOA采样下达到96.3%的全局最优恢复率，实现了自然语言引导的任务分配在以前不可扩展的规模上。

Conclusion: 该端到端框架成功绕过了经典计算的扩展壁垒，将自然语言理解与量子优化相结合，为大规模组合优化问题提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: As multi-drone fleets scale, zone assignment rapidly evolves into an intractable NP-hard combinatorial problem that overwhelms classical exhaustive search. While quantum optimization promises to shatter these classical bottlenecks, mapping complex spatial tasks from human intent to restricted quantum hardware remains a severe challenge. To bridge this gap, we present an end-to-end framework integrating a fine-tuned Large Language Model (LLM) front-end with a highly scalable, domain-specific quantum-classical backend. The front-end utilizes Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) and Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) to translate free-form natural language instructions into structurally robust Quadratic Unconstrained Binary Optimization (QUBO) constraints without false negatives. To overcome the strict qubit limits of near-term quantum devices, our framework features a novel constraint-preserving graph partitioner and a compressed separator-based dynamic programming (DP) merge. By structurally encoding constraints via W-state initialization and XY-mixers in Conditional Value-at-Risk Quantum Approximate Optimization (CVaR-QAOA), the pipeline stays highly compact. Empirical results demonstrate that this architecture circumvents classical scaling walls, recovering the global optimum on 100% of idealized oracle cases and 96.3% under real QAOA sampling, enabling natural-language-guided task allocation at previously intractable scales.

</details>


### [136] [ReactVLA: Fast and Lightweight Reactive Robot Manipulation via Improved Mean Flow Action Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14255)
*Yanzhao Guo,Wenkai Chen,Jianwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReactVLA是一个轻量级、低延迟的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）框架，通过改进的Mean Flow（iMF）动作生成器将多步扩散采样减少到单步或几步，并引入Attention Residuals（AttnRes）动态深度特征路由机制，在LIBERO、RoboIMI等仿真基准和真实机器人操作任务中，相比同类VLA基线（如SmolVLA和π₀），任务性能提升高达1.65倍，推理速度提升超过4倍，实际策略延迟降至38.6ms以下，实现快速反应式控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于扩散的VLA策略虽然能建模表达性强且多模态的动作分布，但其迭代采样过程引入了显著的推理延迟，限制了其在反应式闭环机器人操作中的应用。

Method: 提出了ReactVLA框架，包含两个互补设计：(1) 改进的Mean Flow (iMF) 动作生成器，将昂贵的多步扩散采样缩减为一步到几步的动作生成；(2) Attention Residuals (AttnRes)，一种动态深度特征路由机制，替代均匀残差累积以更好地保留与任务相关的多模态表示。

Result: 在LIBERO、RoboIMI等大规模仿真基准和真实机器人操作任务中，ReactVLA一致优于同类VLA基线（SmolVLA和π₀），在精密操作任务中任务性能提升高达1.65倍，推理速度提升超过4倍，实际策略延迟降至38.6ms以下。

Conclusion: ReactVLA通过减少扩散采样步数和优化特征路由，有效解决了扩散VLA策略的高延迟问题，为实时反应式机器人操作提供了可行的轻量级解决方案。

Abstract: Diffusion-based Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies have demonstrated strong capability in modeling expressive and multimodal action distributions. However, their reliance on iterative sampling introduces substantial inference latency, which limits their applicability to reactive closed-loop robot manipulation. To address this limitation, we propose \texttt{ReactVLA}, a lightweight and low-latency VLA framework for real-time robotic manipulation. \texttt{ReactVLA} combines two complementary designs: (1) an improved Mean Flow (iMF) action generator that reduces expensive multi-step diffusion sampling to one-to-few-step action generation, and (2) Attention Residuals (AttnRes), a dynamic depth-wise feature routing mechanism that replaces uniform residual accumulation to better preserve task-relevant multimodal representations. We evaluate \texttt{ReactVLA} on large-scale simulation benchmarks, including LIBERO and RoboIMI, as well as real-world robotic manipulation tasks. Experimental results show that \texttt{ReactVLA} consistently outperforms similarly sized VLA baselines, including SmolVLA and $π_0$. On challenging precision manipulation tasks, \texttt{ReactVLA} achieves up to a 1.65$\times$ improvement in task performance while providing more than a 4$\times$ increase in inference speed compared with leading VLA models. Finally, it reduces real-world policy latency to below 38.6 ms, enabling fast reactive control on physical robot platforms. Please check out our project website at: https://game-loader.github.io/ReactVLA/.

</details>


### [137] [FloVerse: Floor Plan-Guided Multi-Modal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14267)
*Weiqi Huang,Shuangyi Dong,Jiaxin Li,Yifei Guo,Zan Wang,Wei Liang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FloVerse任务，统一了基于楼层平面图引导的PointNav、ObjectNav和ImageNav三种具身导航范式，并构建了包含1.6K场景、240K专家轨迹和12M RGBD帧的大规模数据集FloVerse-1.6K。同时提出ThreeDiff两阶段模仿学习策略，包含基于扩散模型的多模态目标推理规划器和深度轨迹精炼器，实验证明楼层平面先验能有效提升导航性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有楼层平面图引导的导航工作主要局限于PointNav和少量环境，缺乏统一的多模态导航框架和大规模基准。为解决这一空白，本文旨在统一PointNav、ObjectNav和ImageNav三种导航任务，并构建大规模数据集和高效算法。

Method: 提出ThreeDiff两阶段模仿学习策略：(1) 规划器Planner，包含基于扩散模型的多模态目标推理模块，通过掩码模态建模（masked-modality modeling）学习；(2) 精炼器Refiner，基于深度信息的轨迹精炼模块用于安全执行。数据集FloVerse-1.6K包含来自HM3D和Gibson 4+的1.6K场景及其对应的楼层平面图。

Result: (1) 楼层平面先验在所有目标模态（PointNav、ObjectNav、ImageNav）下均能提升导航性能；(2) ThreeDiff能够隐式地从楼层平面图中捕获空间信息。

Conclusion: 楼层平面图作为空间先验在具身导航中具有显著有效性，本文提出的统一方法FloVerse为楼层平面图引导的具身导航提供了新的范式，验证了空间先验在多种导航任务中的通用价值。

Abstract: Floor plans encapsulate compact spatial priors, enabling agents to navigate unseen scenes more efficiently. While prior work has explored floor plan-guided navigation, it has focused mainly on PointNav and a limited set of environments. To bridge this gap, we introduce FloVerse, a new task for floor plan-guided embodied navigation that unifies PointNav, ObjectNav, and ImageNav. To support FloVerse, we assemble FloVerse-1.6K, a large-scale dataset of 1.6K scenes from HM3D and Gibson 4+, paired with corresponding floor plans, comprising 240K expert trajectories and 12M RGBD frames. We further propose ThreeDiff, a two-stage imitation learning policy comprising a planner, a diffusion-based multimodal goal-reasoning module trained via masked-modality modeling, and a refiner, a depth-based trajectory-refinement module for safe execution. Extensive experiments demonstrate that (1) floor-plan priors improve navigation performance across all goal modalities, and (2) ThreeDiff implicitly captures spatial information from floor plans. These results underscore the effectiveness of spatial priors and validate our proposed unified approach for floor plan-guided embodied navigation.

</details>


### [138] [Robust Fall Recovery for Armless Bipedal-Wheeled Robots Via Force-Guided Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14270)
*Haidong Hou,Zhangguo Yu,Tao Han,Hengbo Qi,Khaleel Ghazal,Yu Zhang,Yidong Du,Xuechao Chen,Fei Meng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FTSR框架，通过力引导的约束强化学习与阶段式奖励机制，使无臂双轮腿机器人在仿真中学会跌倒恢复，并成功迁移到实体机器人，同时泛化到高自由度人形机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无臂双轮腿机器人因缺乏手臂或其他肢体的支撑辅助，仅靠腿部驱动完成跌倒恢复极其困难，现有方法难以解决这一挑战。

Method: 采用力引导方法在仿真训练中构建与机器人实时高度直接关联的外部辅助力，将其形式化为可优化约束；通过约束强化学习逐步降低力依赖并提升身体高度；设计高度渐进式的阶段奖励机制来结构化恢复过程；结合教师-学生架构蒸馏力效应与恢复动态的先验知识。

Result: 在实体无臂双轮腿机器人上验证了多种挑战条件下的可靠跌倒恢复能力，展示了强环境适应性与运动鲁棒性，且恢复后保持完整运动能力；方法有效泛化到高自由度人形机器人。

Conclusion: FTSR框架成功解决了无臂双轮腿机器人的跌倒恢复难题，具备良好的泛化能力和实用价值。

Abstract: Fall recovery is critical for autonomous legged locomotion. Existing methods have demonstrated that some legged robots, such as humanoids and quadrupeds, are capable of fall recovery from diverse postures by utilizing arms or coordinating multi-legs to generate support forces. Without arms or other legs to provide supportive assistance, a bipedal-wheeled robot must rely solely on the actuation of its legs, making recovery particularly difficult. To address this, we introduce FTSR (Force-guided Teacher-student framework with Stage-wise Rewards). The force-guided method constructs an external auxiliary force during simulation training that correlates directly with the robot's real-time height, explicitly formulating this force as an optimizable constraint. Through constrained reinforcement learning, the policy is guided toward reducing force dependency gradually and increasing the body height, developing internal recovery strategies despite having no arms for support. Height-progressive stage-Wise rewards progressively structure posture stabilization during recovery and transition to sustained locomotion, integrated with teacher-student architecture distilling privileged knowledge of force effects and recovery dynamics. After simulation training, the policy is deployed on a physical armless bipedal-wheeled robot and extensively evaluated. Experiments confirm robust and reliable fall recovery under diverse challenging conditions, demonstrating strong environmental adaptability and motion robustness, while maintaining full post-recovery motion capability. The framework also generalizes effectively to a high-DOF humanoid, confirming its practical generalizability. The project page is available at https://2350575870.github.io/force-guided.github.io/

</details>


### [139] [Elastic Queries Reinforcement Learning: Self-Aware Policy Execution for VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14375)
*Ge Wang,Xinyu Tan,Xiang Li,Man Luo,Chengsi Yao,Shenhao Yan,Jiahao Yang,Fan Feng,Honghao Cai,Xiangyuan Wang,Zhixin Mai,Yiming Zhao,Yatong Han,Zhen Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出弹性查询强化学习（EQRL）框架，让VLA模型在机器人操作中根据状态难度动态调整推理预算、去噪步数和动作块长度，在不微调基础模型的前提下降低推理成本并保持或提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型采用固定的推理和重规划调度，忽略机器人控制中不同状态（如接触丰富或不确定状态 vs. 简单状态）的难度差异，导致计算资源浪费或关键状态响应不足。

Method: 1) 设计轻量级潜在调度适配器，联合选择潜在输入、去噪预算和动作块长度；2) 训练评论家网络对联合潜在-调度动作进行评估，利用评论家集成的分歧度导出状态难度信号；3) 将可变块执行建模为查询级宏观动作强化学习，引入块依赖折扣和摊销的NFE预算。

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人操作实验中，EQRL在降低摊销推理成本的同时保持或提升了任务成功率。

Conclusion: 弹性调度策略能够有效感知状态难度，将计算资源合理分配到困难状态，从而在效率与性能之间取得更好的平衡。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models are powerful action generators for robot manipulation, but they are typically executed with fixed inference and replanning schedules. This rigidity ignores the uneven difficulty of robot control: contact-rich or uncertain states may need more computation and fresher feedback, while easier states can often be handled with fewer inference steps and longer open-loop execution. We propose Elastic Queries Reinforcement Learning (EQRL), a framework that makes each VLA policy query elastic. A lightweight latent-schedule adaptor jointly selects the latent input, denoising budget, and action chunk length, without fine-tuning the underlying VLA model. To make scheduling difficulty-aware, EQRL trains a critic over the joint latent-schedule action and derives a state difficulty signal from critic ensemble disagreement. This signal guides compute toward difficult states, while a learned residual allows task-driven correction. We formulate variable chunk execution as query-level macro-action RL with chunk-dependent discounting and an amortized number-of-function-evaluations (NFE) budget. Across simulation and real-robot manipulation, EQRL reduces amortized inference cost while preserving or improving task success.

</details>


### [140] [Hy-Embodied-0.5-VLA: From Vision-Language-Action Models to a Real-World Robot Learning Stack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14409)
*He Zhang,Lingzhu Xiang,Haitao Lin,Zeyu Huang,Minghui Wang,Dingyan Zhong,Yubo Dong,Yihao Wu,Yongming Rao,Dongsheng Zhang,Wanjia He,Ling Chen,Kai Huang,Jiahao Chen,Sichang Su,Xumin Yu,Ziyi Wang,Chengwei Zhu,Xiao Teng,Yuchun Guo,Yufeng Zhang,Yuandong Liu,Rui Wang,Zisheng Lu,Han Hu,Zhengyou Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HyVLA-0.5是一个完整的端到端机器人学习系统，涵盖数据收集、模型设计、持续预训练、监督微调、强化学习后训练和真实环境部署全流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建一个完整的、端到端的机器人学习系统，覆盖从数据收集到真实环境部署的全栈流程，以验证各组件在整体系统中的协同作用。

Method: 采用端到端框架，包括数据收集、模型设计、持续预训练、监督微调、RL后训练和真实世界部署六个环节。

Result: 成功构建了Hy-Embodied-0.5-VLA (HyVLA-0.5)系统，实现了全栈机器人学习流程。

Conclusion: 该报告展示了完整机器人学习栈中各组件如何协同工作，为端到端机器人学习提供了系统化方案。

Abstract: In this report, we present Hy-Embodied-0.5-VLA, abbreviated as HyVLA-0.5, an end-to-end system that spans the full robot learning stack: data collection, model design, continued pre-training and supervised fine-tuning, RL post-training, and real-world deployment. Each component serves a distinct role in this stack.

</details>


### [141] [ForestBack: Breadcrumb-Based Pedestrian Dead Reckoning for Infrastructure-Free Return Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14421)
*Aueaphum Aueawatthanaphisut,Chanakan Chaipan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ForestBack，一种基于面包屑行人航位推算（PDR）的无基础设施回程导航框架。系统将用户行走路径记录为可逆面包屑节点序列，无需GPS、Wi-Fi、蓝牙信标或预装基础设施即可生成反向路径引导。实验表明，与传统PDR相比，ForestBack将均方根误差从1.129米降至0.965米（改进15.76%），最终位置误差从1.781米降至1.388米，转向事件检测一致性达99.90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在GPS拒止环境中，可靠的回程导航仍然是一个重要挑战，因为外部定位基础设施可能不可用或不可靠。现有方法依赖基础设施（如Wi-Fi、蓝牙信标），限制了在无设施环境中的适用性。

Method: ForestBack框架集成了基于加速度的步数检测、自适应步长估计、磁力计辅助航向估计、气压计高度校正以及双向面包屑路径重建，实现无基础设施的行人回程导航。

Result: 基于36次步行试验和42,474个时间序列样本的评估表明：均方根误差从1.129米降至0.965米（改进15.76%），最终位置误差从1.781米降至1.388米，转向事件检测一致性达99.90%。

Conclusion: ForestBack在避障场景中改善了轨迹重建和路径保持的回程引导效果，发布的数据集和分析笔记支持无基础设施PDR回程导航系统的可复现性和未来基准测试。

Abstract: Reliable return navigation remains an important challenge in GPS-denied environments where external positioning infrastructure may be unavailable or unreliable. This paper presents ForestBack, an infrastructure-free pedestrian return navigation framework based on breadcrumb-based pedestrian dead reckoning (PDR). The system records a user's walking route as a sequence of reversible breadcrumb nodes and generates reverse-path guidance without requiring GPS, Wi-Fi, Bluetooth beacons, or pre-installed infrastructure. ForestBack integrates acceleration-based step detection, adaptive step-length estimation, magnetometer-assisted heading estimation, barometric-altitude correction, and bidirectional breadcrumb path reconstruction. The system was evaluated using an indoor obstacle-avoidance route with five checkpoints, where the user navigated around a central obstacle. A dataset of 36 walking trials and 42,474 time-series samples was used for evaluation, including IMU signals, magnetometer readings, barometric variables, turn-event labels, ground-truth trajectories, baseline PDR outputs, proposed ForestBack outputs, and power-related measurements. Experimental results show that ForestBack reduced the mean RMSE from 1.129 m to 0.965 m compared with traditional PDR, corresponding to a 15.76% improvement. The mean final-position error was reduced from 1.781 m to 1.388 m, while turn-event detection consistency reached approximately 99.90%. These results indicate that ForestBack improves trajectory reconstruction and route-preserving return guidance in obstacle-avoidance scenarios. The released dataset and analysis notebook support reproducibility and future benchmarking of infrastructure-free PDR-based return navigation systems.

</details>


### [142] [Kine2Go: Kinematic dataset for the Unitree Go2 robot with diverse gaits and motions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14433)
*Władysław Pałucki,Paweł Siwak,Krzysztof Ciebiera,Marek Cygan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了Kine2Go数据集，包含从40种不同策略中提取的800条Unitree Go2四足机器人步态运动学轨迹数据，并构建了将不同形态四足数据转换为Go2兼容格式的管线，通过强化学习训练策略并收集带扰动的鲁棒运动学数据及对应电机动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对使用Unitree Go2四足机器人进行模仿学习、强化学习等研究时，获取机器人运动学信息和电机动作的演示数据困难、需要构建复杂管线且耗时的问题。

Method: 首先构建数据管线，接受不同四足形态的数据并转换为Go2兼容格式；然后使用强化学习训练策略以跟随给定运动；最后从这些策略中收集数据，获得鲁棒的、带扰动的运动学数据及对应的电机级动作。

Result: 生成了包含800条多样化步态运动学轨迹的Kine2Go数据集，覆盖40种不同策略，数据包含运动学信息和电机动作。

Conclusion: Kine2Go数据集为使用Unitree Go2机器人进行机器学习研究的社区提供了高质量、可直接使用的演示数据，降低了数据获取门槛，有助于推进四足机器人相关研究。

Abstract: The recent popularity of robotics, combined with the steadily decreasing cost of robotic hardware, has lowered the entry barrier to robotics research and enabled rapid advancements in the field. One of the primary examples is the Unitree Go2 quadruped robot, which is often used by researchers in the areas of locomotion, navigation, control, and others. Many researchers use the Go2 robot in combination with techniques like imitation learning, reinforcement learning, and behavioral cloning to allow machine learning systems to take full control of the robot. At the same time, many of those techniques require demonstration data consisting of the robot's kinematics information and actions applied to the motors. Obtaining such data is difficult, requires building complex pipelines, and can take significant time. To aid in those kinds of efforts, we present Kine2Go - a dataset with 800 diverse gait kinematics trajectory motion data for the Unitree Go2 robot, derived from 40 distinct policies. Our pipeline accepts data from various quadruped morphologies and translates them to a Go2-compatible format. Then we use Reinforcement Learning to train policies following a given motion, and finally we gather data from those policies, which grants robust, perturbed kinematic data with corresponding motor-level actions.

</details>


### [143] [CADET: Physics-Grounded Causal Auditing and Training-Free Deconfounding of End-to-End Driving Planners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14438)
*Zikun Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CADET框架，无需训练即可审计、基准测试和修复预训练端到端自动驾驶规划器中的虚假因果依赖问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端自动驾驶规划器通过模仿学习训练时，容易产生统计捷径，即依赖与专家动作共现但非因果相关的场景元素（如路边物体、建筑立面）做出驾驶决策。这种因果混淆在长尾场景中暗中损害可靠性，且难以被现有开环指标（L2位移和碰撞率）检测。

Method: 提出CADET框架，这是一种无需训练的方法，无需任何参数更新即可审计、基准测试和修复预训练端到端规划器中的虚假依赖问题。

Result: CADET能够检测并修复预训练规划器对虚假线索的依赖，且无需重新训练模型。

Conclusion: CADET为已部署的端到端自动驾驶规划器提供了一种轻量级的因果混淆检测与修复方案，弥补了现有方法需要重新训练且无法审计已部署模型的缺陷。

Abstract: End-to-end (E2E) autonomous-driving planners trained by imitation are prone to statistical shortcuts: they associate scene elements that merely co-occur with expert actions (a roadside object, a building facade) with driving decisions, rather than the variables that causally determine them. Such causal confusion silently compromises reliability in long-tail scenarios, and it is difficult to detect, because prevailing open-loop metrics (L2 displacement and collision rate) are dominated by ego status and do not indicate whether a planner depends on spurious cues. Existing remedies based on causal-intervention training require retraining large models and cannot audit a planner that is already deployed. We present CADET, a training-free framework that audits, benchmarks, and repairs spurious reliance in pretrained E2E planners without any parameter update.

</details>


### [144] [AERMANI-PLACE: Language Guided Object Placement with Aerial Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14531)
*Sarthak Mishra,Ritama Sanyal,Rishabh Dev Yadav,Wei Pan,Spandan Roy*

Main category: cs.RO
