TL;DR: AERMANI-PLACE是一个基于语言引导的空中机械臂物体放置框架，通过图像编辑模型在场景中生成视觉标记，结合深度观测将标记映射到物理环境，从而从自然语言指令推断放置位置，在测试集上达到87%的成功率，在真实平台上达到72%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空中机械臂的物体放置系统需要用户以公制坐标显式指定放置位置，这种交互方式不直观，要求用户理解坐标系和场景几何，实际部署困难。受人类通过语言和手势传达空间目标的启发，作者希望构建一个更直观的语言引导放置框架。

Method: 输入场景图像和自然语言指令后，使用图像编辑模型生成带有视觉标记的修改场景图；然后利用深度观测将标记映射到物理环境，恢复公制放置点；最后生成并执行放置轨迹。

Result: 在100个语言引导放置任务的测试集上平均成功率为87%；在真实空中机械臂平台上平均成功率为72%。

Conclusion: 提出的方法能够可靠地从语言指令推断放置位置，并有效迁移到真实空中机械臂操作场景，为空中机械臂提供了一种更直观的人机交互方式。

Abstract: Object placement is a fundamental component of aerial manipulation tasks, yet existing systems typically require the desired placement position to be specified explicitly in metric coordinates. Such interfaces are not intuitive and require users to reason about coordinate frames and scene geometry, making them difficult to use in practical deployments. In contrast, humans often communicate spatial goals through a combination of language and pointing gestures. Inspired by this observation, we present AERMANI-PLACE, a framework for language-guided object placement with aerial manipulators. Given a scene image and a natural language instruction, an image editing model generates a modified version of the scene containing a visual marker that indicates where the object should be placed. This marker is then grounded into the physical environment using depth observations to recover a metric place point, after which a placement trajectory is generated and executed by the aerial manipulator. We evaluate the proposed approach on a test set of 100 language-guided placement tasks and demonstrate successful execution on a real aerial manipulation platform. Experimental results show that the proposed method reliably infers placement locations from language instructions with an average success rate of 87\% on the test-set and transfers effectively to real-world aerial manipulation with an average success rate of 72\%.
  Video: https://youtu.be/SgwwgLBsv0g

</details>


### [145] [Spatially Conditioned Diffusion Policy: Learning Precise and Robust Manipulation with a Single RGB Camera](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14535)
*Seoyoon Kim,Kanghyun Kim,Dongwoo Ko,Yeong Jin Heo,Min Jun Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出空间条件扩散策略(SCDP)，一种基于扩散的视觉运动策略，利用末端执行器轨迹作为视觉注意力锚点，在单摄像头设置下实现精确且鲁棒的模仿学习，性能媲美多摄像头基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉模仿学习系统普遍依赖多摄像头（尤其是腕部摄像头）设置，单全局视角下策略难以捕获细粒度交互细节并识别任务相关区域，因此需要一种能在单摄像头场景下实现精确操作的方法。

Method: SCDP包含两个关键组件：(i) 多尺度特征图视觉编码器，同时捕获全局上下文和细粒度视觉特征；(ii) 空间条件模块，在扩散循环中沿中间末端执行器轨迹采样逐点特征，利用轨迹作为视觉注意力锚点引导策略关注任务相关区域。

Result: 仿真实验表明SCDP一致优于强单视图基线，性能媲美多摄像头基线；真实实验进一步验证了其对视觉干扰物的鲁棒性和精确操作能力。

Conclusion: SCDP证明了单摄像头模仿学习的潜力，通过空间条件扩散机制有效解决了单全局视角下精细操作难题，为简化模仿学习系统的摄像头配置提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Recent visual imitation learning systems have widely adopted multi-camera setups with wrist-mounted cameras as the de facto standard. However, manipulation from a single global view remains challenging, as the policy should capture fine-grained interaction details and identify task-relevant regions without local wrist views. To address this challenge, we present Spatially Conditioned Diffusion Policy (SCDP), a diffusion-based visuomotor policy that achieves precise and robust manipulation in a single-camera setting. Our key idea is that end-effector trajectories can serve as visual attention anchors that reflect task-relevant regions. Building on this idea, SCDP consists of two key components: (i) a visual encoder that produces multi-scale feature maps to capture both broader context and fine-grained visual features, and (ii) a spatial conditioning module that samples point-wise features along intermediate end-effector trajectories in the diffusion loop. Extensive simulation experiments show that SCDP consistently outperforms strong single-view baselines and achieves performance comparable to multi-camera baselines. Real-world experiments further demonstrate precise manipulation and robustness to visual distractors, highlighting the potential of single-camera imitation learning.

</details>


### [146] [TRACE: Trajectory-Routed Causal Memory for Delayed-Evidence Visuomotor Imitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14551)
*Zihao Li,Ranpeng Qiu,Yincong Chen,Guoqiang Ren,Weiming Zhi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TRACE（轨迹路由因果证据）记忆框架，通过路径签名索引存储视觉和机器人状态证据，解决自主机器人操作中因早期线索消失导致的视觉模糊决策问题，在长时域操作任务中优于短历史记忆和循环记忆基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自主机器人操作中，早期视觉线索（如物体身份、目标选择）在后续决策点消失，导致当前观测不足以作为控制状态，而现有方法缺乏高效、有界的证据记忆机制。

Method: TRACE框架利用路径签名（path signatures）作为轨迹条件键，将任务相关视觉与状态证据写入固定大小的潜在记忆矩阵；在歧义观测点通过路径签名检索对应记忆，作为策略的条件输入。框架通过轻量适配器附着于现有策略，不修改策略主干或目标函数。

Result: 在真实世界长时域操作任务中，面对视觉歧义分支点，TRACE在分支选择和任务成功率上均优于短历史记忆和循环记忆等基线方法。

Conclusion: TRACE通过轨迹路由的因果证据存储与检索机制，有效解决了延时证据任务中的状态不充分问题，为长时域视觉运动策略提供了一种轻量、有界的记忆增强方案。

Abstract: Robots under autonomous operation may require decisions based on evidence that is no longer visible. We study \emph{delayed-evidence} tasks, where an early cue disappears before a later decision point, so visually similar observations can require different actions. In these settings, the current observation is not a sufficient state for control. We introduce TRAjectory-routed Causal Evidence (TRACE), a memory framework for visuomotor imitation policies. TRACE stores task-relevant visual and robot-state evidence, such as object identity, target choice, or route-dependent state, in a fixed-size latent memory that remains bounded over long episodes. Instead of indexing memory by raw time or manually provided task labels, TRACE uses \emph{path signatures}: compact, order-sensitive features of the executed robot-state trajectory. These signatures do not store the visual cue itself; rather, they provide trajectory-conditioned keys for writing and retrieving the evidence stored when the cue was visible. When the robot later reaches an ambiguous observation, the policy conditions on TRACE memory to recover the missing context and choose the correct branch. TRACE attaches through lightweight adapters to policies, without changing the policy backbone, action head, or imitation objective. Across real-world long-horizon manipulation tasks with visually ambiguous branch points, TRACE improves branch selection and task success over alternative baselines, including short-history and recurrent memory. Project page: https://jeong-zju.github.io/trace

</details>


### [147] [ORCA: A Platform for Open-Source Dexterity Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14561)
*Francesco Capuano,Maximilian Eberlein,Fabrice Bourquin,Clemens Claudio Christoph*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍ORCA学习栈，一个开源的研究框架，将灵巧手（尤其是拟人手）的低级控制、仿真、遥操作和重定向统一到单一接口，并原生集成流行的机器人学习框架（如LeRobot），为灵巧操作研究提供可复现的基础设施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作研究多聚焦于二指平行夹爪，虽有效但受限于形态，往往需要双臂设置才能完成简单任务；拟人手更接近人手，可从人类视频学习，但因软件分散、与机器人学习生态脱节而难以在研究中使用。

Method: 构建ORCA学习栈，统一低级控制、仿真、多平台遥操作（如消费级VR头显）和手部重定向，原生集成LeRobot等流行框架，提供端到端工作流。

Result: 通过消费级VR头显遥操作采集手内重定向任务的专家演示，使用LeRobot训练自主策略，在完全可复现和可观测的设置中评估所学策略，验证了完整工作流。

Conclusion: ORCA作为共享、可复现的基础设施，将灵巧操作提升为机器人学习的一等研究领域，使灵巧手研究者能利用与非灵巧机器人学习相同的数据、训练和评估流程。

Abstract: Robotics manipulation research increasingly focuses on two-finger parallel grippers for their effectiveness, affordability, and ease of teleoperation. Grippers are nonetheless limited by their form factor, often requiring bimanual setups even for simple reorientation tasks. Anthropomorphic hands are a more natural platform for dexterous robot learning -- closer to the human hand, and capable of learning from human video -- yet they remain hard to use in learning research: even where open and accessible hand hardware exists, the software for control, simulation, teleoperation, and retargeting is scattered in one-off code bases, and largely disconnected from the robot-learning ecosystem. In this work, we introduce the \orca~learning stack, an open-source research stack for dexterity as a first-class robot learning domain. Our \orca~stack unifies low-level control, simulation, teleoperation from a range of consumer platforms, and hand retargeting, behind a single interface, and integrates natively with popular robot-learning frameworks such as \lerobot, so dexterous hand researchers can leverage the same data, training, and evaluation pipelines used for non-dexterous robot learning. We demonstrate a complete end-to-end workflow, collecting expert demonstrations of an in-hand reorientation task by teleoperation with a consumer-grade VR headset, training an autonomous policy with \lerobot, and evaluating the learned policy in a fully reproducible and observable setup. We open-source the entire stack as a shared, reproducible foundation for dexterous-manipulation research.

</details>


### [148] [Sensitivity Shaping for Latent Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14585)
*Hongzhan Yu,Chenghao Li,Ruipeng Zhang,Henrik Christensen,Sicun Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出支持条件控制灵敏度正则化方法，通过在训练高支持区域增强动力学模型对控制输入的局部敏感性，解决现有OOD检测方法在动力学模型对关键动作选择局部不敏感时失效的问题，实验证明该方法在视觉避障、操作和真实机器人导航中提升了OOD检测性能和闭环规划安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OOD检测方法将学习到的动力学模型视为固定的并附加后验支持代理，但当动力学模型对关键动作选择局部不敏感时，这些代理会失效——不支持的控制动作可能产生与演示转换相似的潜在预测，从而抑制OOD信号，导致较大的真实预测误差被忽略。

Method: 提出支持条件控制灵敏度正则化（support-conditioned control-sensitivity regularization），在训练高支持区域促进学习到的动力学模型对控制输入变化的局部敏感响应，保留控制引起的变异性，同时限制因弱经验支持导致的不稳定外推。

Result: 在视觉避障、操作任务和真实机器人导航实验中，该方法提升了OOD检测性能，并实现了更安全的闭环规划。

Conclusion: 通过增强动力学模型在训练高支持区域对控制输入的局部敏感性，可以有效解决现有OOD检测方法的失效问题，提升机器人系统安全部署能力。

Abstract: Generative dynamics models enable planning in challenging robotic systems, but safe deployment requires reliably detecting policy-induced out-of-distribution (OOD) transitions. Existing methods typically treat the learned dynamics as fixed and attach post hoc support surrogates. We show that these surrogates can fail when the dynamics are locally insensitive to critical action choices: unsupported control actions may produce latent predictions that resemble demonstrated transitions, suppressing OOD signals despite large true predictive errors. To address this, we introduce support-conditioned control-sensitivity regularization, which promotes sensitive local response to control input changes in learned dynamics in high-support training regions. This preserves control-induced variation while limiting unstable extrapolation due to weak empirical support. Experiments in vision-based obstacle avoidance, manipulation, and real-robot navigation show improved OOD detection and safer closed-loop planning.

</details>


### [149] [What Robots Do Matters More Than What They Look Like: Task Context Shapes Trust in Educational HRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14602)
*Anna-Maria Velentza,Konstantina Nikou,Anne-Gwenn Bosser,Nikolaos Fachantidis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 通过视频实验(N=81)发现，在社交辅助机器人(SARs)的教育应用场景中，任务类型（教学、程序指导、个人信息询问）对信任度有显著主效应，而机器人外观无显著主效应，二者交互效应边缘显著。信任更多由任务情境而非物理外观塑造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管社交辅助机器人在教育和信息分享场景中应用日益广泛，但单一机器人外观是否适用于不同交互任务、信任是否主要取决于情境因素，尚不明确。

Method: 采用被试内视频实验(N=81)，让参与者评估三种不同外观的机器人在三种教育相关任务（教学、程序指导、个人信息讨论）中的表现，通过重复测量分析信任评分。

Result: 任务对信任有强主效应：程序指导时信任最高，教学时中等，个人信息询问时显著最低；机器人外观无显著主效应；外观与任务的交互效应仅边缘显著。

Conclusion: 人机交互中的信任更多由任务情境而非物理外观塑造；教育场景中部署机器人时应根据任务目标对齐角色和行为，而非单纯依赖拟人化设计。

Abstract: Socially assistive robots (SARs) are increasingly deployed in educational and information-sharing contexts, supported by advances in large language models that enable fluent real-time interaction. Despite the growing diversity of robot embodiments, it remains unclear whether a single robot appearance is appropriate across different interaction tasks or whether trust depends primarily on contextual factors. In this study, we examine how robot appearance and task type jointly influence trust in robots. Using a within-subjects video-based experiment (N = 81), participants evaluated three robots with distinct appearances while performing three educationally relevant tasks: teaching, procedural instruction, and personal-information discussion. Results from repeated-measures analyses show a strong main effect of task on trust, with participants reporting the highest trust during instructional guidance, moderate trust during teaching activities, and significantly lower trust when robots requested personal information. In contrast, robot appearance showed no significant main effect, and the interaction between appearance and task was marginal. These findings suggest that trust in human-robot interaction is shaped more strongly by task context than by physical embodiment alone. By focusing on future educators as end users, this work contributes empirical evidence toward task-aware robot deployment in educational environments and highlights the importance of aligning robot roles and behaviors with interaction goals rather than relying solely on anthropomorphic design.

</details>


### [150] [Impedance MPC with Disturbance Estimation for Dexterous Hand Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14606)
*Yongyan Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种阻抗模型预测控制(Impedance MPC)框架，用于灵巧手的手指控制，在保持稳定性的同时实现了高精度轨迹跟踪与安全柔顺接触的平衡，在液压手指上达到0.5 mrad RMS精度，性能优于经典阻抗控制183倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧手需要同时满足精确的指尖轨迹跟踪与安全柔顺接触这两个相互矛盾的目标，而固定增益控制器无法兼顾两者。现有方法在轨迹精度与接触柔顺性之间存在张力，需要一种能够自适应调节阻抗的控制框架。

Method: 基于常数A_d偏移自由架构的阻抗MPC框架，通过代数前馈将肌腱传动（液压、缆线、气动、扭绳、串联弹性）简化为常系数双积分器，使得QP代价矩阵逆可离线预计算；采用仅编码器的增广卡尔曼扰动状态估计器，在任意恒定接触负载下实现零稳态误差；在10步滚动时域QP中强制执行接触力(ISO/TS 15066)、驱动限制和加加速度的硬约束。

Result: 在液压驱动手指上，500 Hz Kalman MPC实现了0.5 mrad RMS、0.1 mrad稳态误差、6.6 mrad峰值偏差，分别比经典阻抗控制好183倍、1500倍和23倍；首次移动刚度从18到323 Nm/rad可调；在16-DOF LEAP Hand MuJoCo仿真中，2.5 N抓取负载扰动在0.7秒内恢复。

Conclusion: 该阻抗MPC框架继承了pHRI中偏移自由架构的稳定性、递归可行性和输入-状态稳定性保证，通过代数前馈将复杂传动系统统一为双积分器模型，实现了高更新率(500 Hz)下的实时优化控制，兼具高精度轨迹跟踪与可控柔顺性，且可扩展到多自由度灵巧手系统。

Abstract: Dexterous hands must simultaneously track precise finger trajectories and maintain safe, compliant contact -- objectives in tension for any fixed-gain controller. We present an actuator-agnostic Impedance Model Predictive Control (Impedance MPC) framework for dexterous fingers, instantiating the constant-$A_d$ offset-free architecture established for physical human-robot interaction (pHRI); its stability, recursive-feasibility, and input-to-state-stability guarantees are inherited by preserving the architectural assumptions. An algebraic feedforward reduces the tendon transmission -- hydraulic, cable, pneumatic, twisted-string, or series-elastic -- to a constant-coefficient double integrator, so the QP cost inverse is precomputed offline and a 10-step receding-horizon quadratic program runs at 500\,Hz while enforcing hard constraints on contact force (ISO/TS 15066), actuation limits, and jerk. An encoder-only augmented-Kalman disturbance state drives steady-state error to zero under any constant contact load. On a hydraulically actuated finger -- the worked example platform, adding pressure and cavitation constraints -- the 500\,Hz Kalman MPC attains 0.5\,mrad RMS, 0.1\,mrad steady-state, and 6.6\,mrad peak deflection under 1.5\,Nm contact: 183$\times$, 1500$\times$, and 23$\times$ better than classical impedance. The realized first-move stiffness (18$\to$323\,Nm/rad with update rate) is independently verified. The architecture scales to a 16-DOF LEAP Hand MuJoCo simulation, recovering from 2.5\,N grasp-load disturbances within 0.7\,s.

</details>


### [151] [Safe Reinforcement Learning of Autonomous Highway Driving: A Unified Framework for Safety and Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14609)
*Chufei Yan,Zhihao Cui,Yiyan Lv,Taojie Chen,Ning Bian,Yulei Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种整合安全距离(SD)、奖励机(RM)和混合专家(MoE)的统一安全强化学习框架MoE-RM-SRL，用于自动驾驶车辆的决策制定，在训练和部署阶段同时提升安全性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习在自动驾驶决策中具有潜力，但其试错式训练难以保障安全，且部署时安全与效率难以兼顾。

Method: SD和RM联合构建规则感知奖励以编码交通法规和阶段目标；引入稀疏门控MoE层（最多11个DQN专家），基于SD的门控规则激活最小专家集用于车道保持和变道，避免异构控制器切换带来的不稳定性。

Result: 在CARLA和6自由度驾驶员在环虚拟现实平台上的随机双车道交通实验中，MoE-RM-SRL在安全性和效率上显著优于最先进基线，且可自然扩展至多车道驾驶、匝道合流和驶出场景。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效解决了DRL在自动驾驶中安全与效率的权衡问题，具有良好可扩展性。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning (DRL) offers a compelling route to decision-making for advanced autonomous vehicles (AVs), yet its trial-and-error nature makes it difficult to guarantee safety during training and to achieve both safety and efficiency at deployment. We propose a unified safe reinforcement learning (SRL) framework that integrates safe distance (SD), reward machines (RM), and mixture-of-experts (MoE), termed MoE-RM-SRL. For deployment, SD and RM jointly shape a rule-aware reward that encodes highway traffic regulations and stage-wise objectives, enabling safe and reliable behavior without sacrificing efficiency. For training, we introduce a sparsely gated MoE layer comprising up to 11 deep Q-networks (DQNs); an SD-based gating rule activates a minimal set of experts for lane-keeping and lane-changing, mitigating the instability, discontinuities, and impulsive transients commonly induced by switching between heterogeneous controllers (e.g., MPC/rule-based modules and learned policies). We implement the proposed architecture in CARLA and integrate it with a 6-DoF driver-in-the-loop virtual-reality (DiL-VR) platform. Experiments in stochastic two-lane traffic show that MoE-RM-SRL substantially improves safety and efficiency over state-of-the-art baselines, and the framework naturally extends to multi-lane driving as well as on-ramp merging and exiting scenarios.

</details>


### [152] [Whole-Body Impedance Model Predictive Control for Safe Physical Human--Robot Interaction on Floating-Base Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14617)
*Yongyan Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种三层次架构的阻抗MPC框架，用于浮动基座机器人在刚性接触约束下与人交互时的平衡控制。该框架通过质心MPC规划接触力、优先级驱动的WBC层将平衡分解为关节力矩、再通过卡尔曼增强的滚动时域QP预测并抑制人机交互扰动。接触一致的反馈线性化将臂端执行器简化为双积分器，支持离线预计算和1kHz以上运行。协方差膨胀协议保证在接触模式切换时扰动估计的连续性，实现零稳态误差。阻抗等价定理表明无限时域极限可恢复经典任务空间阻抗律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有浮动基座机器人全身控制框架要么将全关节空间分配给 locomotion，要么依赖固定增益阻抗反馈，在持续的人机物理交互力下会产生稳态误差。需要一种能够同时满足平衡约束和人机交互安全性的控制方法。

Method: 三层次架构：(1) 质心MPC在500ms时域内规划接触力；(2) 优先级驱动的WBC通过接触一致零空间投影将平衡解析为关节力矩；(3) 残差零空间由滚动时域QP管理，使用卡尔曼增强状态预测和抑制pHRI扰动。接触一致反馈线性化将臂端简化为常状态矩阵双积分器，支持离线QP代价预计算和1kHz以上运行。协方差膨胀协议保证接触模式切换时扰动估计不中断。

Result: 在17-DOF双足机器人和Unitree G1人形机器人上的仿真验证了该设计的有效性。实现零稳态误差、1kHz以上运行频率、阻抗参数可随姿态和接触配置自适应调整。

Conclusion: 提出的三层次阻抗MPC框架能有效解决浮动基座机器人在人机交互下的平衡控制问题，实现了零稳态误差和自适应阻抗特性，且计算效率高，具有实用价值。

Abstract: Floating-base robots must balance under rigid contact constraints while interacting safely with humans. Existing whole-body control~(WBC) frameworks allocate the full joint space to locomotion or rely on fixed-gain impedance feedback that accumulates steady-state error under sustained physical human--robot interaction~(pHRI) forces. This paper extends the authors' fixed-base two-layer Impedance MPC to floating-base platforms through a three-level architecture: a centroidal MPC plans contact forces over a 500\,ms horizon; a priority-driven WBC layer resolves balance into joint torques through contact-consistent null-space projection; and the residual null space is governed by a receding-horizon quadratic program~(QP) that predicts and rejects pHRI disturbances using a Kalman-augmented state. A contact-consistent feedback linearization reduces the arm end-effector plant to a double integrator with a \emph{constant} state matrix within each contact mode, enabling offline precomputation of the QP cost and ${\geq}1$\,kHz operation. A covariance-inflation protocol preserves the disturbance estimate across contact-mode switches, guaranteeing zero steady-state error under bounded constant pHRI loads, and an Impedance Equivalence Theorem shows the infinite-horizon limit recovers a classical task-space impedance law whose effective mass, damping, and stiffness adapt to posture and contact configuration. Simulations on a 17-DOF biped and the Unitree G1 humanoid validate the design.

</details>


### [153] [EgoGuide: Egocentric Guidance for Efficient Robot-Free Demonstration Collection and Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14665)
*Yue Xu,Mingtao Nie,Tianle Li,Hong Li,Yibo Luo,Siyuan Huang,Yong-Lu Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EgoGuide提出了一种结合腕部与头部/自我中心视角同步记录的数据收集接口，并引入在线视觉-几何数据质量指导，同时设计门控自我中心残差策略以提升机器人学习的数据效率和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的UMI-style数据收集管线存在两个问题：一是收集的演示数据冗余，二是缺乏全局场景上下文。这些限制导致数据效率低下，亟需更高效的数据收集与利用方法。

Method: 1) 设计EgoGuide数据收集接口，同步记录腕部与头部/自我中心视角的观测数据，并耦合在线视觉-几何数据质量指导；2) 提出Gated Egocentric Residual Policy（门控自我中心残差策略），利用头部/自我中心上下文纠正模糊的局部观测，同时保留稳定的腕部视角控制。

Result: 真实世界实验表明，EgoGuide减少了所需的数据集轮次，提升了数据效率；残差策略在视觉遮挡情况下进一步提升了鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过引入自我中心视角的全局上下文感知和在线质量指导，EgoGuide有效缓解了数据冗余问题，提升了机器人从人类演示中学习的数据效率和对遮挡的鲁棒性，为灵巧操作的数据收集提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Robot learning from real-world demonstrations is currently constrained by data scaling. Universal Manipulation Interface (UMI) provides an efficient robot-free data collection interface, yet current UMI-style pipelines often collect redundant demonstrations and lack global scene context. To improve data efficiency, we present EgoGuide, a collection interface that records synchronized wrist and head/egocentric observations and couples them with online visual-geometric data quality guidance. We also introduce a Gated Egocentric Residual Policy for robust learning from a viewpoint-varying egocentric camera, allowing head/egocentric context to correct ambiguous local observations while preserving stable wrist-view control. Real-world experiments show that EgoGuide reduces the required number of data episodes and improves data efficiency. The residual policy further improves robustness under visual occlusion. Project Page: https://silicx.github.io/EgoGuide

</details>


### [154] [Cross-Stage Sensorimotor Perception Scheduling and Sparse Map Encoding for Efficient Edge Embodied Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.14154)
*Yaotian Liu,Sri Sai Rakesh Nakkilla,Xiangyu Zhou,Yu Cao,Jeff Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对边缘端具身智能体在ObjectNav任务中的部署问题，提出了SKIP（自适应传感器调度器）和SCOUT（稀疏上下文编码器）两个优化方案，在HM3D数据集上实现了1.7倍加速、50.5%峰值内存降低和7.1% SPL提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘端具身智能体在紧延迟、内存和能量预算下执行感知-行动闭环，部署本质是系统级协同设计问题而非单纯模型精度问题。现有方法缺乏对资源约束下模块化ObjectNav流水线的系统性优化。

Method: 提出两个正交优化旋钮：1) SKIP：自适应传感器调度器，将安全跳过形式化为有界地图影响准则，并学习轻量级预测器从廉价传感器线索估计该准则；2) SCOUT：稀疏上下文编码器，将活动地图区域的子流形稀疏卷积与轻量密集上下文流耦合。

Result: 在HM3D数据集服务器和嵌入式平台上，SKIP+SCOUT相比密集基线在选定操作点实现1.7倍端到端加速、50.5%峰值内存降低和7.1% SPL提升，且SKIP可迁移至PONI流水线并保持近无损性能，对深度传感器噪声具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SKIP+SCOUT为边缘物理AI系统提供了一系列设备感知的Pareto最优操作点，展示了将部署视为系统级协同设计问题而非单纯模型精度问题的有效性。

Abstract: Embodied agents must close a perception-to-action loop on embedded hardware under tight latency, memory, and energy budgets, making deployment a system-level co-design problem rather than a model-accuracy problem. We study this challenge for modular Object Goal Navigation (ObjectNav), where our profiling shows semantic mapping dominates per-step latency while goal prediction dominates peak memory. We formulate edge embodied navigation deployment as a budget-constrained design-space problem and introduce two orthogonal optimization knobs: SKIP, an adaptive sensorimotor scheduler that formalizes safe skipping as a bounded map-impact criterion and learns a lightweight predictor to estimate it from cheap sensor cues at each \texttt{FORWARD} step, exposing a principled quality-efficiency knob (depth-based updates are always retained); and SCOUT, a sparse-context encoder that couples submanifold sparse convolutions on active map regions with a lightweight dense context stream. On HM3D across server and embedded platforms, SKIP+SCOUT delivers up to 1.7x end-to-end speedup, 50.5% lower peak memory, and 7.1% higher SPL than the dense baseline at the selected operating point, outperforming naively smaller perception backbones. SKIP transfers to a second modular pipeline (PONI) with near-lossless performance and remains robust under depth-sensor noise. Together, SKIP+SCOUT expose a family of device-aware Pareto operating points for edge physical AI systems.

</details>


### [155] [ADAPT: An Autonomous Forklift for Construction Site Operation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.14331)
*Johannes Huemer,Markus Murschitz,Matthias Schörghuber,Lukas Reisinger,Thomas Kadiofsky,Christoph Weidinger,Mario Niedermeyer,Benedikt Widy,Marcel Zeilinger,Csaba Beleznai,Tobias Glück,Andreas Kugi,Patrik Zips*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了ADAPT（自主动态全地形托盘运输车），一种专为建筑环境设计的全自动越野叉车。该系统集成了AI驱动的感知技术与传统决策、规划和控制方法，能够在动态障碍物、非结构化地形和多变天气条件下可靠运行。通过广泛的实地测试，对比了系统与经验丰富的人类操作员在不同天气条件下的持续性能，结果表明自主室外叉车能够达到接近人类水平的性能，为更安全、更高效的建筑物流提供了可行路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑行业中手动物料处理存在效率低下、延误和安全风险等问题，同时面临劳动力短缺的挑战。尽管自主叉车在结构化仓库环境中已有应用，但建筑工地复杂多变的环境（动态障碍物、非结构化地形、多变天气）使得现有解决方案难以直接迁移。

Method: 提出ADAPT系统，将AI驱动的感知技术与传统的决策、规划和控制方法相结合。在真实建筑工地上进行广泛测试，并与经验丰富的人类操作员在不同天气条件下的持续性能进行对比。

Result: 自主户外叉车能够达到接近人类操作员的性能水平，验证了系统在复杂建筑环境中的可靠性和有效性。

Conclusion: 自主户外叉车为实现更安全、更高效的建筑物流提供了可行路径，能够有效缓解劳动力短缺问题，并降低人为操作带来的安全风险。

Abstract: Efficient material logistics play a critical role in controlling costs and schedules in the construction industry. However, manual material handling remains prone to inefficiencies, delays, and safety risks. Autonomous forklifts offer a promising solution to streamline on-site logistics, reducing reliance on human operators and mitigating labor shortages. This paper presents the development and evaluation of ADAPT (Autonomous Dynamic All-terrain Pallet Transporter), a fully autonomous off-road forklift designed for construction environments. Unlike structured warehouse settings, construction sites pose significant challenges, including dynamic obstacles, unstructured terrain, and varying weather conditions. To address these challenges, our system integrates AI-driven perception techniques with traditional approaches for decision making, planning, and control, enabling reliable operation in complex environments. We validate the system through extensive real-world testing, comparing its continuous performance against an experienced human operator across various weather conditions. Our findings demonstrate that autonomous outdoor forklifts can operate near human-level performance, offering a viable path toward safer and more efficient construction logistics.

</details>


### [156] [Schrödinger's Navigator: Imagining an Ensemble of Futures for Zero-Shot Object Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.21201)
*Yu He,Da Huang,Zhenyang Liu,Zixiao Gu,Qiang Sun,Guangnan Ye,Yanwei Fu,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Schrödinger's Navigator框架，通过推理时想象多条轨迹条件下的3D未来场景，实现零样本目标导航，在仿真和真实Go2四足机器人上优于强基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零样本目标导航（ZSON）在仿真中表现良好的方法在真实杂乱场景中因严重遮挡和潜在危险而性能下降，大范围未观测区域导致单场景推理脆弱且不安全。

Method: 提出信念感知框架，包含三个组件：1）轨迹条件3D世界模型预测假设观测并维持多种场景叠加态；2）自适应遮挡感知采样器聚焦不确定性关键区域；3）未来感知价值图（FAVM）聚合想象未来以进行鲁棒主动动作选择。

Result: 在仿真和真实Go2四足机器人上，Schrödinger's Navigator优于强ZSON基线，在遮挡密集的导航场景中提升了隐藏目标发现率和风险感知航点选择能力。

Conclusion: 想象3D未来是一种可扩展且可泛化的策略，适用于不确定真实环境中的零样本导航。

Abstract: Zero-shot object navigation (ZSON) requires robots to find target objects in unseen environments without task-specific fine-tuning or pre-built maps, a key capability for general-purpose service robots. Yet methods that perform well in simulation often degrade in cluttered real-world scenes with severe occlusion and latent hazards, where large unseen regions make single-scene inference brittle and unsafe. We propose Schrödinger's Navigator, a belief-aware framework that reasons at inference time over multiple trajectory-conditioned imagined 3D futures. Given candidate paths, a trajectory-conditioned 3D world model predicts hypothetical observations and maintains a superposition of plausible scene realizations rather than committing to one map. An adaptive occluder-aware sampler directs imagination to uncertainty-critical regions, while a Future-Aware Value Map (FAVM) aggregates imagined futures for robust, proactive action selection. Experiments in simulation and on a physical Go2 quadruped show that Schrödinger's Navigator outperforms strong ZSON baselines, improving hidden-target discovery and risk-aware waypoint selection in occlusion-heavy navigation scenarios. These results highlight imagined 3D futures as a scalable and generalizable strategy for zero-shot navigation in uncertain real-world environments.

</details>


### [157] [Asymmetric Friction in Geometric Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.22484)
*Ross L. Hatton,Yousef Salaman,Shai Revzen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文将阻力从各向异性扩展到非对称（前后方向阻力不同），用Finsler度量替代Riemannian度量来描述身体部件的运动，并展示了亚Riemannian方法可自然推广到亚Finsler方法，用于构建系统的运动能力图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有几何力学模型假设阻力是各向异性的（前后/左右系数不同），但未考虑前后方向非对称阻力的情况。作者希望将理论推广到更一般的非对称阻力场景。

Method: 将各向异性且非对称的阻力建模为Finsler度量（替代Riemannian度量），并引入亚Finsler约束来构建系统的运动能力图，同时分析约束曲率等性质。

Result: 亚Riemannian方法可以自然推广到亚Finsler方法，非对称阻力系统具有与亚Riemannian系统约束曲率类似的性质，可用于刻画系统运动能力。

Conclusion: 该工作扩展了几何力学模型的适用范围，使非对称阻力系统也能通过几何方法分析和表征。

Abstract: Geometric mechanics models of locomotion have provided insight into how robots and animals use environmental interactions to convert internal shape changes into displacement through the world, encoding this relationship in a ``motility map''. A key class of such motility maps arises from (possibly anisotropic) linear drag acting on the system's individual body parts, formally described via Riemannian metrics on the motions of the system's individual body parts. The motility map can then be generated by invoking a sub-Riemannian constraint on the aggregate system motion under which the position velocity induced by a given shape velocity is that which minimizes the power dissipated via friction. The locomotion of such systems is ``geometric'' in the sense that the final position reached by the system depends only on the sequence of shapes that the system passes through, but not on the rate with which the shape changes are made.
  In this paper, we consider a far more general class of systems in which the drag may be not only anisotropic (with different coefficients for forward/backward and left/right motions), but also asymmetric (with different coefficients for forward and backward motions). Formally, including asymmetry in the friction replaces the Riemannian metrics on the body parts with Finsler metrics. We demonstrate that the sub-Riemannian approach to constructing the system motility map extends naturally to a sub-Finslerian approach and identify system properties analogous to the constraint curvature of sub-Riemannian systems that allow for the characterization of the system motion capabilities.

</details>


### [158] [A Pragmatic VLA Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.18692)
*Wei Wu,Fan Lu,Yunnan Wang,Shuai Yang,Shi Liu,Fangjing Wang,Qian Zhu,He Sun,Yong Wang,Shuailei Ma,Yiyu Ren,Kejia Zhang,Hui Yu,Jingmei Zhao,Shuai Zhou,Zhenqi Qiu,Houlong Xiong,Ziyu Wang,Zechen Wang,Ran Cheng,Yong-Lu Li,Yongtao Huang,Xing Zhu,Yujun Shen,Kecheng Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LingBot-VLA是一个基于约20,000小时真实数据、涵盖9种双臂机器人配置的视觉-语言-动作基础模型，在3个平台、每平台100个任务的评估中展现卓越性能和泛化能力，配合高效代码库（8GPU下261样本/秒，快1.5~2.8倍），并开源代码、模型和基准数据以推动机器人学习领域发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA基础模型在跨任务、跨平台的泛化能力以及适配成本（数据量和GPU训练时间）方面仍存在不足，亟需一种兼顾高性能、强泛化能力和成本效益的统一解决方案。

Method: 收集约20,000小时来自9种主流双臂机器人构型的真实世界数据，训练LingBot-VLA模型；构建高效代码库，优化训练吞吐量（8GPU达261样本/秒，相比现有VLA代码库提速1.5~2.8倍）。

Result: 在3个机器人平台上各完成100个任务（每任务130次后训练回合），LingBot-VLA在性能与泛化能力上显著优于竞品；代码库吞吐量达261样本/秒（8GPU），相对现有VLA代码库实现1.5~2.8倍加速。

Conclusion: LingBot-VLA实现了跨平台、跨任务的强泛化能力和高效训练/推理，适合真实世界部署；通过开源代码、模型和基准数据，推动更具挑战性的机器人任务和标准化评估。

Abstract: Offering great potential in robotic manipulation, a capable Vision-Language-Action (VLA) foundation model is expected to faithfully generalize across tasks and platforms while ensuring cost efficiency (e.g., data and GPU hours required for adaptation). To this end, we develop LingBot-VLA with around 20,000 hours of real-world data from 9 popular dual-arm robot configurations. Through a systematic assessment on 3 robotic platforms, each completing 100 tasks with 130 post-training episodes per task, our model achieves clear superiority over competitors, showcasing its strong performance and broad generalizability. We have also built an efficient codebase, which delivers a throughput of 261 samples per second with an 8-GPU training setup, representing a 1.5~2.8$\times$ (depending on the relied VLM base model) speedup over existing VLA-oriented codebases. The above features ensure that our model is well-suited for real-world deployment. To advance the field of robot learning, we provide open access to the code, base model, and benchmark data, with a focus on enabling more challenging tasks and promoting sound evaluation standards.

</details>


### [159] [A Unified Control Architecture for Macro-Micro Manipulation using a Active Remote Center of Compliance for Manufacturing Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.01948)
*Patrick Frank,Christian Friedrich*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型控制架构，将宏机械臂纳入主动交互控制中，相比传统方案将控制带宽提升了2.1倍（对比leader-follower方法）和12.5倍（对比传统基于机器人的力控制），并提出了用于高效控制器设计和硬件适配的替代模型，通过碰撞、力轨迹跟踪和工业装配任务验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统宏-微机械臂系统中，宏机械臂仅负责位置控制，微机械臂处理与环境交互，这限制了交互控制带宽。为解决该问题，本文提出将宏机械臂纳入主动交互控制的新架构。

Method: 提出新型控制架构，将宏机械臂集成到主动交互控制回路中；并构建替代模型以实现更高效的控制器设计和硬件变更的快速适配。

Result: 控制带宽相比state-of-the-art的leader-follower方法提升2.1倍，相比传统基于机器人的力控制提升12.5倍；通过碰撞实验、力轨迹跟踪和工业装配任务验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 所提架构显著提升了宏-微机械臂系统的交互控制带宽，且替代模型有助于简化控制器设计和适应硬件变化。

Abstract: Macro-micro manipulators combine a macro manipulator with a large workspace, such as an industrial robot, with a lightweight, high-bandwidth micro manipulator. This enables highly dynamic interaction control while preserving the wide workspace of the robot. Traditionally, position control is assigned to the macro manipulator, while the micro manipulator handles the interaction with the environment, limiting the achievable interaction control bandwidth. To solve this, we propose a novel control architecture that incorporates the macro manipulator into the active interaction control. This leads to a increase in control bandwidth by a factor of 2.1 compared to the state of the art architecture, based on the leader-follower approach and factor 12.5 compared to traditional robot-based force control. Further we propose surrogate models for a more efficient controller design and easy adaptation to hardware changes. We validate our approach by comparing it against the other control schemes in different experiments, like collision with an object, following a force trajectory and industrial assembly tasks.

</details>


### [160] [Estimation of Ground Reaction Forces from Kinematic Data during Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.03177)
*Gautami Golani,Dong Anh Khoa To,Ananda Sidarta,Arun-Kumar Kaliya-Perumal,Oliver Roberts,Lek Syn Lim,Jim Patton,Domenico Campolo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需测力台、仅基于标记点运动捕捉数据即可估算地面反作用力（GRF）的方法，通过16个身体节段的运动学估算质心（CoM）并采用最小化方法分解GRF，实验验证了该运动学驱动方法的可行性，为临床步态分析提供了无测力台的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地面反作用力（GRF）对步态力学分析至关重要，但因测力台系统的实际限制（成本、空间、操作复杂性），GRF在临床工作流程中并未得到充分利用。

Method: 基于16个身体节段的标记点运动捕捉数据估算质心（CoM），进而计算GRF，再通过最小化方法将GRF分解为各分量，实现步态站立相识别及临床相关动力学指标获取。

Result: 实验结果表明，仅基于运动学数据即可有效估算质心和地面反作用力，验证了无测力台步态分析的可行性。

Conclusion: 该运动学驱动方法有望克服测力台的实际限制，推动GRF在临床步态评估中的广泛应用。

Abstract: Ground reaction forces (GRFs) provide fundamental insight into human gait mechanics and are widely used to assess joint loading, limb symmetry, balance control, and motor function. Despite their clinical relevance, the use of GRF remains underutilised in clinical workflows due to the practical limitations of force plate systems. In this work, we present a force-plate-free approach for estimating GRFs using only marker-based motion capture data. This kinematics only method to estimate and decompose GRF makes it well suited for widespread clinical depolyment. By using kinematics from sixteen body segments, we estimate the centre of mass (CoM) and compute GRFs, which are subsequently decomposed into individual components through a minimization-based approach. Through this framework, we can identify gait stance phases and provide access to clinically meaningful kinetic measures without a dedicated force plate system. Experimental results demonstrate the viability of CoM and GRF estimation based solely on kinematic data, supporting force-plate-free gait analysis.

</details>


### [161] [X-Loco: Towards Generalist Humanoid Locomotion Control via Synergetic Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03733)
*Dewei Wang,Xinmiao Wang,Chenyun Zhang,Jiyuan Shi,Yingnan Zhao,Chenjia Bai,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: X-Loco是一个基于视觉的通用人形机器人运动策略框架，通过多专家策略协同蒸馏和自适应专家选择机制，首次实现了直立行走、全身协调和跌倒恢复的联合控制，仅依赖速度指令即可运行，无需参考运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人形机器人研究在单一技能（如直立行走、跌倒恢复、全身协调）上表现不错，但学习一个统一策略来掌握所有技能仍然困难，因为不同技能涉及不同的动力学特性和相互冲突的控制目标。

Method: 训练多个专家策略（oracle specialists），然后采用协同策略蒸馏（synergetic policy distillation）结合案例自适应专家选择机制（case-adaptive specialist selection），动态利用多个专家策略来指导基于视觉的学生策略训练。

Result: 实验表明X-Loco在跌倒恢复和地形穿越等任务上表现优异，消融研究证实该方法有效利用了专家知识并提高了学习效率。

Conclusion: X-Loco是首个在纯速度指令下、无需参考运动即可实现视觉人形机器人直立行走、全身协调和跌倒恢复联合控制的框架，为人形机器人通用运动策略训练提供了有效方案。

Abstract: While recent advances have demonstrated strong performance in individual humanoid skills such as upright locomotion, fall recovery and whole-body coordination, learning a single policy that masters all these skills remains challenging due to the diverse dynamics and conflicting control objectives involved. To address this, we introduce X-Loco, a framework for training a vision-based generalist humanoid locomotion policy. X-Loco trains multiple oracle specialist policies and adopts a synergetic policy distillation with a case-adaptive specialist selection mechanism, which dynamically leverages multiple specialist policies to guide a vision-based student policy. This design enables the student to acquire a broad spectrum of locomotion skills, ranging from fall recovery to terrain traversal and whole-body coordination skills. To the best of our knowledge, X-Loco is the first framework to demonstrate vision-based humanoid locomotion that jointly integrates upright locomotion, whole-body coordination and fall recovery, while operating solely under velocity commands without relying on reference motions. Experimental results show that X-Loco achieves superior performance, demonstrated by tasks such as fall recovery and terrain traversal. Ablation studies further highlight that our framework effectively leverages specialist expertise and enhances learning efficiency.

</details>


### [162] [Micro-Swarm Locomotion Optimization in Dynamic Flow using Multi-Objective Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25025)
*Josef Berman,Oren Gal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合CFD-MO-MARL框架，将高保真纳维-斯托克斯求解器与去中心化近端策略优化相结合，在振荡流环境中训练微机器人集群控制策略。通过PCGrad解决多目标冲突，在脉动动脉波形场景中实现了上游推进、能量效率和运动平滑性的联合优化，并发现了三种涌现行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在真实的、时间依赖的流体环境中协调微型机器人集群，对于生物医学和环境应用仍是一个重大挑战。现有方法难以在复杂流体动力学条件下同时优化多个竞争性目标。

Method: 采用混合CFD-MO-MARL框架，将不可压缩纳维-斯托克斯求解器与去中心化PPO算法结合，并使用PCGrad（投影冲突梯度）技术解决多目标优化中的梯度冲突问题。在2mm通道中模拟16个磁驱动微型机器人在脉动动脉波形中的导航。

Result: 收敛策略实现了进展奖励6.5-7.0、能量效率0.63-0.65、平滑度0.97-0.99，主目标比暴力基线高出8个奖励单位。无PCGrad时训练崩溃，证明了梯度冲突解决的必要性。发现了三种涌现行为：流体动力学节流编队、周期同步棘轮机制和个体化最终逼近策略。

Conclusion: 物理真实的流体-智能体交互可以直接集成到多目标强化学习中，为生物医学导航、环境监测和微流体系统中的微型集群控制提供了可扩展的框架。

Abstract: Coordinating micro-robotic swarms in realistic, time-dependent fluid environments remains a major challenge for biomedical and environmental applications. We present a hybrid CFD-MO-MARL (Computational Fluid Dynamics-Multi Objective-Multi Agent Reinforcement Learning) framework that couples a high-fidelity incompressible Navier--Stokes solver with decentralized proximal policy optimization to learn swarm control policies in oscillatory flow. Sixteen magnetically actuated micro-robots were simulated to navigate a pulsatile arterial waveform within a 2 mm channel while jointly optimizing upstream progression, energy efficiency, and motion smoothness. Conflicting objectives are resolved using Projected Conflicting Gradient (PCGrad) surgery. Without PCGrad, energy and smoothness rewards collapse during training, demonstrating that gradient conflict resolution is essential for stable multi-objective learning. The converged policy achieves progress rewards of 6.5-7.0, energy efficiency of 0.63-0.65, and smoothness of 0.97-0.99, outperforming brute-force baselines by more than 8 reward units on the primary objective. Training reveals three emergent behaviors not encoded in the reward function: hydrodynamic throttling formations that reduce peak flow velocities, a cycle-synchronized ratchet mechanism that exploits flow reversals for upstream movement, and individualized final-approach strategies near the target boundary. These results demonstrate that physically realistic fluid--agent interactions can be integrated directly into multi-objective reinforcement learning, providing a scalable framework for micro-swarm control in biomedical navigation, environmental monitoring, and microfluidic systems.

</details>


### [163] [Benchmarking Vision-Language-Action Models on SO-101: Failure and Recovery Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08881)
*Yi Yu,Xinchuan Qiu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于低成本SO-101机器人平台的标准真实世界基准，用于评估VLA模型和模仿学习策略在操作任务中的鲁棒性。实验表明，更强的预训练VLA策略通常优于模仿学习基线，但执行不稳定是主要失败原因，恢复能力在不同架构间差异显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型评估主要在高仿真环境或昂贵机器人平台上进行，缺乏在低成本真实机器人上的鲁棒性分析。

Method: 在低成本SO-101机器人平台上构建标准化基准，包含4个代表性操作任务和统一评估协议，采用结构化失败分类法、语义/执行级失败分解和恢复感知评估指标，对π0.5、SmolVLA、Wall-X和ACT进行微调与评估。

Result: 强预训练VLA策略总体优于模仿学习基线，但性能高度依赖具体任务；执行不稳定性是主要失败来源；不同架构的恢复能力差异显著。

Conclusion: 超越二元任务成功率的失败与恢复分析对评估具身AI系统的鲁棒性至关重要，SO-101可作为低成本真实机器人部署条件下的实用评估基准。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated strong generalization in robotic manipulation, yet existing evaluations are primarily conducted in simulation or on expensive robotic platforms, leaving their robustness on affordable real-world robots largely unexplored. We present a standardized real-world benchmark for evaluating representative VLA and imitation learning policies on the low-cost SO-101 robotic platform. The benchmark comprises four representative manipulation tasks together with unified evaluation protocols, enabling systematic comparison under embodiment uncertainty. Using real-world teleoperated demonstrations, we fine-tune and evaluate $π_{0.5}$, SmolVLA, Wall-X, and ACT directly on the physical platform. Beyond conventional task success rates, the benchmark incorporates a structured failure taxonomy, semantic- and execution-level failure decomposition, and recovery-aware evaluation metrics to characterize policy robustness. Experimental results show that stronger pretrained VLA policies generally outperform the imitation learning baseline, although performance remains highly task-dependent under low-cost robotic deployment conditions. Execution instability emerges as the dominant failure source, while recovery capability varies substantially across architectures. These results highlight the importance of failure and recovery analysis beyond binary task success and establish SO-101 as a practical benchmark for evaluating embodied AI systems under realistic low-cost robotic deployment conditions.

</details>


### [164] [Improving Robotic Generalist Policies via Flow Reversal Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13675)
*Andy Tang,William Chen,Andrew Wagenmaker,Chelsea Finn,Sergey Levine*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Flow Reversal Steering (FRS)方法，通过逆流过程将次优动作映射为通用策略中的优质动作模式，从而提升机器人操控性能，并结合行为克隆和强化学习实现零样本控制改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用策略虽能从多样数据集中学习广泛技能，但在处理具有挑战性的新任务时，直接指令策略往往失败，需要一种方法从策略丰富的行为先验中推断并调用适当动作。

Method: 提出Flow Reversal Steering (FRS)：将次优但"合理"的动作通过逆流策略传递以找到其潜在噪声，然后将这些噪声映射到邻近的通用策略动作模式。

Result: 在模拟和真实操控场景中，FRS可将来自人类或视觉语言模型的粗略语义引导转化为优质机器人动作；通过行为克隆训练辅助策略输出噪声，在不到一分钟训练内实现高达95%的绝对任务成功率提升；FRS还能利用语义知识引导强化学习，在标准RL无法改进的任务上实现策略提升。

Conclusion: FRS有效弥合了次优动作与通用策略优质动作模式之间的差距，为零样本控制、快速行为克隆和强化学习引导提供了统一框架。

Abstract: Generalist policies can learn a wide range of skills from diverse robot datasets. In order to solve or improve on challenging new tasks, we need a way to infer and invoke the appropriate actions from the policy's rich behavioral prior, especially when directly commanding the policy fails. We focus on flow matching generalists and propose Flow Reversal Steering (FRS): a method that takes suboptimal but ``reasonable'' actions, finds their latent noises by passing them through the flow policy in reverse, and maps them to nearby generalist action modes. We evaluate FRS across many simulated and real-world manipulation settings. First, FRS can turn coarse semantic guidance from humans or vision-language models (VLMs) into corresponding good robot actions, improving zero-shot control. These gains can be distilled with behavioral cloning by training an auxiliary policy to output noises that the generalist maps to good actions -- showing up to 95% absolute task success rate boosts in under a minute of training. Finally, FRS enables policy improvement by bootstrapping reinforcement learning with semantic knowledge, improving on several tasks that standard RL fails to improve on.

</details>


### [165] [EquiDexFlow: Contact-Grounded SE(3)-Equivariant Dexterous Grasp Generative Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12728)
*Clinton Enwerem,John S. Baras,Calin Belta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 EquiDexFlow，一个 SE(3)-等变流匹配模型，可联合预测灵巧手抓取中的腕部姿态、关节角、指尖接触、法向量和接触力，通过投影确保摩擦锥和表面接触约束，并在真实机器人上成功完成开环抓取任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灵巧抓取生成器主要关注运动学可行性，将接触力分析作为后处理验证步骤，导致运动学可行的抓取姿态仍可能违反稳定物理抓取的条件，缺乏对力与接触约束的联合建模。

Method: 1) 构建 SE(3)-等变流匹配模型，联合预测腕部姿态、关节角、指尖接触点、表面法向量和接触力；2) 通过将接触点投影到物体表面、将力约束在库仑摩擦锥内来确保约束满足；3) 对解码后的指尖接触通过每指逆运动学重定位到另一机械手；4) 引入硬件可行的精化步骤保证关节在作动器包络内。

Result: 在200次旋转测试中腕部残差低于0.04°且关节偏差为零；在Allegro Hand上训练后实现零摩擦锥违反、最佳综合得分和最低扳手残差；重定位到LEAP Hand后所有关节至少5%在作动器包络内；物理机器人上所有6个测试对象开环抓取成功，非对称物体在标准姿态和120°共旋转下均成功。

Conclusion: 通过等变流匹配联合建模运动学与力学约束，可生成满足物理稳定条件的灵巧抓取，且具备旋转泛化能力与硬件可行性。

Abstract: Most learned dexterous grasp generators relegate contact forces to a downstream verification step, so a kinematically-plausible pose can still violate the conditions for a stable physical grasp. We address this with EquiDexFlow, an SE(3)-equivariant flow-matching model that jointly predicts wrist pose, joint angles, fingertip contacts, surface normals, and contact forces from an object point cloud. Our architecture projects contacts onto the object surface and forces into the Coulomb friction cone by construction, so placement and friction compliance hold without loss penalties. We prove end-to-end SE(3) equivariance and verify it empirically over 200 rotations, with wrist residuals below $0.04^\circ$ and exactly zero joint deviation. Trained on 8,100 force-closure grasps across 81 objects for the 16-DoF Allegro Hand, our model achieves zero friction violations, the best composite score, and the lowest wrench residual among all ablation variants. We retarget decoded fingertip contacts to a 16-DoF LEAP Hand via per-finger inverse kinematics, and our hardware-feasible refinement places every joint at least 5% inside its actuator envelope while preserving wrench balance. On the physical robot, retargeted EquiDexFlow-decoded grasps complete open-loop pick-and-hold trials on all six test objects, with every asymmetric object succeeding at both the canonical pose and a $120^\circ$ co-rotation. Videos, code, and checkpoints are available at https://equidexflow.github.io.

</details>


### [166] [Bounding Boxes as Goals: Language-Conditioned Grasping via Neuro-Symbolic Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12910)
*Allison Andreyev,Landon Eum,Nestor Tiglao,Romel Gomez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GRASP是一个面向开放词汇桌面操作的框架，利用预训练VLM将自然语言查询转化为神经符号目标状态，通过边界框检测管道实现物理世界接地，无需微调即可在90次真实机器人试验中达到73.3%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在机器人任务与运动规划中虽实现零样本泛化，但计算负担重或需要大量演示训练，缺乏轻量级、无需微调的自然语言驱动机器人操作方案。

Method: 利用预训练VLM将自然语言查询翻译为神经符号目标状态，通过边界框检测管道实现物理世界接地，避免依赖固定颜色列表或硬编码坐标，支持抽象空间概念（如"顶层架子"）的解释与执行。

Result: 在90次真实机器人试验中，三个难度级别总体成功率达73.3%，无需任务特定训练。

Conclusion: GRASP展示了轻量级、无需微调的自然语言驱动机器人操作可行性，为开放词汇桌面操作提供有效方案。

Abstract: For robotics to be effectively integrated into household or industrial environments, machines must adapt to natural-language prompts in real time. Although Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have enabled zero-shot generalization in robot task and motion planning (TAMP), current state-of-the-art approaches often remain computationally "heavyweight" or require extensive training on thousands of demonstrations. We present GRASP (Grounded Reasoning and Symbolic Planning), a framework designed as a step toward open-vocabulary tabletop manipulation. Our approach leverages a pretrained VLM to translate natural-language queries into neuro-symbolic goal states, grounded in the physical world via a bounding-box detection pipeline. Unlike methods that rely on fixed color lists or hard-coded coordinates, GRASP enables robots to interpret abstract spatial concepts such as "top shelf" and execute tasks without additional fine-tuning. We achieve 73.3% overall success across 90 real-robot trials at three difficulty levels, requiring no task-specific training.

</details>


<div id='cs.SI'></div>

# cs.SI [[Back]](#toc)

### [167] [Fair Influence Maximization in Hypergraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14189)
*Zoë Abhelakh,Tianrui Mao,Huijuan Wang*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于超图公平影响力最大化（FIMH）的启发式种子选择算法，在SICP传播模型下通过无参乌托邦距离准则平衡影响力与公平性，实验证明其能在保持影响力水平的同时显著降低社区间影响力差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有影响力最大化方法在具有社区结构的网络中会导致被影响节点在社区间分布不均，引发公平性问题。虽然成对网络中的公平影响力最大化已有研究，但超图（能更准确刻画群体交互）上的公平影响力最大化问题尚未被探索。

Method: 提出FIMH算法，在SICP传播模型下，迭代估计每个候选节点对影响力和公平性的贡献，通过无参的乌托邦距离准则权衡这两个目标，选择最佳节点作为种子。

Result: 在7个真实世界超图上的实验表明，FIMH能达到与最先进IM方法相当的影响力水平，同时显著降低影响力差异。对种子节点拓扑属性及其对影响力和公平性贡献的分析进一步验证了FIMH的有效性。

Conclusion: FIMH成功将公平影响力最大化问题从成对网络拓展到超图，为群体交互场景下的公平传播提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: The influence maximization problem aims to select a set of seed nodes that maximize the influence, i.e., the average number of influenced nodes, at the end of a spreading process. It has been widely studied with applications in viral marketing, public health campaigns, and social influence. In networks with pronounced community structure, existing approaches often yield an uneven distribution of influenced nodes across communities, which is unfair. Although the fair influence maximization (FIM) problem has been studied for pairwise networks, it remains largely unexplored for hyper graphs, which more accurately represent real-world systems involving group interactions. We introduce FIMH, a heuristic seed-selection algorithm for FIM on hyper graphs, under the Susceptible-Infected Contact Process (SICP) spreading model. FIMH iteratively estimates the contribution of each candidate node to influence and fairness and selects the node that best trades off these two objectives as an additional seed using a parameter-free utopia-distance criterion. Experiments on seven real-world hypergraphs demonstrate that FIMH achieves an influence comparable to that of state-of-the-art IM methods while significantly reducing influence disparity. Analysis of the topological properties of the selected seed nodes and their contributions to influence and fairness further supports the effectiveness of FIMH.

</details>


### [168] [Games Mapper: Topological Data Analysis of Steam Genres](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14376)
*Nicolas Grelier,Stéphane Kaufmann,Johannes Pfau*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于拓扑数据分析中Mapper算法的分析工具Games Mapper，用于捕捉视频游戏类型随时间演化的连续拓扑关系，并通过自动化聚类标签方法实现可解释的可视化。通过对Steam平台2015-2025年模拟类游戏的案例分析，该工具能自主分割出连贯的子类型并捕捉市场动态变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频游戏行业主题和类型不断演变，工作室和发行商需要理解特定游戏类别的结构动态和时间演化，以识别可行的市场切入点。传统聚类技术难以捕捉数据集之间随时间变化的连续拓扑关系。

Method: 基于拓扑数据分析中的Mapper算法，扩展了标准算法，加入了自动化聚类标签方法，生成高度可解释和交互式的类型演化可视化。

Result: 通过对Steam平台2015-2025年模拟类游戏的案例分析，Games Mapper能够自主将类型分割为连贯、持久的子类型，并捕捉到动态的市场变化。

Conclusion: Games Mapper是一个可扩展、通用化的工具，能够帮助研究人员和产业界揭示复杂的市场结构，追踪Steam生态系统的演化。

Abstract: The video game industry comprises a vast, continuously evolving landscape of themes and genres. For studios and publishers that navigate this competitive market, understanding the structural dynamics and temporal evolution of specific game categories is crucial for identifying viable entry points. In this paper, we introduce Games Mapper, a novel analytical tool based on the Mapper algorithm from topological data analysis. Unlike traditional clustering techniques, Games Mapper captures the continuous topological relationships between datasets over time (or other guiding variables). We extend the standard algorithm with an automated cluster labelling method, ensuring highly interpretable and interactive visualisations of genre evolution. To demonstrate the efficacy of our approach, we present a comprehensive case study on Simulation games released on Steam between 2015 and 2025. Games Mapper autonomously segments the genre into coherent, persistent subgenres, and captures dynamic market shifts. Ultimately, we provide a scalable, generalisable tool for researchers and industrials to unravel complex market structures and track the evolution of the Steam ecosystem.

</details>


### [169] [Understanding the Personal Networks of People Experiencing Homelessness in King County, WA with aggregate Relational Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.13517)
*Zack Almquist,Ihsan Kahveci,Owen Kajfasz,Janelle Rothfolk,Amy Hagopian*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 基于2022-2024年对华盛顿州金县3000多名无家可归者的调查数据，本文分析了无家可归者的社交网络（熟人、密友、亲属和同伴推荐网络），发现社交连接性随时间下降：平均熟人数量从80降至40，亲属网络扩张表明更多家庭成员也经历无家可归，而密友数量虽稳定但相对人口增长意味着连接减弱。研究指出政策应减少流离失所并提供聚集空间以促进社会连接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无家可归者的社交网络是未被充分研究但对获取信息、支持和安全至关重要的方面，现有研究对此关注不足。

Method: 采用基于网络的抽样方法，收集2022-2024年华盛顿州金县超过3000名无家可归者的调查数据，分析熟人、密友、亲属和同伴推荐网络。

Result: 平均熟人数量从2023年的80人降至2024年的40人；密友数量稳定在2.5人（但人口增长表明连接减弱）；亲属网络扩张。

Conclusion: 无家可归者面临日益严重的社交隔离，政策应减少流离失所并提供聚集空间以促进社会连接和社区建设。

Abstract: The social networks of people experiencing homelessness are an understudied but vital aspect of their lives, offering access to information, support, and safety. In 2023, the U.S. Department of Housing and Urban Development reported 653,100 people experiencing homelessness on any given night -- a 23% rise since 2022, though likely an undercount. This paper examines a unique three-year dataset (2022-2024) of survey responses from over 3,000 unhoused individuals in King County, WA, collected via network-based sampling methods to estimate the unsheltered population. Our study analyzes the networks of the unsheltered population, focusing on acquaintance, close friendship, kinship, and peer referral networks. Findings reveal a decline in social connectivity over time. The average number of acquaintances dropped from 80 in 2023 to 40 in 2024. Close friendship levels remained stable at 2.5, but given the growth in the homeless population, this suggests decreased network connectivity. Kinship networks expanded, indicating that more family members of unhoused individuals are also experiencing homelessness. These trends suggest increasing social disconnection, possibly driven by displacement and a rise in newly homeless individuals. The growing isolation may reduce opportunities for information sharing and mutual support. However, the increased reliance on family networks highlights the shifting dynamics of social support within this community. This research underscores the need for policies fostering social connections and community building, such as reducing displacement and providing spaces for congregation, to counter the growing anomie among unhoused populations.

</details>


### [170] [Climate Policy Elites' Twitter Interactions across Nine Countries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.15545)
*Ted Hsuan Yun Chen,Arttu Malkamäki,Ali Faqeeh,Esa Palosaari,Anniina Kotkaniemi,Hasti Narimanzadeh,Laura Funke,Cáit Gleeson,James Goodman,Antti Gronow,Marlene Kammerer,Myanna Lahsen,Alexandre Marques,Petr Ocelik,Shivangi Seth,Mark Stoddart,Martin Svozil,Pradip Swarnakar,Matthew Trull,Paul Wagner,Yixi Yang,Mikko Kivelä,Tuomas Ylä-Anttila*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 对9个国家904个气候政策行为体的Twitter活动（2017-2022年，16086个账号，4000万条活动）进行多中心治理分析，发现各国对气候事件响应模式相似，但国内政策系统间直接互动稀少，主要通过国际组织、气候活动家和研究者账号的相同内容间接连接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体已成为气候政策行为体互动和政治博弈的关键平台，但现有研究缺乏从多中心治理视角系统分析跨国气候政策行为体在社交媒体上的互动模式，特别是公私边界模糊如何影响在线政策过程。

Method: 识别9个国家904个气候政策行为体的Twitter账号，收集2017-2022年共16086个账号的4000万条活动数据，从多中心治理系统视角分析行为体及其互动，考察时间变化、跨国差异及国家内外的互动模式。

Result: 气候相关活动的突出程度存在显著时间和跨国差异，但各国政策系统对气候抗议等气候事件的反应模式相似；各国政策系统间直接互动极少，但通过国际组织、气候活动家和研究者账号的一致内容消费实现间接连接。

Conclusion: 多中心治理框架适用于分析社交媒体上的气候政策互动；国家政策系统在社交媒体上呈现"间接连通但直接分割"的特征，国际行为体和气候活动家充当了连接不同国家政策系统的关键枢纽。

Abstract: Social media is an important space for interactions between climate policy actors, with a burgeoning literature recognizing them as critical platforms of political contestation. We identified Twitter accounts associated with 904 climate change policy actors across nine countries, and collected their activities from 2017--2022, totalling 40 million activities from 16,086 accounts at different organizational levels. We studied these actors and their interactions as a polycentric governance system, emphasizing how boundary blurring between the public and private on social media platforms uniquely shapes the online policy process. Initial results show there is considerable temporal and cross-national variation in how prominent climate-related activities were, but all national policy systems generally responded to climate-related events, such as climate protests, in a similar manner. Examining patterns of interaction within and across countries, we find that these national policy systems rarely directly interact with one another, but are connected through consistently engaging with the same content produced by accounts of international organizations, climate activists, and researchers.

</details>


### [171] [From Birdwatch to Community Notes, from Twitter to X: four years of community-based content moderation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.09585)
*Saeedeh Mohammadi,Narges Chinichian,Hannah Doyal,Anna Bertani,Kristina Skutilova,Hao Cui,Michele d'Errico,Siobhan Grayson,Taha Yasseri*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文对X平台（原Twitter）推出的社区笔记（Community Notes，前身Birdwatch）机制进行了为期四年的描述性研究，涵盖语言多样性、信息来源、贡献者行为、评分模式和互动网络，并发布了数据集、源代码及文献综述，以支持后续研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着社区笔记模式在多个社交媒体平台推广，亟需系统评估其底层动态和有效性，为此本文对该机制进行首次大规模描述性调查。

Method: 解析了社区笔记项目前四年的笔记和评分数据，对所有笔记进行语言检测；对英文笔记提取嵌入URL并识别讨论主题；构建贡献者月度互动网络。

Result: 提供了对社区笔记在语言多样性、来源实践、贡献者活动、评分行为和互动网络等方面的描述性发现，同时发布了经过整理的数据集、源代码及文献综述。

Conclusion: 本研究的描述性分析、数据集、代码和文献综述为推进社区笔记及社区化内容审核机制的研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Community Notes (formerly known as Birdwatch) is the first large-scale crowdsourced content moderation initiative launched by X (formerly Twitter) in January 2021. As the Community Notes model gains momentum across other social media platforms, there is a growing need to assess its underlying dynamics and effectiveness. This paper provides a descriptive investigation of Community Notes during its first four years, examining its linguistic diversity, sourcing practices, Contributor activity, rating behaviour, and interaction networks. In addition, we release a curated dataset and accompanying source code to support future research, along with a review of prior research on Community Notes. We parsed Notes and ratings data from the first four years of the program and conducted language detection across all Notes. For English-language Notes, we extracted embedded URLs and identified discussion topics in each Note. Additionally, we constructed monthly interaction networks among the Contributors. Together, the descriptive analysis, dataset, code, and literature review provide a foundation for advancing research on Community Notes and community-based content moderation more broadly.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.NC'></div>

# q-bio.NC [[Back]](#toc)

### [172] [Decoding Semantic Categories from Picture-Naming EEG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14614)
*Wei Hu,Binbin Xu*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 该研究利用高密度EEG和预训练编码器，在图片命名任务中解码语义类别信息，结合早期和命名相关时间窗口实现了0.781的平衡准确率，证明语义类别结构在EEG活动中可被恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在自然图片命名过程中，语义类别信息是否可以从高密度EEG信号中解码恢复，以验证现代神经解码方法在语言产生研究中应用的有效性。

Method: 16名法语被试进行线条画图片命名任务；使用多语言文本嵌入模型将图片标签嵌入并组织为9个语义类别；利用预训练单通道EEG编码器对早期（感知）、命名相关（后期）及组合时间窗口的EEG数据进行通道级表征；进行9类语义分类解码。

Result: 所有时间窗口的解码均高于随机水平；早期窗口平衡准确率0.562，命名相关窗口0.610，两者组合达0.781，最大Macro-F1为0.784；各类语义类别均有一致增益；传感器级解码图显示空间分布的信息。

Conclusion: 语义类别结构在图片命名时的EEG活动中有所反映，早期和命名相关时间窗口提供互补信息，支持现代神经解码方法用于研究口语产生中的词汇-语义加工。

Abstract: Picture naming requires the transformation of visual object information into a spoken lexical response through perceptual, semantic, lexical, and articulatory processes. This study asked whether semantic-category information is recoverable from high-density EEG during overt picture naming. Sixteen native French-speaking participants performed a picture-naming task using line drawings. Picture labels were embedded with a multilingual text-embedding model and organized into nine interpretable semantic categories, providing a data-driven semantic target space for neural decoding. EEG activity was represented channel-wise using a pre-trained single-channel EEG encoder over an early post-stimulus window, a later naming-related window, and their combination. Nine-class decoding showed above-chance semantic-category discrimination in all temporal representations. Balanced accuracy increased from 0.562 in the early window to 0.610 in the naming-related window, and reached 0.781 when both windows were combined, with a maximum Macro-F1 of 0.784. Class-level F1 scores showed consistent gains across semantic categories, and sensor-level decoding maps indicated spatially distributed category information. These findings suggest that semantic-category structure is reflected in EEG activity during overt picture naming and that early and naming-related temporal windows provide complementary information. The results support the use of modern neural decoding methods as tools for investigating lexical-semantic processing in spoken language production.

</details>


### [173] [Prospective Coding and Path Integration Emerge as Equilibrium Solutions of Self-Organizing Neural Networks with Firing-Rate Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14649)
*Facundo Emina,Emilio Kropff*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本研究提出一个理论框架，揭示连续吸引子神经网络（CANNs）中的结构化连接如何通过Hebbian可塑性、发放率适应和全局抑制自组织形成。平移不变输入驱动稳定的高斯型前馈权重涌现，前馈结构可自发产生预期性位移，而引入递归连接可维持活动凸包的自持续运动，并通过外部速度调制实现精确的单向路径积分。研究提出前瞻编码和路径积分是自组织竞争网络自然共现的属性，而非人工设计的特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CANNs依赖预接线的递归连接来建模空间表征、路径积分和预期动力学，但其结构化连接如何通过学习涌现的生物学机制尚未被充分探索。

Method: 构建理论框架，结合Hebbian可塑性、发放率适应和全局抑制机制，分析平移不变输入下前馈权重的自组织过程，并研究递归连接、外部速度调制对网络动力学的影响。

Result: ①平移不变输入驱动稳定高斯型前馈权重的涌现；②前馈架构中自发出现预期性位移，无需递归兴奋性侧支；③多层网络中预期位移可线性放大，与内嗅皮层浅层预期活动一致；④引入递归连接可学习自维持移动凸包；⑤外部时变基线电流（编码速度）调制使系统实现精确单向路径积分。

Conclusion: 前瞻编码和路径积分不是人工设计的特征，而是单一自组织竞争网络自然共现的属性。

Abstract: Continuous Attractor Neural Networks (CANNs) traditionally rely on pre-wired recurrent connectivity to model spatial representations, path integration, and anticipatory dynamics. However, the biological mechanisms through which this structured connectivity emerges via learning remain relatively unexplored. This work presents a theoretical framework revealing how continuous attractor connectivity and its computational properties self-organize through Hebbian plasticity, firing-rate adaptation, and global inhibition. We show that translationally invariant inputs naturally drive the emergence of stable, Gaussian-profiled feedforward weights. Crucially, anticipatory dynamics arise spontaneously within these feedforward architectures, shifting the activity bump forward without requiring recurrent excitatory collaterals. This predictive shift can be linearly amplified across multilayer networks, consistent with anticipatory activity observed in the superficial layers of the entorhinal cortex. Furthermore, introducing recurrent interactions allows the network to learn connections capable of self-sustaining a moving bump of activity. Finally, by modulating the network with an external, time-varying baseline current that encodes speed, the system adjusts its intrinsic velocity to function as a precise unidirectional path integrator. Ultimately, this study suggests that prospective coding and path integration are not manually engineered features, but rather naturally co-emergent properties of a single self-organizing competitive network.

</details>


### [174] [Implications of hierarchical Markov models of behavior: on irreversibility, predictability, and dimensionality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14692)
*John J. Vastola,Kanaka Rajan*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文探讨了用马尔可夫模型描述动物行为的理论含义，指出模型矩阵的特征值和特征向量可解释行为的时间尺度及其上的行为修饰，并通过玩具示例和真实数据验证了这些观点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着量化动物行为高层结构的工具日趋成熟，尤其是将自发行为表示为一系列刻板、神经定义明确的"音节"的工具，领域需要重新审视一个基本理论问题：如果行为的粗粒度结构能被马尔可夫模型准确描述，这些模型究竟告诉我们关于行为的什么信息？

Method: 从理论上探讨马尔可夫模型的行为描述含义，分析模型相关矩阵的特征值和特征向量如何提供可解释的时间尺度及该尺度上的行为修饰，并用玩具示例和拟合真实数据的马尔可夫模型进行说明。

Result: 模型矩阵的特征值和特征向量能够提供可解释的时间尺度，以及在这些时间尺度上发生的行为修饰，从而定量刻画行为的序列性质和有效维度。

Conclusion: 通过分析马尔可夫表示的理论后果，澄清了行为量化进展的理论意义。

Abstract: The maturation of quantitative tools for studying the high-level structure of animal behavior, and especially tools which represent spontaneous behavior as a sequence of stereotyped and neurally well-defined 'syllables', demands that the field revisit a fundamental theoretical question: if the coarse structure of behavior can be accurately described by Markov models, what do these models really tell us about behavior? In this work, we explore the theoretical implications of these models and discuss how they allow us to quantitatively formulate questions about the sequence-like nature and effective dimensionality of behavior. One important insight is that the eigenvalues and eigenvectors of various model-associated matrices furnish interpretable time scales and modifications of behavior that occur on those time scales. We illustrate our points using both toy examples and Markov models fit to real data. By analyzing the consequences of Markov representations, we clarify the theoretical meaning of progress in quantifying behavior.

</details>


<div id='cs.CY'></div>

# cs.CY [[Back]](#toc)

### [175] [Cross-Dataset Bloom Question Classification: Supervised Models and Prompted LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13684)
*Abdolali Faraji,Mohammadreza Molavi,Zohreh Rasoulkhani,Mohammadreza Tavakoli,Gábor Kismihók*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 现有机器学习/深度学习方法在跨数据集场景下泛化能力差，而大语言模型结合上下文示例与课程特定行为动词的提示策略表现稳定；基于此开发的轻量级界面在可用性研究中展现出低工作负荷和高可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 试题自动布鲁姆分类可减轻教师工作量，但标注存在主观性；现有ML/DL方法缺乏跨数据集评估，LLM在此任务上的效果也未系统研究。

Method: 在五个数据集上评估现有ML/DL方法的跨数据集泛化能力，并测试LLM多种提示策略；最佳策略结合上下文示例与课程特定行为动词。基于此开发轻量级UI并开展可用性研究。

Result: 监督ML/DL模型在未见数据集上性能显著下降，而LLM更为稳定；最佳提示策略驱动的UI在可用性研究中表现低负荷、高可用性。

Conclusion: LLM结合上下文示例与课程特定行为动词的策略可作为跨教育场景鲁棒替代方案，其轻量级实现可有效辅助教师完成大规模试题自动分类。

Abstract: Automatic Bloom's taxonomy classification of assessment questions can substantially reduce instructor workload, but labeling is subjective and teacher-dependent. Prior machine learning (ML) and deep learning (DL) approaches reported strong within-dataset results, yet were rarely evaluated in cross-dataset settings, leaving real-world generalizability unclear; meanwhile, LLM effectiveness for Bloom question classification has not been systematically studied. We evaluated the cross-dataset generalization of existing ML/DL methods and assessed LLMs with multiple prompting strategies on five datasets; the best prompting strategy combined in-context examples with course-specific action verbs. Supervised ML/DL models degraded substantially on unseen datasets, whereas LLMs were more stable, suggesting a robust alternative across diverse educational contexts. Based on the best prompting strategy, we also presented a lightweight UI that supports instructors in automatically classifying large question banks; a usability study indicated low workload and high usability.

</details>


### [176] [Picturing Perceptions: An Open-Source Toolkit to Uncover Bias in Humans and Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13688)
*Saurabh Khanna,Zhijun Chen,Chei Billedo,Jiayi Yan,Irene van Driel,Alex Barco Martelo,Hugo Moreda Cartagena,Haizea Gonzalez Atorrasagasti,Markel Adanez Perez,Daniela An,Qianyi Wang,Xinkangrui Gao,Lauren Taylor,Olga Eisele,Sindy Sumter*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文介绍PictoPercept，一个通过视觉强制选择比较来测量偏见（包括人类和AI系统）的开源工具包，并在美国全国代表性样本和GPT-5上进行了验证，发现人类和AI都存在系统性偏见，且AI的偏见比人类更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统偏见测量工具存在根本性局限：难以捕捉交叉身份、无法评估AI系统、缺乏人口统计现实基础、易受社会期望效应影响，因此需要一种统一的、以人口基准为基础的偏见评估方法。

Method: 开发PictoPercept开源工具包，使用规范化的面部照片进行视觉强制选择比较，参与者在照片对中判断谁更可能拥有更高收入，然后将选择结果与美国劳工统计局的实际数据进行比较。

Result: 三个关键发现：1) 参与者严重低估亚裔美国人的收入（尽管该群体实际收入最高），同时高估拉丁裔男性和白人男性的收入；2) 内群体偏好并非普遍存在——白人男性表现出明显内群体偏见，但亚裔参与者实际上低估自己群体的收入；3) GPT-5表现出比人类更强的偏见，对所有女性群体存在系统性严重低估。

Conclusion: PictoPercept能够对人类和AI系统进行统一的偏见评估，揭示出偏离人口统计现实的系统性错误认知。

Abstract: Bias in human judgment and artificial intelligence systems poses critical challenges across consequential domains like hiring, loans, and criminal justice. However, traditional bias measurement tools face fundamental limitations: they struggle to capture intersectional identities, cannot evaluate AI systems, lack grounding in demographic reality, and remain vulnerable to social desirability effects. We introduce PictoPercept, an open-source toolkit that measures bias through visual forced-choice comparisons grounded in population level benchmarks. Participants view pairs of normed facial photographs and assess who is more likely to have higher earnings, with selections compared against actual U.S. Bureau of Labor Statistics data. We validate PictoPercept with a nationally representative sample of 283 American adults and assess GPT-5, a mainstream generative model, using identical stimuli. Our study reveals three key findings: First, participants dramatically underestimate Asian American earnings despite this group having the highest actual earnings, while overestimating Latino male and White male earnings. Second, ingroup favoritism is not universal as White males show clear ingroup bias, but Asian participants actually underestimate their own group's earnings. Third, GPT-5 exhibits substantially stronger biases than humans, with stark systematic underestimation of all female groups. These findings suggest that PictoPercept enables unified bias assessment across human and AI systems while revealing systematic misperceptions that diverge from demographic reality.

</details>


### [177] [Empirical Evidence on Genre-Time Correlation in Box-Office Success Using Exploratory Data Analysis and Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13689)
*Iqra Tariq*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究通过EDA和监督式机器学习方法，探究电影类型与上映时间对票房成功的影响。基于200部卖座片和100部失败片的分析发现，特定类型在特定月份有显著成功模式（如动画/超级英雄片在6-7月、惊悚/爱情片在11月），并通过多种回归模型验证了类型-时间的强相关性，为制片和排片策略提供数据驱动决策支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电影行业制作成本高、财务风险大，准确预测票房成功对制片方和投资者至关重要。现有研究对电影类型与上映时间的交互作用关注不足，本文旨在通过数据驱动的分析揭示这一关系，以降低投资风险。

Method: 采用探索性数据分析（EDA）发现类型-时间的统计模式，再运用监督式机器学习方法进行验证。数据集由200部票房成功片和100部失败片组成，来源包括IMDb、Box Office Mojo等。机器学习算法包括LWT、多层感知机、随机树和决策桩，采用交叉验证和百分比分割方法评估模型。

Result: EDA显示动画和超级英雄片在6-7月成功率最高（28%和29%），惊悚和爱情片在11月成功率更高；动作和喜剧片在3月、4月、8月失败率更高。多种回归模型均表现出高预测准确率，验证了类型与上映时间之间存在强相关性。

Conclusion: 研究证实电影类型与上映月份对票房成功存在显著关联，为内容制作和上映排期的战略规划提供了有价值的见解，凸显了在媒体行业应用数据分析进行风险缓解和优化决策的必要性。

Abstract: The movie industry is one of the fastest-growing global sectors, characterized by high production costs and significant financial risk. Given the capital-intensive nature of filmmaking, accurately predicting box office success is of critical importance for stakeholders ranging from producers to investors. This study investigates the correlation between movie genre and release timing as predictive factors for commercial success. A combined approach involving EDA and supervised machine learning techniques is proposed to assess this relationship. The dataset, comprising the top 200 box office hits and the top 100 flops, was curated from reliable sources, including IMDb, Box Office Mojo, The Numbers, and Wikipedia. EDA revealed that specific genres show statistically significant patterns of success or failure in particular months. For instance, animated and superhero movies achieved their peak success rates in June and July (28% and 29%, respectively), while thrillers and romance genres showed higher hit rates in November. Conversely, the flop dataset showed genres like action and comedy more frequently underperforming in March, April, and August. To validate these findings, multiple regression-based machine learning models were applied using both cross-validation and percentage-split methods. Algorithms such as LWT, Multilayer Perceptron, Random Tree, and Decision Stamp demonstrated high predictive accuracy, reinforcing the hypothesis of genre-time dependency. The results consistently indicated a strong correlation between release month and genre performance, providing valuable insight for strategic planning in content production and release scheduling. This study highlights the growing need to apply data analytics in the media industry, like other data-driven domains, for risk mitigation and optimized decision-making.

</details>


### [178] [Indirect Computing Model with Indirect Formal Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13690)
*Xiaohui Zou*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文从人机接口与协同计算程序结合的协同智能计算系统视角，探讨了间接计算模型与间接形式方法支持的优化云计算技术原理，介绍了兼容大小字符串的间接计算模型和间接形式理论，并以中文信息数据为例展示了协同智能计算系统原型设计，其意义在于促进云计算从数据中心向知识中心的优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在系统回顾图灵可计算性理论、克林小字符串形式理论、冯·诺依曼数字计算机架构及图灵AI判定假设等已有理论对主流通用数字计算机范式影响的基础上，提出一种新型计算模型以优化云计算。

Method: 采用人机接口与协同计算程序相结合的协同智能计算系统视角，引入兼容大小字符串的间接计算模型和间接形式理论，并以中文信息数据为例进行原型设计。

Result: 提出了一个协同智能计算系统原型，该原型能够促进云计算从数据中心向知识中心的优化转变。

Conclusion: 该成果有助于优化云计算架构，推动其从传统的数据中心模式向知识中心模式演进。

Abstract: This paper,from the perspective of a collaborative intelligent computing system formed by combining human-computer interface and collaborative computing programs, discusses the principles of optimized cloud computing technology supported by the combination of an indirect computing model and an indirect formal method. On the basis of systematically reviewing the influence of previous theoretical achievements Turing's computability theory,Kleene's formal theory of small strings,von Neumann's digital computer architecture and Turing's hypothesis on AI judgment on the mainstream general-purpose digital computer paradigm,the author focuses on introducing an indirect computing model and an indirect formal theory compatible with both large and small strings. Using Chinese information data as an example,the design concept of a collaborative intelligent computing system prototype is presented. The significance is that this achievement facilitates optimization of cloud computing from data centers to knowledge centers.

</details>


### [179] [Incentives Of EdTech: A Systematic Review Of EduNLP Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13691)
*Gabrielle Gaudeau,Aoife O'Driscoll,Jasper Degraeuwe,Andrew Caines,Donya Rooein,Zeerak Talat*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文对2024-2025年ACL教育应用方向的204篇论文进行系统文献综述，发现教育NLP研究中存在利益相关者失衡：教师作为最受影响的群体仅33.3%被列为受益者，真实部署率仅9.8%，伦理参与多停留在表态层面，反映出私营部门激励与教育基础设施需求之间的张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NLP社区投入大量资源开发教育技术，但尚不清楚这些研究究竟服务了哪方利益相关者。作者希望通过系统综述揭示教育NLP研究中的利益分配失衡问题。

Method: 对2024-2025年ACL计算语言学会教育应用兴趣组204篇论文进行系统文献综述，并与更广泛的ACL文集中的教育技术论文进行交叉验证，分析利益相关者纳入情况与研究任务优先级。

Result: 教师作为受益者的比例仅33.3%；真实世界部署仅占9.8%；伦理参与多停留在"承认"而非"行动"层面；存在私营部门激励与教育基础设施需求之间的拉锯。

Conclusion: 基于语料中的典型案例，作者提出了更具责任感的教育NLP研究实践建议，呼吁关注教师需求、推动真实部署、从伦理表态转向伦理行动。

Abstract: While the Natural Language Processing community has dedicated significant resources in developing educational technologies (EdTech) that support this shift, it remains unclear whose interests are being best served among the stakeholders of education.
  In this paper, we present a systematic literature review of 204 papers published in venues of the Association for Computational Linguistics' Special Interest Group on Building Educational Applications in 2024 and 2025, and validate these against EdTech papers from the wider ACL Anthology. By examining stakeholder inclusion and the prioritisation of research tasks, our findings reveal a critical tension: a push and pull between private-sector incentives and the foundational needs of educational infrastructure. Our analysis reveals that teachers are systematically under-represented as beneficiaries of research (33.3%) despite being the most affected, that real-world deployment remains rare (9.8%), and that ethical engagement tends toward acknowledgement rather than action. Drawing on exemplary papers in our corpus, we offer concrete recommendations for more responsible EduNLP research practices.

</details>


### [180] [Limited Marginal Benefit of Reasoning-Heavy LLM Deployment in ESG Narrative Scoring: A 4-Model Consensus Study on Japanese Listed Firms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13693)
*Hiroyuki Kokubu*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文探究了在ESG文本评分任务中，使用推理密集型前沿大语言模型是否值得其高昂成本。通过对10家日本上市公司、3个评分维度的120个评分数据进行四模型共识设计实验，发现推理型模型与三个非推理型模型的平均绝对偏差仅为0.38（5分制），仅2%的配对比较出现两分偏差，且无一超过两分。但推理型模型的成本却是三个非推理型模型集成成本的5.6倍。结论是：在基于区间的ESG叙事评分中，推理密集型部署并未带来实质性改进，反而大幅增加运营成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前使用大语言模型自动化评分ESG叙事披露的方式日益流行，但推理密集型前沿模型是否值得其高昂成本尚未得到实证检验。作者希望填补这一实证空白，为ESG自动评分系统和企业LLM部署治理提供成本效益方面的决策依据。

Method: 使用四模型共识设计，包含一个推理型前沿模型（reasoning-on）和三个非推理型模型（reasoning-off），对10家日本上市公司在三个评分维度（定量目标、进展跟踪基础设施、外部标准一致性）上进行评分，共获得120个（公司×维度×模型）评分数据。通过计算平均绝对偏差（MAD）和配对偏差分布来量化差异，同时进行每家公司成本核算。

Result: 推理型模型与非推理型模型之间的平均绝对偏差为0.38（5分制）；仅2%的配对比较出现两分偏差，且无一超过两分。推理型模型的单独成本约为三个非推理型模型集成成本的5.6倍。

Conclusion: 在基于区间的ESG叙事评分中，推理密集型部署并未显著改善评分结果，但大幅增加了运营成本。研究建议采用非推理型模型的共识方法作为更具成本效益的方案，并对ESG自动评分管线的部署治理提出了启示。

Abstract: Automated scoring of ESG narrative disclosures with large language models (LLMs) is gaining traction, yet whether reasoning-heavy frontier models add value commensurate with their cost remains empirically unsettled. We evaluate this question on a corpus of ten Japanese listed firms across three rubric axes -- quantitative targets, progress-tracking infrastructure, and external-standard alignment -- using a four-model consensus design that combines a reasoning-on frontier model with three reasoning-off contemporaries. Across 120 firm x axis x model scores, the pooled mean absolute deviation between the reasoning-on model and each reasoning-off counterpart is 0.38 on a 5-point scale; only 2% of pairwise comparisons reach a two-point deviation, and none exceeds two points. Per-firm cost accounting shows the reasoning-on arm alone costs roughly 5.6x as much as the three-provider reasoning-off ensemble, for outcomes that differ only within small margins. We conclude that in span-based ESG narrative scoring, reasoning-heavy deployment does not materially improve outcomes relative to reasoning-off consensus, while substantially increasing operational cost. We discuss implications for cost-effective ESG auto-scoring pipelines and LLM deployment governance in applied accountability settings. An earlier version of this work is available on SSRN (Abstract ID 6683303).

</details>


### [181] [AGORA: Can Deliberation and Governance Gates Absorb Participation Bias in Transit Planning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13696)
*Jung-Hoon Cho,Cathy Wu*

Main category: cs.CY
