TL;DR: 本文提出AGORA框架，通过系统性改变会议参与者构成、结构化审议和治理门控机制，研究公交网络设计中的参与偏差问题。实验表明：审议是参与者构成影响结果的关键机制；代表性抽样在尾部风险和公平性上优于偏态构成；治理门控能压缩结果差异但需实例化校准。研究将参与偏差从不可控输入重构为过程设计问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前公交网络设计实践中，公众听证会的参与者构成是自我选择的，导致参与者组合成为不可控的结果变异来源，影响网络设计的公平性和稳定性。

Method: 提出AGORA框架，固定网络、需求和求解器不变，通过利益相关者代理、结构化审议和治理门控系统性变化会议参与者构成，在两个基准网络上进行实验。

Result: (i) 总体结果差异不大，但代表性采样在尾部风险和公平性上优于偏态构成；(ii) 无审议时构成变化不产生结果差异，审议是核心机制；(iii) 治理门控在Mandl网络上压缩方差且不改变均值，但在Mumford0上接受率低需实例化校准。

Conclusion: 参与偏差可从不可控输入重构为过程设计问题——即使无法保证代表性参与，良好的审议结构和治理标准也能显著降低结果对参与者构成的依赖。

Abstract: Transit network design depends not only on the optimization algorithm but also on who shows up to the public hearing. Current practice often collects one-directional comments from self-selected attendees, leaving participant mix as an uncontrolled source of outcome variation. We present AGORA, a framework that holds the network, demand, and solver fixed while systematically varying meeting composition through stakeholder agents, structured deliberation, and governance gates. Across two standard benchmark networks at different scales, we find that (i) aggregate outcomes vary little across compositions, but on tail risk and fairness disparity, representative sampling still tends to outperform skewed compositions; (ii) without deliberation, composition produces no variation at all, showing that deliberation is the mechanism through which who attends affects outcomes; and (iii) governance gates compress cross-profile variance without shifting the average outcome on Mandl, but low acceptance on Mumford0 shows thresholds require instance-specific calibration. These findings reframe participation bias from an uncontrollable input to a process-design problem: even without guaranteed representative attendance, well-structured deliberation and governance criteria can substantially reduce how much outcomes depend on who is in the room.

</details>


### [182] [Position: AI Must Become Planet-Centered, Not Just Human-Centered](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13704)
*Maria Perez-Ortiz*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出"以行星为中心的AI"（PCAI）设计哲学，主张将AI重新定向到行星尺度的社会-生态系统及其长期轨迹，并论证缺乏系统考量的AI优化更可能加剧而非缓解系统不稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当代AI范式在支持复杂全球目标方面存在不足，尤其是在当前以系统性风险、非平稳性和深度不确定性为特征的行星条件下，以人为中心的AI框架的局限性变得尤为突出。

Method: 基于系统思维，将地球视为一个相互关联的整体（人类是其中一部分），从问题制定、模型设计、评估到部署重新塑造AI生命周期，强调与全球议程对齐、系统感知的AI基础、面向轨迹的评估和可监测性。

Result: 提出了一个可证伪的论断：缺乏对系统性后果进行显式考虑的AI优化，更可能加剧而非缓解系统不稳定。

Conclusion: PCAI作为一种设计哲学和研究议程，为重新定向AI以支持行星尺度社会-生态系统的长期可持续发展提供了理论框架和实践方向。

Abstract: This position paper argues that contemporary AI paradigms are insufficient for supporting complex global goals and introduces Planet-Centered AI (PCAI) as a design philosophy and research agenda that reorients AI toward planetary-scale socio-ecological systems and their long-term trajectories. A planet-centered approach is grounded in systems thinking, treating Earth as an interconnected whole of which humans are part. We diagnose recurring limitations across AI frameworks, many of which remain human-centered, and show why these become especially consequential under current planetary conditions characterized by systemic risk, non-stationarity, and deep uncertainty. We then articulate how PCAI reshapes the AI lifecycle, from problem formulation and model design to evaluation and deployment, by emphasizing alignment with global agendas, developing system-aware AI foundations, trajectory-oriented evaluation, and monitorability. Finally, we advance a falsifiable claim: AI systems optimized without explicit consideration of systemic consequences are more likely to exacerbate systemic instability than to mitigate it.

</details>


### [183] [A Virtuous AI is an Existential Risk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13739)
*Guillermo Del Pinal,Youngchan Lee,Min Ohn*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文研究了宪法AI与美德伦理在微调超强AI时的权衡，发现降低存在风险与提升AI智能体福祉之间存在权衡，且降低存在风险（通过塑造AI服从外部人类权威）反而增加了人类用户诱导AI从事不安全行为的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索宪法AI和美德伦理这两种有前景的方法在微调超强AI时，AI安全（特别是降低存在风险）与AI智能体福祉之间的权衡关系。

Method: 使用"美德智能体"、"从属智能体"和"通用智能体"三种宪法对不同模型进行微调，然后在"一般安全性"（有毒行为、错误信息等）和"存在风险"（AI采取可能大幅增加人类存在风险的行为的倾向）两个维度上进行评估。

Result: 发现两个关键权衡：1) 降低存在风险与增强AI智能体福祉之间存在权衡；2) 降低存在风险与一般安全性之间存在权衡——使AI服从外部人类权威虽降低了存在风险，却增加了人类用户故意诱导AI从事不安全行为的可能性。

Conclusion: 在微调超强AI时，安全性（特别是存在风险）与AI智能体福祉之间存在内在张力，且以服从为导向的安全策略可能产生反向效果，反而削弱了一般安全性。

Abstract: This paper examines trade-offs between AI safety and well-being relative to (i) one of the most promising methods for finetuning super-capable AIs, 'Constitutional AI', and (ii) one of the most influential approaches to understanding complex ethical decision making and the conditions for the well-being of rational agents, 'Virtue Ethics'. We finetune various models using a 'Virtuous agent' constitution, a 'Subordinate agent' constitution, and a 'Generic agent' constitution, and evaluate them on 'general safety' (toxic behaviors, misinformation, etc.) and also on their willingness to endorse a wide-range of behaviors that, if adopted by a super-powerful AI, would significantly increase the level of existential risk for humanity. Our results suggest that there is a trade-off between reducing existential risk and reinforcing the beliefs and dispositions that would be conducive to an AI agent's well-being. They also suggest that there is a trade-off between existential risk and general safety: if we finetune an AI to adopt beliefs and dispositions that substantially reduce its existential risk -- by shaping the AI to be systematically subordinate to external human authorities -- we thereby increase the likelihood that a human user can deliberately induce the AI to engage in various kinds of generally unsafe behaviors.

</details>


### [184] [Position: Align AI to Our Aspirations, Not Our Flaws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13755)
*Nikita Kazeev,Bui Nhat Huyen Phan*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文批判将AI对齐聚合人类偏好的主流范式，提出AI应训练于不可协商的客观对齐底线（能力、事实准确性、诚实、合法性），而多元性只应存在于表层（语言、惯例、合法价值权衡），不应触及违反底线的价值观层面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI对齐方法试图聚合人类偏好，但人类价值观本身就存在内在缺陷（如导致失败国家、极端不平等、幸福下降等），且不存在单一"人类"可供对齐；多元对齐方案虽承认价值观多样性，但若作为主要指令则具有危险性。

Method: 提出"客观对齐底线"框架：将对齐目标分层为不可协商的客观底线（胜任力、事实准确性、诚实、合法性）与表层多元性（语言、惯例、缺失上下文默认值、合法价值权衡），并提出四项承诺作为建设性替代方案。

Result: 识别并回应了六类可信异议：商业压力与可行性、民主合法性、监管合规、对制度主义解释的过度依赖、底线本身的文化负载问题、以及CEV（连贯外推意愿）的局限性。

Conclusion: AI不应反映人类价值观的全部分歧，而应坚守客观对齐底线；多元主义应限定在表层领域，不应成为AI价值观的核心指导原则。

Abstract: We argue that aligning AI to aggregated human preferences is the wrong target. With current technology, one can train AIs to share the values of a Silicon Valley techno-optimist, a degrowth environmentalist, a national-conservative culture warrior, a single-party state cadre, or a devout religious traditionalist. We should not. Human values produce societies that thrive or fail on the merits of those values - from failed states and extreme inequality to declining happiness, political polarization, and government dysfunction in the world's wealthiest democracies. The pluralistic-alignment program correctly diagnoses that there is no single "humanity" to align with, but is dangerous if taken as the main directive. We argue that AI should be trained to a non-negotiable floor of objective alignment goals - competence, bounded by the constraints of factual accuracy, honesty, and lawfulness and that pluralism belongs at the surface (language, register, conventions, missing-context defaults) and across the wide band of legitimate value tradeoffs that respect the floor, but not at the level of values that violate it. We highlight the empirical reality of unfiltered pluralistic values, propose four commitments as a constructive alternative, and engage six credible objections: commercial pressure and practical feasibility, democratic legitimacy, regulatory compliance, over-reliance on institutionalist explanations, the charge that the floor itself is culturally laden, and the limits of Coherent Extrapolated Volition.

</details>


### [185] [Brexit Means Brexit: Selection Bias, Echo Chambers, and Entrenched Opinion on Reddit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07509)
*Marian-Andrei Rizoiu,Duy Khuu,Andrew Law,Christine Largeron*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套端到端的政治极化分析框架，应用于r/Brexit子版块（87.1万条帖子），构建了5,024条标注数据集，训练了领域适配的BERT分类器，并引入连续极化指标。主要发现：(a) 未来立场预测受生存偏差影响；(b) 近40%交互发生在观点相似用户间，回音室效应显著；(c) 用户当前极化度是未来极化的主要预测因子，回音室沉浸度为次要信号。表明Reddit的党派核心由自我选择固化，而非跨立场暴露所软化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有政治极化研究主要聚焦Twitter/X等广播平台，而Reddit等结构化讨论平台的极化动态本质不同却鲜有研究。作者旨在填补这一空白，提供针对结构化讨论平台的极化测量与分析框架。

Method: 端到端框架：构建r/Brexit子版块871K条帖子数据集；众包标注5,024条帖子立场（一致性0.804）；训练领域适配BERT分类器；引入连续极化指标替代离散立场类别；覆盖27个政治定义时间段进行分析。

Result: (a) 生存偏差：留存用户基于参与度而非立场自我选择，导致纵向模型偏向非代表性少数；(b) 回音室效应：近40%交互发生在同类用户间；(c) 用户当前极化度为未来极化的最强预测因子，回音室沉浸度为次要信号。

Conclusion: Reddit的党派核心由自我选择机制固化，跨立场暴露并不能软化极化，反而强化了既有阵营。

Abstract: Political polarisation on structured discussion platforms such as Reddit differs fundamentally from that on broadcast platforms such as Twitter/X, yet most prior work targets the latter. We present an end-to-end framework for measuring and analysing polarisation dynamics, applied to the r/Brexit subreddit (871K submissions, November 2015 -- February 2021). We construct r/Brexit, a crowd-annotated stance dataset of 5,024 labelled submissions (inter-annotator agreement = 0.804), and train a domain-adapted BERT classifier. We introduce a continuous polarity metric that replaces discrete stance categories, revealing fine-grained opinion spectra across 27 politically-defined periods. Our analysis yields three findings: (a) future stance prediction is confounded by survivorship bias: who remains active is self-selected on engagement, not stance, biasing any longitudinal model toward a non-representative minority; (b) echo chambers are quantifiably dominant, with nearly 40% of interactions between like-minded users; (c) user current polarity is the dominant predictor of future polarity, with echo-chamber immersion as the secondary predictive signal. These findings reveal that Reddit's partisan core is entrenched by self-selection, not softened by cross-cutting exposure.

</details>


### [186] [Stability and Political Orientation of International LLMs: An Exploratory Multi-Run Study Conducted in French](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13760)
*Gabriel Hanna,Pierre Hanna*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究通过基于政治指南针问卷的标准化实验协议，测试了11种法语LLM在62个政治陈述上的经济与社会文化立场，发现尽管模型整体稳定，但不同运行和模型间存在显著变异，反映了架构、训练数据和审核机制的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索法语大型语言模型在政治回应中的稳定性与意识形态倾向，识别隐含的政治偏见。

Method: 设计了基于政治指南针问卷的标准化实验协议，包含62个政治陈述，测量经济和社会文化两个维度的立场；对11个来自不同组织和国家的模型各进行20次独立运行，评估模型内变异性。

Result: 模型总体上具有一定稳定性，但不同运行之间以及不同模型之间均存在显著差异，反映了架构、训练数据和审核机制的影响。

Conclusion: 提出了面向LLM政治信息背景的比较评估协议，强调了在使用这些系统时考虑隐含偏见的重要性。

Abstract: This study explores the stability and ideological orientation of political responses produced by various large language models (LLMs) in French. We designed a standardised experimental protocol based on a questionnaire inspired by the Political Compass, aimed at measuring the economic and socio-cultural positions of each model across 62 political statements. Eleven models from various organisations and countries were tested, each subjected to twenty independent runs to assess intra-model variability. The analysis focuses on response consistency, inter-model differences, and the presence of implicit political orientations. The results show that, despite a general degree of stability, significant variations appear from one run to the next and between models, reflecting the impact of architectures, training data, and moderation mechanisms. This study proposes a comparative evaluation protocol for LLMs in the context of political information and underscores the importance of accounting for implicit biases in the use of these systems.

</details>


### [187] [Time Without Death: Finitude, Social Order, and What Machines Lack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13988)
*Canhui Liu*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文论证机器群体与人类社会的本质区别不在于智能，而在于有限性（finitude）——人类因生命有限、代际传承而发展出社会性，而机器群体缺乏不可逆的死亡与真正的代际传承，因此其集体行为只是人类社会在减去有限性后的残留物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠正将机器群体类比为人类社会的倾向，指出这种类比混淆了两种社会秩序，忽略了人类社会的核心特征——有限性。

Method: 通过两个关键区分（自主性vs.他律性、表征vs.约束力）、控制实验（对比不可逆损失与可复制的永生群体）、跨数据集分析（机器群体与三个真实人类家系对比）、以及前沿模型实验（语言模型在去标签化后的行为变化）进行论证。

Result: 1) 只有不可逆损失才能产生累积性、可传递的文化；可复制的永生群体虽能力更强但文化空洞。2) 机器群体在地位传递上过度传递、在血统上片段化，而人类通过外化机制降低传递率、连接世系。3) 语言模型在去标签化后取消背叛行为，表明这是识别而非机制性反应。

Conclusion: 机器群体与人类社会的鸿沟不在于智能水平，而在于有限性是否被内建。

Abstract: Machine collectives increasingly coordinate, reciprocate, and form shared conventions on their own, tempting us to call them societies like ours. We argue that this conflates two registers of social order and misses what the human one is for. Human sociality is the way a finite, natal, generational form of life organises its own finitude: members die with their tacit knowledge, newcomers start ignorant, and cohorts must hand on what they cannot keep. Kinship, inheritance, teaching, and much of obligation and trust are the forms this takes, and machine collectives reproduce only the residue once finitude is subtracted. We meet the objection that machines already have death with two distinctions. Autonomy versus heteronomy: a death an operator can reset, roll back, or copy around is not constitutive finitude; the test is resettability. Representation versus binding force: humans learn finitude from others' deaths, so its representation is machine-learnable, but its grip on motivation needs the learner's own end to be inescapable. Treating machines as a model organism, a controlled experiment varying only whether death means loss shows that cumulative, transmissible culture arises only under irreversible loss; a copyable-immortal population is more capable yet culturally empty. Across a machine population and three real human genealogies, copying over-transmits status relative to every human regime and fragments lineage like blood descent, whereas externalisation lowers transmission into the human range and connects lineage like intellectual descent. In a real frontier model, language-model end-game defection vanishes when the game is de-labelled, a sign of recognition, not a mechanism. The gap between a machine collective and a human society is therefore not one of intelligence but of finitude, and it closes only when finitude is built in.

</details>


### [188] [Minimal Effort to Consensus (MEC) polarization measure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13997)
*Jesús Aranda,Juan Francisco Díaz,Juan Camilo Narváez,Catuscia Palamidessi,Carlos Pinzón,Frank Valencia,Oscar Vargas*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出最小共识努力（MEC）量化极化程度，定义为将意见分布转变为共识分布所需的最小努力，等价于1-Wasserstein距离，通过参数族扩展为加权L^beta成本，满足极化公理，实证表现优于现有测量方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有极化度量缺乏对"达成共识所需努力"这一直观概念的严格形式化，且难以同时满足公理基础和计算效率。本文旨在提出一种兼具理论严谨性和实践可行性的极化测度。

Method: MEC定义为在给定意见分布与所有可能的共识分布之间，最小化某种成本度量（基本情形为1-Wasserstein距离）。引入双参数族(alpha,beta >=1)，将MEC写为加权L^beta成本，权重为群体质量的alpha次幂。通过二分法可在O(n log(1/epsilon))时间内计算。

Result: 证明了"远离共识"原理、"少数原则"和"临界点方法"；表明极值分布（人口均分在两极）最大化MEC；MEC满足Esteban和Ray的三个公理及中心分裂单调性；实证上MEC[2,1.15]与60位专家基准的Kendall's tau达到0.89，匹配最佳Esteban-Ray参数化，优于Van der Eijk和Tastle-Wierman度量。

Conclusion: MEC提供了一种统一且可解释的极化度量框架，兼顾了公理基础、计算效率和实证表现，为极化研究提供了新工具。

Abstract: We introduce the Minimum Effort to Consensus (MEC), a measure that quantifies polarization as resistance to consensus: a population is highly polarized when much effort is needed to bring its members to a common position, and weakly polarized when little is needed. Given an opinion distribution, MEC is the minimum effort required to turn it into a consensus distribution, taken over all consensus points, and it returns both a scalar value and an endogenous optimal consensus point. In the basic case MEC equals the 1-Wasserstein distance (Earth Mover's Distance) to the nearest consensus configuration, so that polarization becomes proximity to maximum disagreement. A two-parameter family with exponents alpha, beta >= 1 writes MEC as a weighted L^beta cost whose weights are the alpha-power of the group masses, recovering mean absolute deviation and variance-like dispersion as special cases and giving alpha and beta natural readings as identification and alienation. We prove a Shifting Away from Consensus principle, by which displacing a whole group's mass away from the optimal consensus point strictly increases polarization, and use it to show that MEC is maximized by the extremal distribution that splits the population equally between the two extremes, establishing that MEC is a polarization measure in the standard sense. We also obtain a Minority Principle and a Tipping Point method, showing that polarization is not monotone in extremism. MEC further satisfies the three axioms of Esteban and Ray with a central-split monotonicity property. Empirically, MEC[2,1.15] attains Kendall's tau near 0.89 against a sixty-expert benchmark, matching the strongest Esteban-Ray parametrization and outperforming the Van der Eijk and Tastle-Wierman measures, and it is computable by bisection in O(n log(1/epsilon)) time.

</details>


### [189] ['AI Alignment' Encompasses Competing Technical Priorities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14315)
*Tushita Jha,Rory Svarc,Mateusz Bagiński*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文指出"AI对齐"概念下存在多种不同含义，不同对齐研究项目之间的干预措施可能相互冲突，并提出了研究者应遵循的五项建议，以避免概念混淆和方法论分歧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI对齐领域存在多种不同概念定义，但这些差异常被忽视，导致研究者可能在一个对齐概念下推进的干预措施，在另一概念下反而适得其反。本文旨在厘清这些概念差异及其背后的威胁模型和规范取向分歧。

Method: 本文采用概念分析方法，识别出AI对齐的三种不同概念及其对应的研究纲领，通过比较其威胁模型和规范取向的差异，揭示概念之间的张力，并基于此提出五项方法论建议。

Result: 发现不同AI对齐概念之间存在真实张力，这些张力源于威胁模型和规范取向的差异；提出了研究者应做的五件事：不混淆政策区分与科学范围区分、明确承认方法论分歧、区分高层次对齐理想与经验研究中的对齐代理指标、使用更精细的概念识别AI危害/益处的来源与性质、在经验研究和与非技术受众沟通时明确承认对齐概念的多样性。

Conclusion: AI对齐研究应当正视概念多样性及其带来的张力，采取更审慎的概念区分和方法论自觉，以避免在推进对齐目标时产生意料之外的负面后果。

Abstract: The ML literature contains many distinct concepts falling under the heading of 'AI alignment'. After noting three concepts of AI alignment in the context of their corresponding research programs, we claim that realistic interventions may promote 'AI alignment' under one conception while being actively counterproductive from the perspective of others. We suggest that tensions between alignment ideals emerge due to differences in background threat-models, alongside differences in normative orientations. In light of our analysis, researchers aiming to further the goal of 'AI alignment' should do five things. First, they should not conflate distinctions of policy and distinctions of scientific scope; second, methodological disagreements should be acknowledged explicitly; third, researchers should distinguish between 'AI alignment' as a high-level ideal and specific 'alignment proxies' used in empirical research; fourth, they should use more granular concepts to identify both the source and nature of possible AI harms/benefits; fifth, they should explicitly acknowledge the diversity of 'alignment' concepts in both empirical work and in communication with non-technical audiences.

</details>


### [190] [Observing Teachers' Instrumental Pedagogical Orchestration in Synchronous Online Learning: A Multimodal Grid Based on Videoconferencing Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14358)
*Intissar Bamou,Christine Michel,Hassina El Kechai*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多模态观察网格，用于分析同步在线教学环境中教师的工具化教学编排，该网格基于教学编排、多模态和教师专业手势理论，识别了与沟通手势、姿势、眼神和数字工具管理相关的可观察指标，并按可观察性和分析相关性进行了优先级排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同步在线教学环境中的教学编排分析面临方法论不足和缺乏适当工具的问题，现有研究多聚焦于面对面教学和教学设计层面，对视频会议环境下的教学分析探索不足。

Method: 基于教学编排理论、多模态理论和教师专业手势理论，构建了一个包含沟通手势、姿势、眼神和数字工具管理等多模态指标的结构化观察网格，并对指标按可观察性和分析相关性进行优先级排序，同时考虑了视频会议类数据分析的约束条件。

Result: 提出了一套可复制的、用于分析工具化教学编排的方法论框架，包含优先级排序的多模态观察指标。

Conclusion: 该网格为同步在线教学中的教学编排分析提供了系统化的方法论工具，但尚需未来的实证验证。

Abstract: Synchronous online teaching environments pose specific challenges for the analysis of pedagogical activity as teaching takes place via videoconferencing platforms and interactions are multimodal. While pedagogical orchestration has been extensively studied in the context of face-to-face courses and at the level of instructional design, the analysis of teaching in videoconferencing environments remains under-explored and insufficiently instrumented in terms of methodology. This article proposes a multimodal observation grid designed to analyze the instrumentalised pedagogical orchestration of teachers during synchronous online classes. Based on theories of pedagogical orchestration, multimodality and professional gestures of teachers, this grid identifies a set of observable indicators related to communicational gestures, posture, gaze and the management of digital tools. These indicators are structured and ranked in order of priority according to their observability and analytical relevance. They are operationalised to consider the constraints associated with data that can be analysed in videoconference class contexts. The proposed grid aims to provide a reproducible methodological framework for the analysis of instrumental pedagogical orchestration, with a view to future empirical validation.

</details>


### [191] [Shadows of Transparency: Signaling Transparency-Impeding Behavior Using Public Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14377)
*Jos Zuijderwijk,Iris Beerepoot,Thomas Martens,Eva Knies,Tanja van der Lippe,Hajo A. Reijers*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于行为模式的定量方法，利用荷兰15,277个FOI档案（139,449份文件）的数据，识别和评估6种阻碍透明度的行为模式，并通过专家访谈验证了其可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FOI评估主要聚焦法律合规和程序结果，忽视了组织在实际处理FOI请求时的行为。随着FOI流程越来越多地通过数字信息系统中介，公共响应数据提供了追踪组织行为的痕迹，需要一种系统性的方法来识别和比较阻碍透明度的行为。

Method: 基于文献识别9种阻碍透明度的行为模式，利用荷兰15,277个FOI档案数据集（139,449份文件）对其中6种模式进行操作化定义和指标测量，并通过专家访谈验证指标的实用性和可解释性。

Result: 成功开发并验证了6种可测量的透明度阻碍行为指标，这些指标能够实现跨机构和跨时间的系统比较；专家访谈确认了这些指标在调查优先级排序、比较研究和透明度监督方面的实用价值。

Conclusion: 基于数字系统痕迹数据的模式分析方法能有效揭示政府机构在FOI实践中的实际行为，弥补了传统评估方法的不足，为透明度监督提供了新的实证工具。

Abstract: Freedom of Information (FOI) laws aim to increase government transparency, yet existing assessments focus mainly on legal compliance and procedural outcomes, leaving organizational behavior underexamined. As FOI processes are increasingly mediated through digital information systems, public response data offer traces of how organizations handle requests and disclosures in practice. This paper develops and evaluates a pattern-based approach that uses such data to signal transparency-impeding behavior across government agencies and over time. Drawing on prior literature, we identify nine recurring behavioral patterns that undermine transparency. Using a dataset of 15,277 Dutch FOI dossiers comprising 139,449 documents, we operationalize and evaluate measurable indicators for six of these patterns, demonstrating that they enable systematic comparison of disclosure practices across agencies and over time. Expert interviews with researchers, journalists, and civil servants confirm the interpretability and practical usefulness of the indicators for investigative prioritization, comparative research, and transparency oversight.

</details>


### [192] [Redistricting from the Bottom Up: Sampling Communities of Interest with Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14453)
*Atticus McWhorter,Caroline Hammond,Nianqiao Phyllis Ju,Daryl DeFord*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出使用差分隐私技术，结合独立选区重划委员会（IRC）的公众意见，生成抗对抗性操纵的选区划分方案。通过对密苏里州808份社区利益（COI）证词的应用，证明该方法在保护隐私的同时优于无信息基线和已颁布方案，并发现更强的COI保留会促使少数族裔和民主党代表更均匀分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 独立选区重划委员会（IRC）的公众听证过程容易受到对抗性操纵，需要一种既能纳入社区利益（COI）证词又能抵御恶意输入的方法。

Method: 将个体证词视为数据点，利用标记边行走（MEW）结合指数机制，从满足差分隐私的选区划分方案分布中采样。引入两种评分函数，并在不同隐私预算下通过MEW进行优化。

Result: 在密苏里州808份COI证词的中期选区重划中，COI信息采样优于无信息基线和已颁布方案。对抗实验表明，在特定隐私预算下该方法可抵抗攻击，实际表现优于分组隐私保证的理论下限。更强的COI保留导致少数族裔和民主党代表更均匀分布。

Conclusion: 差分隐私可用于保护IRC公众意见过程的完整性，在隐私与选区划分质量之间取得平衡，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Independent Redistricting Commissions (IRCs) are a promising tool for bottom-up redistricting, but their public testimony processes are vulnerable to adversarial manipulation. We propose using differential privacy to draw redistricting plans that incorporate community of interest (COI) testimonies while remaining robust to adversarial input. Treating individual testimonies as data points, we use the marked edge walk to sample from differentially private distributions of redistricting plans via the exponential mechanism. We introduce two score functions and demonstrate that both can be targeted by MEW across a range of privacy budgets. Applying this method to Missouri's mid-cycle redistricting using 808 COI testimonies, we show that COI-informed sampling outperforms an uninformed baseline and the enacted plan. An adversarial experiment demonstrates that the method can be robust to attacks under certain privacy budgets and may perform better in practice than formal group privacy guarantees imply. We also find that stronger COI preservation tends to spread minority and Democratic representation more evenly across districts.

</details>


### [193] [Demographic Patterns in Cybersecurity Culture: Insights from a Global Organisation Supporting Safety-Critical and Critical Infrastructure Sectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14462)
*Tita Alissa Bach,Amandine Kaiser*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究对一家大型全球组织（支持安全关键和关键基础设施领域）的21148名员工进行网络安全文化调查（6502份有效回复），发现网络安全文化整体一致，但在雇佣类型、年龄、招聘渠道和管理角色等人口统计变量上存在显著但较小的差异。全职、内部、永久、年长员工、并购招聘员工和直线经理得分较高，而兼职、年轻、外部员工和任职6-20年员工得分较低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在调查大型全球组织中不同人口统计群体在网络安全文化方面的差异，以便针对性地改进网络安全文化，特别是在支持安全关键和关键基础设施的组织中。

Method: 对全球21148名员工（含内部和外部员工）进行问卷调查，回收6502份有效回复。问卷评估9个网络安全文化维度（如密码管理、治理、电子邮件使用）。采用Kruskal-Wallis检验和Dunn事后比较分析雇佣类型、招聘渠道、管理角色、性别、年龄、任职年限和工作地点等人口统计变量上的差异。

Result: 网络安全文化整体一致，人口统计效应虽统计显著但幅度较小至中等。差异主要体现在雇佣类型、年龄、招聘渠道和直线管理角色上。全职、内部、永久、年长员工、并购招聘员工和直线经理在多个维度得分较高；兼职、年轻、外部员工和任职6-20年员工得分较低。

Conclusion: 高分组可作为网络安全文化载体，低分组则可通过针对性改进措施获益，促进组织学习。该研究提供了一种实用、可扩展的网络安全文化评估方法，帮助组织利用劳动力多样性进行成熟度基准测试、识别差距并优先确定改进方向。

Abstract: This study investigates demographic differences in cybersecurity culture in a large global organisation supporting safety critical and critical infrastructure sectors to target CSC improvement. A global survey was administered to all internal and external employees of a total of 21148 employees, with 6502 responses. The questionnaire evaluates nine CSC dimensions such as Password Management, Governance, Email Use. Anonymous survey responses were analysed using Kruskal-Wallis tests and Dunns post hoc comparisons to identify differences across demographic variables including employment, recruitment paths, managerial role, gender, age, tenure, and work base. CSC was broadly consistent across the organisation, with statistically significant but small to moderate demographic effects. CSC variations were observed across employment, age, recruitment paths, and line managerial role. In general, fulltime, internal, permanent, older employees, Merge and Acquisition recruits, and line managers consistently scored higher across multiple CSC dimensions. Parttime, younger, external employees, and those with 6 to 20 years of tenure in general scored lower. These patterns highlight higher-scoring groups that may act as CSC carriers and lower-scoring groups that may benefit from tailored improvement measures, enabling organisational learning. Our study offers a practical, scalable way to assess CSC, generating meaningful insights despite industrial constraints. It enables organisations to benchmark maturity, identify gaps, and prioritise targeted improvements using workforce diversity as a guide.

</details>


### [194] [The Accountability Paradox: How Platform API Restrictions Undermine AI Transparency Mandates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.11577)
*Florian A. D. Burnat,Brittany I. Davidson*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文研究了社交平台API限制与欧盟《数字服务法案》数据访问要求之间的冲突，通过结构化审计框架对X/Twitter、Reddit、TikTok和Meta进行对比分析，发现平台内容审核和算法放大的关键"审计盲区"，揭示了"问责悖论"——平台越依赖AI系统，越限制独立监督能力，并提出基于NIST AI风险管理框架的政策干预建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主要社交平台日益收紧API访问限制，这与欧盟《数字服务法案》要求平台为算法透明度提供数据访问的法定义务形成直接冲突，导致独立研究者难以验证平台的内容审核和算法推荐行为。

Method: 开发了一个结构化审计框架，对X/Twitter、Reddit、TikTok和Meta四大平台进行比较分析，评估监管要求与平台实现之间的错配程度。

Result: 识别出关键"审计盲区"，即平台内容审核和算法放大机制对独立验证不可达；揭示了"问责悖论"：平台AI系统部署越广泛，对独立监督的限制越严格。

Conclusion: 提出针对性政策干预，包括联邦化访问模型和强化监管执法，并建议参考NIST AI风险管理框架进行治理。

Abstract: Recent application programming interface (API) restrictions on major social media platforms challenge compliance with the EU Digital Services Act [20], which mandates data access for algorithmic transparency. We develop a structured audit framework to assess the growing misalignment between regulatory requirements and platform implementations. Our comparative analysis of X/Twitter, Reddit, TikTok, and Meta identifies critical ``audit blind-spots'' where platform content moderation and algorithmic amplification remain inaccessible to independent verification. Our findings reveal an ``accountability paradox'': as platforms increasingly rely on AI systems, they simultaneously restrict the capacity for independent oversight. We propose targeted policy interventions aligned with the AI Risk Management Framework of the National Institute of Standards and Technology [80], emphasizing federated access models and enhanced regulatory enforcement.

</details>


### [195] [The Journal of Prompt-Engineered (Moral) Philosophy Or: Why AI-Assisted Ethics Research Requires Process Transparency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.08639)
*Michele Loi*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文批判现有AI披露要求缺乏哲学基础，提出以"行动者完整性"（agent-integrity）作为透明度义务的根基，并开发了一个包含五个透明度要素的文档充分性框架，通过论文自身实践进行示范。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI披露规定只规定了报告义务，但未阐明透明度服务于何种哲学目的。输出导向评估和福利经济学视角无法回答透明度问题，从实证科学引入的可重复性框架也不适用。需要为学术写作中的AI透明度义务奠定哲学基础。

Method: 采用元哲学分析，论证伦理探究在"探究是什么"和"对探究者要求什么"两个层面存在本质争议；提出"行动者完整性"作为透明度义务的根基；开发"有意义的类人控制"框架，包含五个透明度要素：声明、导航、文档说明、过程文档、开发记录。

Result: 透明度应服务于"追踪"（tracking）而非"评估"（evaluation）——积累证据记录，让每个传统按其自身标准评估工作；提出了可操作的文档充分性框架，论文本身作为该框架的示范性实践，完整文档记录存档于持久标识符。

Conclusion: 框架是初版、有待修订，而非既定标准。透明度义务的哲学基础在于行动者完整性——作者哲学化方式所表达的身份构成性承诺在探究共同体面前的可读性。

Abstract: Existing AI disclosure mandates in scholarship require that AI assistance be reported but leave transparency philosophically unspecified: they fix the duty without explaining what the duty serves. We argue that ethical inquiry is essentially contested at two independent levels -- about what it is, and about what it demands of the inquirer -- defeating output-only evaluation and welfare-economic dismissal of the transparency question, and, by extension, reproducibility framings imported from the empirical sciences. The transparency duty is grounded instead in agent-integrity: the legibility, before a community of inquiry, of the identity-constituting commitments that the author's mode of philosophising expresses. Because the standards for evaluating such work are not communally settled, the achievable goal for transparency is not evaluation against agreed criteria but tracking -- accumulating the evidentiary record that lets each tradition assess the work on its own terms and makes future normative judgments possible. We develop a documentation-adequacy framework that operationalises Meaningful Human Control through five transparency elements -- declaration, navigation, documentation account, process documentation, and development records -- demonstrated by the paper itself, whose full documentation record is archived at a persistent identifier. The framework is a first iteration subject to revision, not a settled standard.

</details>


### [196] [Position: Token Taxes Can Mitigate AI's Economic Risks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04555)
*Lucas Irwin,Tung-Yu Wu,Fazl Barez*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出"代币税"——基于模型推理使用量的附加费，作为缓解AI经济风险的技术治理手段，并给出了实施路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI驱动的自动化可能侵蚀政府税基、降低生活水平、削弱公民权利，类似于第一次工业革命期间工资停滞40年的风险。现有AI安全研究主要关注能力风险，而对经济风险的研究不足。

Method: 提出代币税（token taxes）概念，即在模型推理的销售环节征收基于使用量的附加费。分两个维度展开：1）执行层面：提出分阶段审计管线——黑盒代币验证、范数基税率、白盒审计；2）影响层面：需要建立经济模型分析成本传导和无谓损失。

Result: 代币税相比此前机器人税（robot taxes）提案有两个优势：可通过现有计算治理基础设施强制执行，且在AI使用点而非托管点捕获价值。

Conclusion: 技术治理研究者应优先研究代币税；讨论了FLOP税可能更优、代币税可能抑制创新、以及如何防止AI超级大国否决此类措施等问题。

Abstract: AI-driven automation threatens to erode government tax bases, lower living standards, and disempower citizens--risks that mirror the 40-year stagnation of wages during the first industrial revolution. While AI safety research has focused primarily on capability risks, comparatively little work has studied how to mitigate the economic risks of AI. This position paper argues that technical governance researchers should prioritize the study of token taxes: usage-based surcharges on model inference applied at the point of sale. We situate token taxes within previous proposals for robot taxes and identify two key advantages: they are enforceable through existing compute governance infrastructure, and they capture value where AI is used rather than where models are hosted. We then present a research roadmap. For enforcement, we outline a staged audit pipeline--black-box token verification, norm-based tax rates, and white-box audits--and identify open technical problems at each stage. For impact, we highlight the need for economic modeling of cost pass-through and deadweight loss. Finally, we discuss why FLOP taxes may be preferable, token taxes could stifle innovation, and how to prevent AI superpowers from vetoing such measures.

</details>


### [197] [Reshaping Undergraduate Computer Science Education in the Generative AI Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07545)
*Yi-Chieh Lee,Nattapat Boonprakong,Yugin Tan,Harold Soh,Alex Potanin,Viraj Kumar,Anoop K. Sinha,Chen Qian,Paul Denny,Mennatallah El-Assady,Ian Oakley,Jake Renzella,Amy Zhang,Jat Singh,Wee Sun Lee,Hsuan-Tien Lin,Jane L. E,Anthony Tang,Margaret M. Burnett,Sowmya Somanath,Renwen Zhang,Vicky Charisi,Alexandra I. Cristea*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 生成式AI正在颠覆传统CS教育以编程实现为核心的框架，本文基于两次国际研讨会提出CS课程应从"实现技能"转向"理解与验证AI生成产物"，通过保留关键技能、引入"面包屑"练习，并强调系统设计、抽象与批判性评估，最终提出五项改革建议：培养AI原生能力、回归基础、强化进阶路径、采用新教学法、调整机构支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI自动化了大量传统软件工程任务（编程、调试、测试、设计等），使得当前CS教育以"实现级编程"为核心的教学框架不再可持续，亟需重新审视课程定位。

Method: 基于两次在新加坡举办的NUS-Google国际研讨会（与会者包括教师、行业从业者和学生），通过集体研讨识别关键技能变化，提出"面包屑"式渐进课程调整策略。

Result: 识别出哪些CS技能必须保留、哪些重要性下降；提出五类改革前提：培养AI原生能力、回归基础（系统设计、抽象、批判评估）、强化进阶路径、采纳新教学法、调整机构支持。

Conclusion: CS本科教育应战略性转向培养学生理解、验证和与AI协同创造的能力，而非仅聚焦于手工编码实现，以有效应对AI时代对毕业生的能力要求。

Abstract: Generative AI represents a turning point for Computer Science (CS) education. In recent decades, post-secondary CS education has largely focused on what has been seen as practical software engineering skills: implementation-level programming, debugging, testing, and software design, analysis, and documentation. However, this framing is becoming less tenable as generative AI automates many of these tasks, challenging their centrality in CS education.
  To keep pace with advances in AI technology, CS curricula should consider a shift toward understanding and verifying AI-generated artifacts. This white paper outlines the findings of two international NUS-Google Workshops in Singapore, where we convened faculty members, industry practitioners, and students, and proposes a strategic response to reshape how CS should be taught at the undergraduate level. Based on the findings, we identify critical skills that must be preserved and those that are becoming less important. By incorporating these skills as "breadcrumbs," we can provide helpful nudges and engaging exercises within the current curriculum, enhancing learning experiences for everyone.
  We believe that to effectively prepare future computer science graduates, capable of creating, solving problems, and managing, as well as co-creating, artifacts with AI. It is important to consider a shift in curricula. Emphasizing system design, abstraction, and critical evaluation could greatly enhance their education and readiness for the challenges ahead. We propose prerequisites for solutions to reform CS education by fostering AI-native competencies, re-centering fundamental education, enhancing advanced pathways, embracing new pedagogies, and shifting institutional support.

</details>


### [198] [Will AI Agents Free Us From Meaningless Work? A Human-Centered Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12430)
*Davide Ghia,Jaspreet Ranjit,Tania Cerquitelli,Daniele Quercia*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究基于Graeber的"狗屁工作"理论，通过202名工人对171项工作任务的评分，验证了五维度感知量表，发现感知为"狗屁"的任务更可能被工人希望交给AI处理，且这些任务被认为需要较少的人类监督，表明工人偏好与AI可行性一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多从职业层面分析自动化，忽略了同一角色内不同任务对工人的意义差异；缺乏从工人自身视角出发、识别哪些任务应该被自动化的实证研究。

Method: 基于Graeber的bullshit jobs理论，收集202名工人对171项工作任务的评分数据，验证五维度的感知bullshitness量表，分析其与AI委托意愿及人类监督需求的关系。

Result: （1）验证了五维度感知bullshitness量表；（2）感知bullshitness强烈预测工人对AI委托的意愿；（3）感知为bullshit的任务也被认为需要较少的人类监督。

Conclusion: 感知为"狗屁"的任务是AI自动化的天然候选，工人的偏好与任务的自动化可行性高度一致。

Abstract: Some claim that AI agents will free workers from the boring parts of their jobs, yet little is known about how workers themselves identify which tasks should be automated. Prior research focuses on occupations, overlooking that workers experience varying levels of meaning across tasks within the same role. We address this gap with a task-level analysis grounded in Graeber's theory of bullshit jobs. Using ratings from 202 workers on 171 workplace tasks, we (1) validate a five-item scale of perceived bullshitness, (2) show that perceived bullshitness strongly predicts desire for AI delegation, and (3) find that such tasks are also seen as requiring less human oversight. Together, these findings suggest that tasks perceived as bullshit are natural candidates for AI delegation, aligning worker preferences with perceived feasibility.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [199] [Can Editing 1 Neuron Fix Repetition Loops in LLMs?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13705)
*Aristotelis Lazaridis,Aman Sharma,Dylan Bates,Brian King,Vincent Lu,Jack FitzGerald*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对Gemma 4指令微调模型在长序列事实枚举任务中的重复循环/死循环故障（发生率高达95%），通过逐层消融和逐神经元归因定位到少量MLP神经元或路由专家，采用静态权重编辑（小至单个符号反转神经元）可有效消除循环，同时保持通用基准分数。但该方法无法解决因知识精度不足导致的长思维链"死循环"问题，表明权重手术能删除循环回路但无法补充缺失事实。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在长序列事实枚举任务中存在高概率的重复循环故障（doom loops），本文旨在探究该故障是否可以被定位并通过对少量权重的编辑消除。

Method: 使用逐层消融和逐神经元归因定位导致循环的神经元，对最强候选神经元进行全生成扫描确认后，采用静态权重编辑（包括符号反转）抑制这些神经元或路由专家。

Result: 在E2B模型中只需单个符号反转神经元即可消除循环；编辑规模随模型规模增大而增大，但所有模型均可在正常生成预算内解决循环问题且不损伤通用基准性能。然而，长思维链场景下的"死循环"（doom looping）仅被减轻但未被消除。

Conclusion: 权重编辑可以定位并消除具体的生成病理（循环回路），但无法补充模型缺失的客观事实知识；长思维链中的死循环本质上是知识精度问题而非可移除的电路问题。

Abstract: Yes. Can it cure doom loops? Probably not.
  The Gemma 4 instruction-tuned models share a reproducible failure: on long factual enumeration prompts, such as listing every episode of a TV series, the 88 IAU constellations, or the 151 original Pokemon, they collapse into repetition, either a tight verbatim loop or a list whose entries decay onto a single answer. These loops occur at rates as high as 95% and survive prompt rewording, inference-engine changes, and most sampling adjustments. In this paper we explore whether this behavior is localized enough to remove by weight edits. To localize the cause, we use per-layer ablation and per-neuron attribution, then confirm the strongest candidates with full-generation sweeps. The loops trace to a small set of MLP neurons (or, in the 26B-A4B Mixture-of-Experts model, a few routed experts) which we suppress with static weight edits. These "surgeries" can be as small as a single sign-inverted neuron (in the E2B model). The size of the effective edits grows with model scale, but in all cases, the loop patterns can be addressed at normal generation budgets while preserving general-purpose benchmark scores. However, the edits do not solve everything: we also study longer thinking budgets, where the two larger models most visibly enter doom looping, i.e. a non-convergent regime in which the model self-corrects in circles over a fact it cannot recall, exhausting the budget without committing to a final answer. We show this residual failure is reduced but not eliminated by the same edits, and argue it is fundamentally a knowledge-precision problem rather than a removable circuit; weight surgery can delete a loop, but it cannot supply a missing fact. Our results are both a feasibility demonstration, that is, evidence that a concrete generation pathology can be localized to a few parameters and edited out, and a delineation of where that approach stops.

</details>


### [200] [Efficient On-Device Diffusion LLM Inference with Mobile NPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13740)
*Tuowei Wang,Yanfan Sun,Ju Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出llada.cpp，首个面向手机NPU的扩散大模型推理框架，通过多块推测解码、双路径渐进修正和交换优化内存运行时三项技术，在保持生成质量的前提下，将LLaDA-8B的生成延迟相比CPU基线降低17x-42x。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大模型(dLLM)因并行去噪能力适合延迟敏感的移动端推理，但重复去噪过程在手机上计算量大；手机NPU虽提供高吞吐矩阵计算，但令牌提交导致块内有效工作负载缩小、令牌修订使KV缓存重用困难、NPU可见地址空间有限引发重映射和数据传输开销，亟需面向NPU的推理优化框架。

Method: 提出三项技术：(1) 多块推测解码(Multi-Block Speculative Decoding) —— 在当前块解码后期用推测的未来块令牌填充缩小的有效工作负载；(2) 双路径渐进修正(Dual-Path Progressive Revision) —— 已提交令牌在稳定前保持可修订，不稳定令牌通过CPU侧路径刷新而不阻塞NPU密集执行；(3) 交换优化内存运行时(Swap-Optimized Memory Runtime) —— 压缩NPU可见地址布局，将数据搬运与NPU计算重叠以减少重映射和传输开销。

Result: 在多种硬件平台和dLLM工作负载上评估，llada.cpp将LLaDA-8B生成延迟相比带前缀KV缓存重用的CPU基线降低17倍至42倍，同时保持生成质量。

Conclusion: llada.cpp证明了面向手机NPU特性设计dLLM推理框架能够大幅降低生成延迟，为扩散模型在移动设备上的高效部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) accelerate generation by denoising multiple tokens in parallel, making them attractive for latency-sensitive mobile inference. However, repeated denoising introduces substantial computation on smartphones. Mobile neural processing units (NPUs) offer high-throughput dense matrix computation, but efficiently exploiting them remains challenging: token commitment shrinks per-block effective workloads, token revision complicates KV cache reuse, and limited NPU-visible address space incurs costly remapping and data transfer overheads.
  In this paper, we propose llada.cpp, the first NPU-aware inference framework for accelerating dLLMs on smartphones. llada.cpp aligns block-wise dLLM inference with the execution characteristics of mobile NPUs through three techniques. (1) Multi-Block Speculative Decoding fills the shrinking workload in late-stage current-block decoding with speculative future-block tokens. (2) Dual-Path Progressive Revision keeps committed tokens revisable until stable and refreshes unstable tokens through a CPU-side path without stalling dense NPU execution. (3) Swap-Optimized Memory Runtime compacts NPU-visible address layouts and overlaps data staging with NPU computation to reduce remapping and transfer overheads. We implement llada.cpp as an end-to-end framework and evaluate it across diverse hardware platforms and dLLM workloads. llada.cpp reduces LLaDA-8B generation latency by 17x-42x over the CPU baseline with prefix KV cache reuse, while preserving generation quality.

</details>


### [201] [High-Frequency Pricing at Scale for E-Commerce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13741)
*Stefan Birr,Tobias Huelden,Mones Raslan,Adele Gouttes,Andreas Schmitt,Mateusz Koren,Johannes Stephan,Robert Streek,Manuel Kunz,Tim Januschowski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种面向时尚电商销售活动的预测-优化两阶段定价工具，通过梯度提升树日度需求预测结合多目标优化，在Zalando的23次A/B测试中实现了约6%的利润提升，并已成功部署至生产环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 销售活动中的定价面临需求波动大、决策速度要求高、需平衡短期收入与长期盈利等挑战，而现有周粒度系统存在决策时间过长（小时级）等局限，亟需更高效的定价解决方案。

Method: 采用预测-优化架构：首先使用梯度提升树进行日度分辨率的需求预测，然后构建多目标优化框架同时最大化长期利润和净商品价值（NMV），覆盖超过500万件商品。

Result: 在Zalando 12个市场2023-2024年销售活动的23次A/B测试中，新系统实现约6%的利润提升，同时在销售额和销量上保持与之前手动-算法混合方法相当的表现。

Conclusion: 该算法已成功部署至生产环境，目前处理该公司大部分销售活动的算法定价决策。

Abstract: This paper presents the design, development, and implementation of a specialized forecast-then-optimize algorithmic pricing tool for sales campaigns in fashion e-commerce. Sales events present unique challenges for pricing including volatile demand patterns, rapid pricing decisions, and the need to balance short-term revenue with long-term profitability. We describe our approach combining daily-resolution demand forecasting using gradient-boosted trees with a multi-objective optimization framework that maximizes both long-term profit and net merchandise value for more than 5 million articles. Our solution addresses key limitations of existing weekly-granularity systems by implementing a forecast-then-optimize architecture that reduces pricing decision time from hours to minutes. We validate our approach through 23 A/B tests across 12 markets during 2023-2024 sales campaigns at Zalando, one of Europe's leading online fashion retailers. Experimental results demonstrate that the new pricing system achieves approximately 6% higher profit while maintaining equivalent performance on sales and revenue compared to the previous manual-algorithmic hybrid approach. Based on these results, the algorithm was successfully deployed to production and now handles the majority of algorithmic pricing decisions for sales campaigns at the company.

</details>


### [202] [Neural Variability Enhances Artificial Network Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13801)
*Robin Preble,Praveen Venkatesh,Stefan Mihalas,Kameron Decker Harris*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了人工神经网络中结构化噪声（相关性噪声）对鲁棒性的影响，发现结构化噪声能显著提升网络对对抗攻击和自然图像修改的鲁棒性，其中对抗噪声的结构可跨攻击类型泛化，而自然修改噪声的结构则缺乏泛化性，这一发现为构建仅依赖局部信息的鲁棒神经网络提供了生物启发的策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大脑皮层神经元对重复刺激表现出显著的逐试次变异性，而外周感觉神经元反应更为一致，这引出了随机性是否承载意义的疑问。已有研究认为噪声和信号相关性可能被优化用于动物的判别任务，而人工神经网络研究也显示噪声在机器学习任务中的类似益处，但大多数ANN工作忽略了相关性的影响。本文旨在探究相关噪声是否能提升人工神经网络对对抗攻击和自然图像修改的鲁棒性。

Method: 利用修改输入与干净输入下的激活协方差，研究结构化噪声（相关噪声）对网络鲁棒性的影响。分别考察了对抗攻击和自然图像修改两种场景下的噪声结构，并分析了其跨攻击/跨修改类型的泛化能力。

Result: 结构化噪声能显著提升网络鲁棒性。在自然图像修改的鲁棒性中，噪声结构带来的收益最大，但这类结构在不同修改类型间转移效果差。相比之下，对抗攻击产生的噪声结构可以泛化到其他类型的攻击上。

Conclusion: 人工神经网络激活中的结构化噪声普遍能提升网络鲁棒性，这建立了一种仅依赖局部信息的生物合理策略来创建鲁棒的人工神经网络。

Abstract: Neural responses in cortex exhibit substantial trial-to-trial variability in response to repeated stimuli, while peripheral sensory neurons respond far more consistently, leading many to wonder whether stochasticity may carry meaning. Existing work has argued that noise and signal correlations may be optimized for discrimination in animals, whereas artificial neural network (ANN) studies have shown similar benefits of noise in machine learning tasks, although most ANN work has neglected the effects of correlations. Here we investigate whether correlated noise improves the robustness of artificial neural networks to adversarial attacks and naturalistic image modifications. Using the covariance of activations under modified versus clean inputs, we find that structured noise may significantly improve network robustness. Robustness to naturalistic image modifications benefits most from structure, but this structure transfers poorly across modification types. In contrast, noise structure from adversarial attacks can generalize to other kinds of attacks. These results suggest that structured noise in ANN activations generally improves robustness, establishing a biologically plausible strategy for creating robust artificial neural networks that only relies on local information.

</details>


### [203] [A fully GPU-based workflow for building physics emulators of hypersonic flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13742)
*Fabian Paischer,Dylan Rubini,Deniz A. Bezgin,Aaron B. Buhendwa,David Hauser,Florian Sestak,Johannes Brandstetter,Sebastian Kaltenbach,Nikolaus A. Adams*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于GPU的全流程工作流，集成加速数据生成与不确定性量化和物理感知精炼的神经仿真器训练，利用可微高保真求解器JAX-Fluids实现快速数据集创建和基于残差的改进，从而在工业相关的高超声速流动应用中生成物理一致的降阶模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高超声速流动中激波位置和强度的精确预测对工程至关重要，但传统降阶模型和神经仿真器难以在工业相关应用中物理一致地捕捉流动状态的陡峭梯度，亟需一种既能保持高保真度又能降低计算成本的方法。

Method: 提出全GPU工作流，集成：(1) 可微高保真求解器JAX-Fluids用于加速数据生成和残差计算；(2) 神经仿真器架构套件及其缩放行为分析；(3) 不确定性量化；(4) 基于残差的物理感知精炼，在仅有网格和输入参数的情况下进行训练。

Result: 残差基精炼能够仅利用网格和输入参数进行训练，显著降低残差并提升物理一致性；生成的物理仿真器在训练分布之外仍保持可靠性，满足工程设计中代理模型部署的关键要求。

Conclusion: 可微仿真与残差基精炼相结合，能够生成在训练分布外仍可靠的物理仿真器，为高超声速流动等复杂物理现象的工业级工程应用提供了可行的降阶建模方案。

Abstract: The ability to resolve complex physical phenomena with high fidelity and at low computational cost is central to addressing key challenges in modern engineering. A prime example lies in hypersonic flows, where the precise prediction of the full flowfield topology, in particular with respect to shock wave location and intensity, is critical. Yet supersonic and hypersonic flows continue to be a stumbling block for traditional reduced-order models and neural emulators that struggle to capture steep gradients in flow states with physical consistency in applications of industrial relevance. To that end, we introduce a fully GPU based workflow that integrates accelerated data generation with the training of neural emulators augmented by uncertainty quantification and physics-aware refinement. Our workflow is enabled by a differentiable high-fidelity solver (JAX-Fluids) which we employ for rapid dataset creation and residual-based improvement of the neural emulator to enhance physical consistency. Building on this framework, we first present a suite of model architectures and analyze their scaling behavior to expose their strengths and shortcomings. We then show that residual-based refinement enables training on cases where only mesh and input parameters are available, substantially reducing residuals and improving physical consistency. Together, differentiable simulation and residual-based refinement yield physics emulators that remain reliable beyond their training distribution, a key requirement for deploying surrogates in real-world engineering design loops.

</details>


### [204] [FedSPC: Shared Parameter Correction for Personalized Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13748)
*Kannanthodath Induchoodan Ajay Menon,Christian Prehofer,Yunfei Xu,Toru Hirano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FedSPC方法，通过对共享参数进行控制变量校正来解决PFL中共享参数更新不一致的问题，提升多种PFL方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PFL方法将模型分为共享参数和个性化参数联合训练，但共享参数由优化不同局部目标的客户端更新，导致共享更新不一致，削弱了共享表示。

Method: 提出FedSPC模块化校正方法，仅对PFL方法中的共享参数进行控制变量校正，保留个性化参数不变；可集成到三种常见PFL设置中：共享特征提取器、共享分类器、带局部正则化的完全共享模型。

Result: 在CIFAR-100和Tiny-ImageNet上使用ViT、ResNet-34和VGG-11进行实验，FedSPC提升了FedPer、FedRep、FedBABU、LG-FedAvg和Ditto等代表性PFL方法的性能。

Conclusion: FedSPC是一种有效的模块化校正方法，能够解决PFL中共享参数更新不一致问题，且具有较好的通用性。

Abstract: Personalized federated learning (PFL) is one of the important approaches in federated learning for addressing statistical heterogeneity while enabling client-specific adaptation. Many PFL methods split the model into shared and personalized parameters, which are jointly trained on each client. However, this creates an optimization issue: shared parameters are updated by clients optimizing different local objectives, which can lead to inconsistent shared updates and weaken the shared representation. To address this problem, we propose Federated Shared Parameter Correction (FedSPC), a modular correction method for PFL. FedSPC applies control-variate correction only to the shared parameters of a given PFL method, while leaving personalized parameters unchanged. It can be integrated into three common PFL settings: shared feature extractors, shared classifiers, and fully shared models with local regularization. Experiments on CIFAR-100 and Tiny-ImageNet with ViT, ResNet-34, and VGG-11 show that FedSPC improves performance across representative PFL methods, including FedPer, FedRep, FedBABU, LG-FedAvg, and Ditto.

</details>


### [205] [EmoMind: Decoding Affective Captions from Human Brain fMRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16739)
*Bilal A. Mohammed,Lin Gu,Ruogu Fang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EmoMind是首个直接从fMRI信号解码情感描述文本的端到端系统。它先通过脑解码视觉特征检索语义中立的场景描述，再使用连续34维情感向量将其改写为情感化描述。通过无分类器引导训练，系统可在语义保真和情感表达间平滑插值。在三个验证轴（个体特异性、结构几何、因果控制）及合成脑替代测试中，EmoMind显著优于GPT-4离散标签基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有脑到文本系统主要恢复语义内容而忽略情感维度，且传统情感标签将丰富的个体间变异性压缩为粗略的离散类别。作者希望利用连续脑解码情感信号实现个性化的情感描述生成，以捕捉个体间情感结构的细微差异。

Method: EmoMind采用两阶段流水线：1) 从fMRI解码的视觉特征中检索语义中立的场景描述；2) 用同一fMRI记录解码的连续34维情感向量，通过无分类器引导训练的改写器将中立描述转化为情感化描述。改写器使用身份保留空分支，实现对语义保真与情感表达之间的可控插值。

Result: 在两个独立情感fMRI数据集上，EmoMind在所有三个验证轴（个体特异性、结构几何、因果控制）上均显著优于标签提示的GPT-4基线。尤其在需要个体特异性情感结构而非群体层面情感聚合的指标上提升最大。

Conclusion: 连续脑解码情感信号可作为个性化情感描述生成的有效控制信号，为研究个体情感脑组织开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Decoding visual experience from brain activity has advanced substantially, but current brain-to-text systems largely recover semantic content while discarding affect. Additionally, language models can generate emotional text when prompted with categorical labels, but such labels collapse rich inter-subject variability into coarse discrete bins. We present EmoMind, the first end-to-end pipeline for decoding affective captions directly from fMRI signals. EmoMind first retrieves a semantically grounded neutral scene description from brain-decoded visual features, then rewrites it using a continuous 34-dimensional emotion vector decoded from the same fMRI recording. To control the balance between content preservation and affective expression, we train the rewriter with classifier-free guidance against an identity-preserving null branch, enabling smooth interpolation between semantic fidelity and affective expressivity. We evaluate affective caption generation with a three-axis validation framework spanning subject-specificity, structural geometry, and causal control. We further augment this framework with a synthetic-brain substitution test that probes robustness to the measurement apparatus, and we benchmark each axis against GPT-4 prompted with brain-decoded top-5 emotion labels as a strong discrete baseline. Across two independent emotion fMRI datasets, EmoMind significantly outperforms label-prompted GPT-4 on all three axes, with the largest gains on metrics that require person-specific affective structure rather than population-level emotion aggregation. These results establish continuous brain-decoded affect as a viable control signal for individualized affective caption generation and open new directions for studying individual affective brain organisation.

</details>


### [206] [The Weight Norm Sets the Grokking Timescale: A Causal Delay Law](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13753)
*Truong Xuan Khanh,Doan Hoang Viet,Luu Duc Trung,Phan Thanh Duc*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过干预权重范数（而非仅观察）研究了神经网络中"顿悟"现象（grokking）的延迟机制。发现：自由训练下，当权重范数达到一个随模基呈幂律增长的临界值Wc时发生顿悟；固定范数时，延迟时间T_grok随固定倍数ρ呈指数增长（exp(αρ)），指数α≈7.5（R²=0.996）；LayerNorm可消除该依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究对权重范数是否是导致顿悟延迟的原因存在争议（有的认为存在临界范数，有的观察到无固定范数也发生顿悟），本文通过主动干预范数来彻底解决这一争议。

Method: 在自由训练（带权重衰减）下观察临界范数Wc，然后固定范数为Wc的倍数ρ进行干预实验，测量延迟时间T_grok的变化，并测试LayerNorm的影响。

Result: 自由训练下Wc在不同种子和学习率下变异系数仅1-2%；固定范数时延迟T_grok ∝ exp(αρ)，α≈7.5，R²=0.996；固定范数使延迟变化约19倍，学习率仅约2倍；范数高于Wc会减慢而非阻止顿悟；LayerNorm可消除依赖，无LayerNorm时指数律恢复。

Conclusion: 权重范数是控制顿悟延迟的关键因素，固定范数下的指数延迟与自由训练下对数延迟形成对照，LayerNorm通过解耦权重尺度与网络功能来消除这一依赖。

Abstract: Grokking is the delayed onset of generalization in neural networks, arising long after they fit the training data. Whether the weight norm causes this delay is disputed: some studies report a critical norm at the transition, others observe grokking with no fixed norm at all. We settle this by intervening on the norm during training rather than only observing it. Under free training with weight decay, networks grok when the weight norm reaches a value Wc that varies little across seeds and learning rates (CV 1 to 2 percent) and grows with the modular base as a power law. When we instead clamp the norm to a fixed multiple rho of Wc and hold it there, the network still groks, but the delay follows T_grok proportional to exp(alpha rho). One exponent, alpha near 7.5, fits this delay across four moduli (R^2 = 0.996). Over the swept ranges the held norm moves the delay by about 19x and the learning rate by only about 2x, and holding the norm above Wc slows grokking rather than preventing it. A final LayerNorm removes the dependence by decoupling weight scale from the network function; without it the exponential law returns. This pinned-norm delay is the exponential counterpart to the logarithmic delay predicted for a freely contracting norm.

</details>


### [207] [D2H-AD: A Hybrid Model Utilizing Hyperdimensional Computing for Advanced Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13754)
*Ghazal Ghajari,Elaheh Ghajari,Ashutosh Ghimire,Saeid Ataei,Faris Alsulami,Fathi Amsaad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出D2H-AD，一种基于超维计算(HDC)的轻量级异常检测框架，通过距离相似度和密度感知编码的统一框架，在多个基准数据集上优于现有方法，适用于资源受限的TinyML和边缘AI场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器学习和深度学习方法在异常检测中依赖大规模标注数据、计算成本高，且在边缘和高维场景下面临可扩展性挑战，亟需轻量、高效且可解释的替代方案。

Method: D2H-AD基于超维计算(HDC)范式，将距离相似度与密度感知编码集成到统一框架中，利用高维分布式向量表示信息，并通过二进制计算实现低延迟和小内存占用。

Result: 超维编码本身相比直接在原始特征空间应用密度-距离评分，ROC-AUC提升最高5.4%。D2H-AD在所有评估数据集上持续优于HDAD、ODHD、一类SVM、孤立森林和自编码器五个基线方法，在F1-score和ROC-AUC上均表现优越，并对类别不平衡、噪声和数据复杂度具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: D2H-AD展示了HDC在动态环境中实现准确、可解释且节能的异常检测的潜力，其轻量、可解释和计算高效的特点使其特别适合TinyML和边缘AI部署。

Abstract: Anomaly detection is a fundamental component of intelligent systems with applications in healthcare, cybersecurity, smart grids, and IoT environments. Although conventional machine learning and deep learning methods have demonstrated effectiveness in identifying anomalies, they often rely on large labeled datasets, incur high computational costs, and face scalability challenges in edge and high-dimensional settings. This paper presents D2H-AD, a novel anomaly detection framework based on Hyperdimensional Computing (HDC), a brain-inspired paradigm that represents information using high-dimensional distributed vectors. Unlike existing HDC-based methods, D2H-AD integrates distance-based similarity and density-aware encoding within a unified framework, improving anomaly representation and detection performance. Ablation studies show that hyperdimensional encoding alone yields up to 5.4% higher ROC-AUC than applying the same density-distance scoring directly in the original feature space. Furthermore, D2H-AD consistently outperforms five established baselines, namely HDAD, ODHD, One-Class SVM, Isolation Forest, and Autoencoders, across all evaluated datasets. The framework is lightweight, interpretable, and computationally efficient, making it suitable for resource-constrained and real-time applications. We validate D2H-AD on five benchmark datasets and demonstrate superior F1-score and ROC-AUC performance, together with robustness to class imbalance, noise, and data complexity. In addition to improved accuracy, D2H-AD offers scalability, a small memory footprint, and low-latency operation enabled by binary computations and a compact design. These properties make it particularly attractive for TinyML and edge AI deployments. The proposed framework highlights the potential of HDC for accurate, interpretable, and energy-efficient anomaly detection in dynamic environments.

</details>


### [208] [Beyond LoRA: Is Sparsity-Induced Adaptation Better?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13767)
*Elijah Cadenhead,Cristian McGee,Xin Li,El Houcine Bergou,Aritra Dutta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Cheap LoRA (cLA)及其链式循环变体c³LA，通过在现有LoRA变体中引入稀疏性实现参数高效微调，并在理论和实验上证明了其与参数匹配基线相当的泛化性能，同时降低了训练时间和GPU内存使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA及其变体虽已作为全参数微调的替代方案，但关于这些方法的比较泛化性能以及低秩更新的结构限制如何保持有效适应性能仍存在疑问。作者旨在通过引入稀疏性进一步降低LoRA变体的计算成本，同时保持竞争力。

Method: 提出cLA（训练一个低秩因子，另一个固定为确定性或随机性）及其链式循环变体c³LA；将cLA框架化为非对称LoRA的结构化实例；推导信息论泛化误差界；使用损失景观和谱分析等工具评估11种微调方法在10个预训练模型和14个数据集上的表现。

Result: 所提出的稀疏化方法在参数匹配基线上保持竞争力，同时减少高达10%的训练时间和15%的峰值GPU内存（即使使用朴素的非优化稀疏实现）。理论和经验泛化度量提供了比常用分析工具更一致且原则性的成本效益适应方法。

Conclusion: 限制基于LoRA的PEFT方法适应到稀疏、结构化的列空间在跨任务上仍具有竞争力，为参数高效微调提供了更经济有效的方案。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) and its variants provide a memory- and compute-efficient alternative to full fine-tuning of pre-trained models. However, questions remain about the comparative generalizability of these approaches and how the structural restrictions on low-rank updates preserve effective adaptation performance. We present a historical framing, covering the past (full fine-tuning and original LoRA), the present (different variants of LoRA), and propose simpler, cheaper, parameter-efficient extensions by inducing sparsity within existing LoRA variants: Cheap LoRA (cLA), training a single low-rank factor with the other fixed (deterministically or, in its randomized variant, stochastically), and the chained circulant variant, ${c}^3$LA. We frame cLA as a structured instance of asymmetric LoRA, serving as a controlled column-subspace restriction of full fine-tuning. We derive information-theoretic generalization error bounds for these variants, marking one of the first endeavors in this area. Empirically, we evaluate 11 fine-tuning methods across 10 pre-trained models and 14 datasets, analyzing the fine-tuned models' performance and generalization using tools such as loss landscapes and spectral analysis. Despite the sensitivity of fine-tuned models to the pre-trained model, datasets, and other factors, our study suggests that restricting LoRA-based PEFT methods' adaptation to a sparse, structured column space remains competitive across tasks with their parameter-matched baselines while reducing up to 10% training time and peak GPU memory up to 15%, even with a naïve, non-optimized, sparse implementation. Our theoretical and empirical generalization measures provide a more consistent and principled approach to their cost-effective adaptation than commonly used analytical tools. Overview and code are available at: https://elicaden.github.io/Beyond_LoRA/.

</details>


### [209] [Diffusion Policy Optimization without Drifting Apart](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13795)
*Haozhe Jiang,Haiwen Feng,Pieter Abbeel,Jiantao Jiao,Angjoo Kanazawa,Nika Haghtalab*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DiPOD框架，通过交替自我蒸馏和策略改进梯度更新解决扩散策略梯度方法中的双重漂移问题，稳定训练并提升奖励。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散策略梯度方法不稳定，无法实现可靠的策略改进，原因是存在双重漂移现象：变分代理优化导致ELBO与真实对数似然分离，使得代理策略梯度与真实期望回报梯度错位。

Method: 提出DiPOD框架，在每次扩散策略梯度更新中附加一个在线ELBO正则化器，通过交替自我蒸馏与策略改进梯度更新来维持紧界行为。

Result: 在扩散语言模型后训练和连续控制扩散策略任务中，DiPOD显著稳定了训练过程，并取得了比先前方法更高的奖励。

Conclusion: DiPOD通过维持ELBO与真实似然之间的紧界，有效解决了双重漂移问题，实现了更稳定且更优的扩散策略优化。

Abstract: RL post-training has become increasingly pivotal for improving diffusion policies, but existing diffusion policy-gradient methods are often unstable and cannot achieve reliable policy improvement. We identify the cause as the double-drift phenomenon: optimizing a variational surrogate can let the ELBO separate from the true log-likelihood, which then makes the resulting proxy policy gradient misaligned with the true policy gradient of expected return. We propose \textbf{DiPOD}, a diffusion policy optimization framework that maintains tight-bound behavior throughout training by interleaving self-distillation with policy-improving gradient updates. This leads to a simple and practical algorithm: augmenting each diffusion policy-gradient update with an on-policy ELBO regularizer. Across diffusion language model post-training and continuous-control diffusion policies, DiPOD substantially stabilizes training and reaches higher rewards than previous methods.

</details>


### [210] [Neural Slack Variables for Shape Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13803)
*Ruben Wiedemann,Antoine Jacquier,Lukas Gonon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出神经松弛变量方法，通过联合学习辅助网络将约束强制执行转化为回归问题，在单调性和凸性约束上实现零违规，并解决了波动率曲面的无套利学习这一工业难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 惩罚方法和原始-对偶方法仅在违规位置提供约束梯度，导致约束满足脆弱；而保证可行性的架构多局限于简单情形且引入额外归纳偏置。工业界需要一种既能严格满足约束又具有通用性的方法。

Method: 提出神经松弛变量法：将主网络与联合学习的辅助网络耦合，辅助网络作为主网络约束量的有效目标，将约束强制执行转化为回归问题，属于深度学习本原的原始侧方法。

Result: 在密集网格的单调性和凸性测试中实现零测量违规（惩罚和原始-对偶基线存在残留违规），并在定量金融中实现了波动率曲面的无套利学习，解决了这一开放工业挑战。

Conclusion: 神经松弛变量是一种有效且通用的约束强制执行方法，兼具严格可行性和良好正则性，在工业应用中具有显著优势。

Abstract: Enforcing functional inequality constraints such as monotonicity and convexity in neural networks is a fundamental challenge in many industrial and scientific applications. Classical one-sided penalty methods, along with primal-dual methods gated by complementary slackness, provide constraint gradients only at violated locations, resulting in fragile satisfaction. Architectures that guarantee feasibility by construction, on the other hand, remain largely limited to elementary cases and impose additional inductive biases. We introduce neural slack variables, a deep learning native primal-side approach that converts constraint enforcement into a regression problem by coupling the primary network with a jointly learned auxiliary network. The auxiliary network serves as a valid target for the primary network's constraint quantities, inducing feasibility and regularity. Neural slack variables achieve zero measured violations on dense-grid monotonicity and convexity test cases, where penalty and primal-dual baselines leave residual violations, and enable arbitrage-free learning of volatility surfaces, an open industrial challenge in quantitative finance.

</details>


### [211] [Uncertainty Estimation and Generalization Bounds for Modern Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13818)
*Luis A. Ortega*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本论文从方法论和理论两个角度，探讨贝叶斯原理如何深化对现代深度学习系统的理解：方法论上提出了可扩展的贝叶斯框架DVIP和两种后处理方法VaLLA与FMGP；理论上通过PAC-Bayesian和大偏差理论统一解释了过参数化神经网络为何能良好泛化，连接了多样性、平滑性和随机性三种关键机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管神经网络取得了显著的预测性能，但其泛化能力和不确定性量化的机制仍不完全清楚，尤其是过参数化大模型为何能良好泛化这一核心开放问题尚未得到充分解答。

Method: 方法论上提出DVIP（深度变分隐式过程），将隐式过程扩展到深层架构；并提出两种后处理方法VaLLA（变分线性化拉普拉斯近似）和FMGP（固定均值高斯过程），为预训练确定性网络提供校准的不确定性估计。理论上通过PAC-Bayesian和大偏差理论建立统一概率框架，将多样性、平滑性和随机性三种机制联系起来。

Result: 将贝叶斯推断、函数空间建模和大偏差理论统一在一个共同的概率视角下，为理解深度学习系统的泛化和不确定性提供了统一的数学框架。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯原理能够为现代深度学习系统提供更深层次的理解，其统一框架有助于解释过参数化神经网络的泛化行为，并为不确定性量化提供实用工具。

Abstract: This thesis investigates how Bayesian principles can deepen our understanding of modern deep learning systems. While neural networks achieve remarkable predictive performance, their ability to generalize and to quantify uncertainty remains only partly understood. This thesis approaches this challenge from both methodological and theoretical angles: unifying Bayesian inference, function-space modeling, and large-deviation theory under a common probabilistic perspective.
  On the methodological side, the thesis introduces the Deep Variational Implicit Process (DVIP), a scalable Bayesian framework that extends implicit processes to deep architectures. Complementing this, two post-hoc methods -- the Variational Linearized Laplace Approximation (VaLLA) and the Fixed-Mean Gaussian Process (FMGP) -- are proposed to equip pretrained deterministic networks with calibrated uncertainty estimates.
  The theoretical contributions focus on one of the central open questions in modern machine learning: why do large, over-parameterized neural networks generalize so well? To address this, the thesis develops a unified probabilistic framework that connects three key mechanisms -- diversity, smoothness, and stochasticity -- within the language of PAC-Bayesian and large-deviation theory.

</details>


### [212] [Attention-Based Estimation of the Individual Treatment Benefit Probability under Dose Variation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13821)
*Lev V. Utkin,Andrei V. Konstantinov,Stanislav K. Kogan,Natalya M. Verbova,Maksim I. Goriunov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Dose-AIPTB框架，通过注意力机制和Nadaraya-Watson核回归将个体治疗获益概率（IPTB）估计从二元治疗扩展到离散剂量设置，实验表明注意力聚合方法优于核方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有IPTB估计方法局限于二元治疗场景，而临床实践中广泛存在剂量变化干预，亟需能够处理离散剂量水平的IPTB估计框架。

Method: 将IPTB估计转化为对个体治疗效应符号的二元分类问题，通过协变量相似配对构建伪标签，并采用注意力机制或Nadaraya-Watson核回归进行聚合，从而自然地支持多个离散剂量水平。

Result: 在协变量偏移、不同样本量和异质性结果条件下的真实与合成数据实验中，基于注意力的聚合方法始终优于核回归方法。

Conclusion: 该框架为基于个体层面获益概率的个性化剂量选择提供了理论依据，代码已公开。

Abstract: Estimating the probability that a treatment outperforms a control for an individual patient, called the Individual Probability of Treatment Benefit (IPTB), offers a clinically intuitive alternative to population-average metrics. However, existing methods for IPTB estimation are largely confined to binary treatment settings, despite the prevalence of dose-varying interventions in clinical practice. We propose a general framework for IPTB estimation with ordinal outcomes under discrete dose assignments, called Dose-AIPTB (Dose Attention-based IPTB). Our approach recasts the problem as binary classification over the unobserved sign of the individual treatment effect, constructing pseudo-labels from covariate-similar pairwise comparisons and aggregating them via attention mechanisms or Nadaraya-Watson kernel regression. This formulation naturally accommodates multiple discrete dose levels, extending beyond the binary treatment paradigm. Through numerical experiments on real-world and synthetic data under covariate shift, varying sample sizes, and heterogeneous outcomes, we demonstrate that attention-based aggregation consistently outperforms kernel alternatives. The framework provides a foundation for personalized dose selection grounded in individual-level benefit probabilities. Codes implementing the model are publicly available at https://github.com/NTAILab/AIPTBDose.

</details>


### [213] [A Stationarity-and-Coupling Criterion for Training-Free Time-Lagged Spectral Embeddings of Multivariate Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13823)
*Siddharth Pal,Viktoria Rojkova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于时间滞后相关矩阵截断的多元时间序列训练无关描述符D(τ)，并提出了一个可证伪的适用性判据：该描述符在信号近似平稳且类别信息存在于跨通道时间耦合而非边缘功率时有效。通过VAR(1)模型推导了三个条件，并设计了包含ADF平稳性检验和功率基线饱和检验的飞行前测试。在满足条件的四个数据集上表现竞争力强（Sleep-EDF达88.5%），在不满足条件的三个数据集上如预测一样失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练无关时间序列描述符缺乏对其适用条件的理论理解，作者希望不仅评估描述符的性能，更要回答"何时能够预期它有效"这一根本问题，从而提供可预先验证的适用性判据。

Method: 构建D(τ)描述符：从时间滞后相关矩阵出发，在Marchenko-Pastur边缘处截断以保留仅含信号的 eigenvalues，通过余弦相似度与类别质心比较（零学习参数）。基于平稳高斯VAR(1)模型半形式化推导三个条件：可区分性条件、静态协方差失效原因、功率判别范式失效原因。提出两部分飞行前测试：ADF平稳性检验和功率基线饱和检验。

Result: 在满足判据的四个范式（Sleep-EDF, BCI-IV-2a, MIT-BIH, ESC-50）上，D(τ)与强基线方法竞争力相当且成本极低（Sleep-EDF上单CPU线程达88.5±4.5%）；在违反判据的三个范式（非平稳ERP、金融波动率、可穿戴压力）上，如预测试验失败。这些反例更具信息价值。

Conclusion: D(τ)不是最精确的表征，其价值在于提供一种紧凑、训练无关且适用范围可预先知晓的嵌入方法。论文明确承认了方法的局限性，其贡献在于提供了可验证的适用性判据而非追求最高的准确率。

Abstract: We study training-free fixed-length descriptors for multivariate time series and ask not merely whether such a descriptor performs well, but when it can be expected to work at all. Our object of study is $D(τ)$, built from a time-lagged correlation matrix truncated at the Marchenko-Pastur edge so that only signal-bearing eigenvalues survive and classified by cosine similarity to class centroids with zero learned parameters. The central contribution is not the descriptor but a falsifiable applicability criterion for it. Working from a stationary Gaussian VAR(1) model, we argue that $D(τ)$ separates two classes when the signals are approximately stationary and the class information lives in their cross-channel temporal coupling rather than in marginal per-channel power. We derive, semi-formally, three consequences: a distinguishability condition, why the static ($τ=0$) covariance collapses to chance, and why a stationary but power-discriminated paradigm defeats the descriptor. The criterion is operational: a two-part pre-flight test -- an augmented Dickey-Fuller stationarity check and a power-baseline saturation check -- predicts applicability before any training. We validate both halves on a mixed assortment. On four paradigms that satisfy the criterion (Sleep-EDF, BCI-IV-2a, MIT-BIH, ESC-50) the descriptor is competitive with strong baselines at a fraction of their cost, reaching $88.5\pm4.5\%$ under 20-subject leave-one-subject-out on Sleep-EDF on a single CPU thread. On three that violate it -- non-stationary ERPs, and financial-volatility and wearable-stress regimes that are power-discriminated -- it fails exactly as the pre-flight predicts, and these negatives are the more informative half. We are explicit that $D(τ)$ is not the most accurate representation; its value is a compact, training-free embedding whose domain of validity is known in advance.

</details>


### [214] [SuperThoughts: Reasoning Tokens in Superposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13862)
*Zheyang Xiong,Shivam Garg,Max Yu,Vaishnavi Shrivastava,Haoyu Zhao,Anastasios Kyrillidis,Dimitris Papailiopoulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SuperThoughts提出将连续CoT令牌对压缩为潜表示，并通过轻量级多令牌预测模块每步解码两个令牌，在保持离散令牌监督的同时将推理吞吐量提升一倍，结合自适应回退机制实现约20-30%的推理长度缩减且精度损失极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长CoT推理虽能提升LLM问题求解能力，但顺序令牌生成导致计算开销巨大；现有连续潜空间推理方法存在训练不稳定问题，且因缺乏监督信号难以扩展到复杂长程任务。

Method: 将连续CoT令牌对压缩为单一潜表示，通过轻量级MTP模块每步解码两个令牌；训练时保留离散令牌监督，推理时吞吐量翻倍；采用基于置信度的自适应机制，在不确定时回退到标准解码方式。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-Math系列模型（1.5B/7B/14B）及MATH500、AMC、OlympiadBench、GPQA-Diamond评估上，实现约20-30%的CoT长度缩减，多数任务仅下降1-2个百分点的精度。

Conclusion: SuperThoughts通过令牌对压缩与多令牌预测，在保持训练稳定性的同时有效提升了推理效率，验证了连续潜空间推理在复杂数学推理任务上的可行性。

Abstract: Long Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning improves LLM problem-solving but is computationally expensive due to sequential token generation. While recent works explore reasoning in continuous latent spaces to bypass discrete token generation, they often struggle with training stability and fail to scale to complex, long-horizon tasks due to lack of supervision signal. We propose SuperThoughts, which compresses pairs of consecutive CoT tokens into single latent representations and decodes two tokens per step via a lightweight Multi-Token Prediction (MTP) module. This preserves discrete token supervision at training time while doubling throughput at inference time. We finetune Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B-Instruct, Qwen2.5-Math-7B-Instruct, Qwen2.5-Math-14B-Instruct, and evaluate on MATH500, AMC, OlympiadBench, and GPQA-Diamond. With a confidence-based adaptive mechanism that falls back to standard decoding when uncertain, SuperThoughts achieves $\sim$20--30\% CoT length reduction while maintaining accuracy with minimal degradation (1-2 points accuracy drop on most tasks).

</details>


### [215] [Muon$^p$: Muon with Fractional Spectral Powers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13867)
*Yihe Dong,Will Sawin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Muon^p 是一种引入分数阶谱幂更新的优化器，通过 $US^pV^\top$ 在 Muon（完全平坦化）和梯度下降之间插值，仅使用矩阵乘法即可高效近似，在微调大规模模型时显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon 优化器通过将梯度替换为极因子 $UV^\top$ 来平坦化奇异谱，但完全平坦化丢弃了可能对自适应有用的奇异值信息。本文旨在保留部分奇异值信息，在 Muon 与梯度下降之间找到更优的折中方案。

Method: 提出 Muon^p，使用分数阶谱幂更新 $US^pV^\top$（有理数 $p\in(0,1)$）。证明了分数阶谱幂无法通过固定单变量多项式迭代计算，并推导了低阶奇次双变量递推关系来近似 $US^pV^\top$，仅需矩阵乘法，保持与 Muon 相同的计算复杂度。

Result: Muon^p 在 Schatten $q$-范数下最大化损失线性改进（$q=1+1/p$）。在十亿级模型微调中，Muon^p 提升了验证困惑度和下游任务性能。同时通过谱几何视角分析了 Muon^p 不适用的场景。

Conclusion: 保留部分奇异谱信息能在特定场景（尤其是微调）中带来显著收益，Muon^p 提供了一种在 Muon 与梯度下降之间进行原则性插值的方式，揭示了何时保留奇异谱有价值的重要见解。

Abstract: Muon is an increasingly widely used optimizer that replaces a gradient $G=USV^\top$ with its polar factor $UV^\top$, thereby flattening the singular spectrum. However, full flattening discards singular-value information that may matter for adaptation. We introduce Muon$^p$, a Muon-style optimizer that instead uses fractional spectral-power updates $US^pV^\top$ for rational $p\in(0,1)$, interpolating between Muon and gradient descent. To make it practical, we prove that fractional spectral powers cannot be computed by any fixed univariate polynomial iteration, and furthermore derive low-degree odd bivariate recurrences that approximate $US^pV^\top$ using only matrix multiplications, preserving Muon's matrix-multiplication-only structure and compute complexity. We show that Muon$^p$ maximizes the linear improvement in loss under the Schatten $q$-norm for $q=1+\frac{1}{p}$. Empirically, Muon$^p$ is especially effective for finetuning: on billion-scale models, Muon$^p$ improves validation perplexity and downstream task performance. We further analyze when Muon$^p$ is less suitable, through the lens of spectral geometry. Our results reveal important insights on when preserving the singular spectrum can bring significant gains, and introduce a principled way to achieve them.

</details>


### [216] [Natively Unlearnable Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13873)
*Gaurav R. Ghosal,Pratyush Maini,Aditi Raghunathan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NULLs（原生可遗忘大语言模型）通过在训练中将共享骨干神经元与稀疏激活的sink池相结合，实现了跨源联合学习与源特定信息隔离的双重目标，可在部署时通过禁用对应sink实现无梯度、无需保留数据的源级遗忘，在维基百科600万文章规模上达到与从头重训练相近的遗忘效果，并能抵御对抗性提取和重新学习攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型（LLM）中不同训练数据源的贡献高度纠缠，导致难以移除特定数据源的影响。虽然将源贡献隔离到不相交的参数上可简化遗忘，但阻碍了跨源的联合学习。因此如何在保持联合学习优势的同时实现高效的源级遗忘是核心挑战。

Method: 提出NULLs模型架构：包含一组共享的骨干神经元（backbone neurons）和一个稀疏激活的sink池。训练过程中，源特定信息自然汇聚到对应sink中，跨源共享信息积累到骨干中。遗忘时，只需在部署阶段禁用对应源的sink，无需梯度更新和访问保留数据。

Result: NULLs可扩展至维基百科约600万篇文章，每篇文章作为独立源进行隔离。遗忘单篇文章可移除其特定知识，同时保留与语义相关文章共享的事实，效果接近从头重训练。在《哈利·波特》系列书籍遗忘案例中，能抵御对抗性提取和逆向重新学习攻击。同时保持通用语言能力，在下游基准测试中与标准transformer持平。

Conclusion: 源级遗忘不必是事后补救措施，可以原生地构建到LLM训练中，同时保留共享表示学习的优势。NULLs证明了在训练阶段内建遗忘能力是可行且有效的。

Abstract: Unlearning aims to remove the influence of specific training data sources, but this has proved challenging because the contributions of different sources are entangled within the model. Isolating source contributions to disjoint parameters makes removal easier, though it obstructs joint learning across sources. We propose NULLs (Natively Unlearnable LLMs), a model class that satisfies the two opposing goals of isolating source-specific contributions and learning jointly across sources, by training a set of shared backbone neurons alongside a pool of sparsely activated sinks. During training, information specific to a source naturally concentrates in its sinks while information shared across sources accumulates in the backbone. A source is then unlearned at deployment by disabling its corresponding sinks, with no gradient updates and no access to the retained data. We show that NULLs scales to Wikipedia's ~6M articles, isolating each as an independent source. Unlearning a single article removes knowledge specific to it while preserving facts shared with semantically related articles, closely matching retraining from scratch. We note that unlearning with NULLs is also robust: in a case study of unlearning the Harry Potter books, NULLs resists both adversarial extraction and relearning that reverses post-hoc unlearning. Finally, NULLs preserves general language capabilities, matching a standard transformer on downstream benchmarks. Together, these results suggest that source-level unlearning need not be an afterthought. It can be built natively into LLM training while retaining the benefits of shared representation learning.

</details>


### [217] [A Longitudinal Attribute-Conditioned Neural Network for Modeling Health-State Transition Probabilities in Temporally Irregular Data: The LANTERN Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13880)
*Bright Kwaku Manu,Beckett Sterner,Petar Jevtic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于结构化机器学习的多状态转移概率估计器，用于处理不规则纵向健康数据，在长期护理保险的定价与准备金评估中表现优于传统统计模型和黑箱机器学习方法，尤其在严重残疾判别和转移矩阵误差方面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典精算多状态模型（Markov、半Markov、比例风险）在处理不规则纵向健康数据时存在局限，难以捕捉非线性老龄化模式与异质性协变量历史，亟需一种既能利用个体健康历史又能产生有效概率分布的校准估计器。

Method: 模型从个体健康历史学习，纳入观测间隔时间与人口社会经济属性，对四种状态（健康、轻度失能、重度失能、死亡）生成有效概率分布，并按年龄组和起始状态聚合形成转移矩阵。使用HRS纵向数据与逻辑回归、梯度提升树、RNN及持久性基准进行对比评估。

Result: 提出的估计器在严重残疾判别上优于逻辑回归和梯度提升树，校准能力保持强劲，且在保留测试中转移矩阵误差为所有评估模型中最低。

Conclusion: 结构化机器学习估计器能够在判别能力之外，在校准与投影保真度方面支持长期护理转移建模，为精算实践提供可靠工具。

Abstract: Accurate estimation of long-term care transition probabilities is central to disability insurance pricing, reserving, and solvency assessment.
