To facilitate reproducibility, we release our code at https://github.com/kargibora/SoftElo .

</details>


### [364] [Quantizing Time-Series Models As Dynamical Systems: Trajectory-Based Quantization Sensitivity Score](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13300)
*Mariya Pavlova,Harrison Bo Hua Zhu,Elizsveta Semenova,Yingzhen Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于轨迹量化敏感度分数(TQS)，从动力系统稳定性视角重新审视训练后量化(PTQ)，实现量化敏感度的先验估计与量化器选择解耦，并构建无需校准数据的混合精度框架TQS-PTQ。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PTQ方法中敏感度分析与量化过程耦合，无法在量化器选择和位宽分配之前预先评估各层的量化敏感度，导致黑盒网络或融合算子网络的量化预算规划困难。

Method: 将网络前向传播建模为离散时间动力系统，通过TQS量化由量化引起的误差在推演时间跨度内的传播与放大特性；TQS与量化器选择和位宽分配解耦，实现先验敏感度估计；基于此构建TQS-PTQ混合精度框架，无需校准数据或二阶近似。

Result: 实验表明动力系统视角为资源受限场景下的低精度部署提供了鲁棒且高性能的量化路径。

Conclusion: TQS通过动力系统稳定性理论有效解耦了量化敏感度分析与量化过程本身，为黑盒网络的混合精度量化预算规划提供了可行的先验度量方案。

Abstract: We introduce the Trajectory-based Quantization Sensitivity Score (TQS), a metric that reframes post-training quantization (PTQ) through the lens of dynamical-systems stability. By modeling the network's rollout as a discrete-time dynamical system, TQS characterizes how quantization-induced errors propagate and amplify over the rollout horizon. Unlike conventional PTQ methods, where sensitivity analysis is often coupled to the quantization procedure, TQS enables a priori sensitivity estimation decoupled from quantizer selection and bit-width assignment. This separation allows for quantization budget planning even for black-box or compiled networks with fused operators. Building on this, we present TQS-PTQ, a flexible mixed-precision framework that requires no calibration data or costly second-order approximations. Our experiments show that a dynamical-systems perspective provides a robust, high-performing pathway for low-precision deployment in resource-constrained settings.

</details>


### [365] [Dense Supervision, Sparse Updates: On the Sparsity and Geometry of On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13657)
*Guo Yu,Wenlin Liu,Yulan Hu,Hao-Xuan Ma,Jun-Peng Jiang,Han-Jia Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过分析多种语言和多模态模型对，揭示了On-policy Distillation (OPD)的参数更新具有稀疏性和谱集中性：更新量小且坐标稀疏，主要分布在FFN模块；更新在数值上满秩但谱集中，且远离源权重的奇异子空间。这些发现表明OPD并非普通的密集参数重写，而是保留了on-policy后训练的重要几何特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPD结合了on-policy学生轨迹和密集教师监督两种特性，已成为突出的后训练范式，但其对模型参数的具体影响机制尚不清楚。作者旨在从稀疏性和几何特性两个维度揭示OPD参数更新的本质。

Method: 在多个语言模型和视觉-语言模型对上，针对不同的OPD使用场景进行分析。通过测量更新的稀疏性、层间分布、秩和谱结构等统计特性，并与SGD/AdamW优化器进行对比消融实验。

Result: 1) OPD更新量小且坐标稀疏，分布在各个层，最大相对移动通常在FFN模块；2) 仅训练发现的子网络可接近全训练性能；3) 密集教师监督保留了有用的动量结构和异质性二阶矩尺度，AdamW优于SGD；4) 更新数值满秩但谱集中，主要位于源权重接近零的坐标上，远离源权重的奇异子空间。

Conclusion: 密集教师监督并未使OPD变成普通的密集参数重写；OPD保留了on-policy后训练的重要几何特征，其参数更新具有显著的稀疏性和谱集中性。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (\textsc{OPD}) has recently become a prominent post-training recipe by combining two desirable ingredients: on-policy student trajectories and dense teacher supervision. However, how this hybrid changes a model's parameters remains unclear. Across several language and vision-language model pairs and \textsc{OPD} use cases, our analysis yields two main findings. On sparsity, \textsc{OPD} updates are small and coordinate-sparse. They are distributed across layers, with the largest relative movement usually appearing in FFN modules. This sparse structure is operationally useful: training only the discovered subnetwork nearly recovers full-training performance. The sparse support does not remove the need for adaptive optimization: SGD, previously reported to be competitive in \textsc{RLVR}, underperforms AdamW in our \textsc{OPD} optimizer ablation, suggesting that dense teacher supervision preserves useful momentum structure and heterogeneous second-moment scales. On geometry, the updates are numerically full-rank but spectrally concentrated; they lie mostly away from the principal singular subspaces of the source weights and fall disproportionately on coordinates where the source weights are close to zero. These findings suggest that dense teacher supervision does not turn \textsc{OPD} into ordinary dense parameter rewriting; instead, \textsc{OPD} retains important geometric signatures of on-policy post-training.

</details>
